- **Trust:** server-side subscriptions need `canSubscribe` in the token, so a modified client could subscribe itself to any track in its world's room. Fix: publishers set per-track subscription permissions from a server-sent allow list, or rooms are sharded by region (0011).
- LiveKit's `TrackUnsubscribed` arrives ~1 s after an unsubscribe (server 1.8 `flushTimeout`, not configurable). Audio stops at once: soak p99 70 ms, and the two-browser test is silent past 48 m + 0.5 s of walking.
- **The shared LiveKit container still runs the old port-range config.** I did not recreate it while other agents were using it. With that config a room fits ~25 participants: bots 26-49 never connected (`docs/evidence/wp11-voice-soak-compose-port-range.txt`). `infra/docker-compose.yml` now uses the UDP mux (7882); after merging, run `docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up -d livekit`. The passing soak ran on a private container with the same config (udp 17882 / tcp 17881).
- livekit-client is pinned to 2.15.16: 2.20+ cannot publish to LiveKit server 1.8.4 ("negotiation timed out"). Upgrading the client needs a newer server image too.
- The roster is polled (ListParticipants every 500 ms, every 1.5 s while a room is empty). A speaker's first push can be heard up to ~0.5 s late, and a pair that joins already in earshot is subscribed p50 1.1 s after both are voice-ready. LiveKit webhooks would remove the poll (needs LiveKit config and a reachable URL).
- Occlusion samples the ground heightfield between the heads, which is exact for terrain. Caves need the wasm raycast, and the cave reverb send is not built (WP09). The cave earshot rule waits for WP09 to set `Player.Cave`.
- Earshot is per zone: players on two sides of a zone boundary don't hear each other until zones mirror the ghost band (spec 9).
- One LiveKit room per world, measured at 50 participants only; spec 10's region sharding is not needed at that scale. Re-measure in WP19's 1,000-player soak.
- Every social process campaigns for every world in `social:worlds`, including stale dev worlds (an idle leader polls an empty room every 1.5 s).
- Voice activation's gate uses the-circle's level threshold with no noise-floor calibration.

Assumptions:
- Party members are added by the social service, which owns parties, not by the zone's earshot computation.
- Per-player mute in the voice settings is local to this device; `/mute Name` is the server-side mute (chat and voice).
- A moderator mute makes voice listen-only, as it does chat ("can read, can't send").
- The speaking bit means transmitting with the local level above the speaking threshold, so holding V in silence shows nothing. The zone strips local profiles' bit.
- Fade/pan acceptance compares A's actual audio output levels (left/right RMS), not just the panner parameters. "Within 500 ms" is measured from the zone's earshot decision; the geometric latency, which adds the 250 ms interval, is reported too.
- Shared files touched:
  - `contracts/protocol.yaml`, plus regenerated `contracts/ts/src/gen/protocol.ts`, `server/pkg/proto/protocol_gen.go` and `contracts/golden/protocol_vectors.json`.
  - New `server/pkg/earshot`, plus a row in `server/pkg/README.md`.
  - `server/zone`: `main.go` (3 lines), `internal/game/server.go` (3 lines), `internal/game/entity.go` (2 lines), `README.md` (section).
  - `server/social`: `main.go`, `internal/social/hub.go`, `session.go`, `friends.go` (a few lines each), `README.md` (2 rows).
  - `client/src/net/NetPlay.tsx` (start/stop voice, speaking flag) and `client/src/net/avatar.ts` (3 lines).
  - `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx` (mount `VoiceHud`).
  - `client/src/social/session.ts` and `index.ts` (`onSocialMessage`).
  - `client/src/devHandle.ts` (`voiceDebug`).
  - `client/src/hud/useDebugHotkeys.ts` and `DebugHud.tsx` (V is push-to-talk online).
  - `client/package.json` and `package-lock.json` (livekit-client 2.15.16).
  - `infra/docker-compose.yml` (LiveKit UDP mux) and `README.md` (port row).
  - `docs/cr/005-*`.

## WP09: Caves, gems and mining ✅

Branch `wp/09-caves`. Contract/generator notes: `docs/cr/006-cave-exports.md`. Docs: `worldgen/README.md` "Caves" (ABI, buffers, collision rules), `client/src/world/caves/README.md`, `server/pkg/caves/README.md`, `server/zone/README.md` "Caves and mining", `contracts/events.md` (701-706).

Works:
- Worldgen (`worldgen/src/cave/`): a cave graph per mouth (entry ramp, descending spine of chambers, branches, loops, ravines, sinkhole shafts; within `caveDensity`/`caveMaxDepth`; connected by construction; roofs checked against the real surface), density (flat-floored ellipsoid chambers + capsule tunnels, smooth union, 3D noise capped per tunnel radius), surface nets with seamless cell borders, depth bands, veins (gem chance by depth^gemRarityCurve x biome affinity, region dominant/secondary elements, ores by band, sunsteel only where daylight reaches, voidglass deepest, starmetal hook `crater_veins` for WP20), glowing fungi/crystals, mouth masks, cave-system id. Default world: 32 systems, ~9 chambers each, deepest chambers 167-203 m down, 685 veins; all systems built in 11 ms native.
- New exports `cave_graph`, `cave_build` (spec 5), `cave_build_edited`, `cave_mask`, `cave_system_at`; genVersion stays 1 (no existing output changed, golden chunk/atlas hashes untouched, no re-mint needed).
- Golden table extended (237 cave cells over every depth band, mouths, sinkholes, deepest chambers, empty cells, edited cells, masks, graph hashes, 1,680 collision/ray/system vectors): Node, headless Chromium worker, Go/wazero (compiler + interpreter subset) and native Rust all agree, 0 failures (`docs/evidence/wp09-golden.txt`). Cave cell in a Chromium worker: mean 0.8 ms, max 3.7 ms on a quiet machine, max 7.0 ms under heavy load (budget 12 ms).
- Cave collision in pure TS (`worldgen/js/src/cave.ts`) and a bit-exact Go port (`server/pkg/caves`): floor under a point, wall/ceiling push-out (two-sphere capsule), raycast, mouth-hole dispatch; 585 floors + 540 collides + 405 rays + 150 system ids identical in Go and TS.
- Movement (merged with WP10's climb/glide/swim/stamina step and WP12's structure overlay): caves plug in through two optional hooks, `GroundQuery.atLevel/collide` (TS, `client/src/sim/caves.ts`) and `movement.LevelGround/Collider` (Go, `server/pkg/movement/caves.go`): the step views the ground at the player's level (cave floors, ledges as real steps, rock reads level, no swimming under lakes) and runs one wall/ceiling push-out; the caves sit under the surface ground (terrain + towers) on both sides. Every WP02/WP10 golden unchanged. Golden cave walk on WP10's step: the TS walker's 4,146 frames from in front of a mouth to the deepest chamber (181.5 m down) replayed in Go: max |Go - TS| 8.9e-16 m (118/139 samples bit-exact, the rest the known Go/V8 sin/cos ulp). Walkability: 32/32 systems of the default world reached their deepest chamber with the scripted walker.
- Zone: `CaveTerrain` ground (cave cell cache by interest, sync build on a rare miss), validation (WP10 per-mode `verticalOK` on the level view), teleports and dev spawns underground (`?spawn=x,y,z`), interest clipped to the cave system plus its mouths, mining via Interact (pickaxe) and the `DamageVein(player, veinId, amount, source)` hook (Earth full, other spells half), personal seeded drops (logged with seed) as owner-only pickups, auto-pickup + `Granter` hook (WP14) + events, `vein_mined` edit with a 1.5 m carve applied to collision at once, WorldEdits on first interest then live deltas (expiry delta on respawn), `world_edits` write-behind + restore on startup, seeded 6-24 h respawn (`LODESTONE_ZONE_VEIN_RESPAWN_MIN/_MAX`), `/debug/caves`, metrics.
- Client: worker `cave` jobs, `CaveCellStore` shared by collision and rendering, cave-aware runtime ground (NetPlay forwards the cave queries to prediction), terrain holes over mouths (lod 0-1, skirts too; mesh cache format 3), `CaveManager` (meshes within 96 m while underground or within 200 m of a mouth, flat-shaded rock, instanced gem crystals/ore nuggets/fungi, 8-light pool + wisp lantern, rock arches and sinkhole rims, near-black ambient underground via WorldScene, mining HUD, sparks, pickups, toasts), minimal reverb audio sized by chamber radius, `window.__lodestoneCaves`.
- Evidence: `docs/evidence/wp09-cave-walk.txt` (headless Chromium on the RTX 3070: real key events walk mouth -> deepest chamber 181.6 m down in 70 s on the merged WP10 step at 60 fps, p99 16.8 ms at vsync; no frame hitch when a vein breaks) and screenshots `wp09-mouth-approach.png`, `wp09-mouth.png` (rock arch over the hole), `wp09-deepest-chamber.png`, `wp09-gem-chamber.png` (lit chamber, ruby clusters, fungi), `wp09-vein-before.png`, `wp09-vein-mining.png`, `wp09-vein-mined.png` (carved pocket); `wp09-mining-respawn.txt` (zone binary + Postgres: mined over the wire, row written, kill + restart restores it, respawned after 14.1 s on a 14-15 s schedule, row deleted, minable again); `wp09-cave-paths.txt` (walkability + Go replay); `wp09-golden.txt`.
- Tests: Rust unit + `tests/caves.rs` (every chamber of every system reachable through air from the sky in 4 worlds, shared border vertices, edits, system query); TS `worldgen/js/test/cave.test.ts`, golden, `client/src/world/caves/cavePath.test.ts`; Go `server/pkg/caves` (golden vectors, golden walk), `server/zone/internal/game/mining_test.go` (break/drops/edits/respawn/restore, cave movement == prediction, interest clipping), `server/zone/zone_caves_integration_test.go`; bots predict on the cave ground.

Known gaps:
- Mouth masks live beside the chunk buffer (an analytic rule over the cave graph applied by every host), not inside it as spec 7 words it; recorded in CR 006 (kept genVersion 1, no re-mint).
- Inventory/items are WP14: drops are item-id strings (`ore:iron`, `gem:ruby:cut`) granted through `Config.Granter` (default: log + event). The pickaxe is implicit (anyone swings for 25); tool tiers, the Earth/spell `DamageVein` callers (WP13) and gem cutting at waypoints are not built. Spec 11's "insert items and mark the vein for that character in one transaction" is WP14's.
- Personal drops go to everyone who hit the vein in the last 2 minutes.
- WorldEdits ride the reliable stream (WebTransport per-chunk streams are WP06's gap). Interest is a 5 x 5 chunk square; leaving and re-entering resends a chunk's edits.
- Starmetal: `veins::crater_veins` returns nothing until WP20 builds meteor craters; mineshafts connecting into caves are WP20 too.
- The cave graph is exposed as the enemy nav graph (chambers + tunnel polylines) but has no consumer until WP13.
- The cave-system id is "within 2 m of planned air" (graph primitives, no noise): right for AOI and voice, not a collision query. WP11: `caves.Graph.SystemAt` / `worldhost.CaveTerrain.SystemAt` (surface points are -1).
- Underground, WP10's slope and climb rules apply to floors and ledges (a ledge within 3 m reads as a real step), but cave walls read as level rock (pushed out, never climbed) and there is no water in caves. Climbing cave walls would need wall normals in the step (WP10 interface, `client/src/world/caves/README.md`).
- On the client an edit rebuilds the touched cells synchronously on the main thread (1-8 ms) so prediction agrees with the zone at once.
- Sounds are synthesized placeholders until an audio package ports wizard-wars' AudioManager/soundManifest.
- Underground the post-chain fog is off (its color is the sky's); no dark cave fog yet.
- The reproduced surface around a mouth uses the cave rock material (light grey in sunlight), not the terrain's splat textures; arch and rim boulders hide most of the seam.
- Timing numbers vary with load on this shared machine (other agents): one headless walk measured a 28.9 ms worker cave build and one earlier `npm run test:golden` ran with every host ~2x slower; the final run after merging main PASSES on all hosts (`wp09-golden.txt`).

Assumptions:
- Depth = height below the system's mouth surface; bands shallow < 64 m, mid < 160 m, deep >= 160 m.
- A vein "touches daylight" when its chamber has a sinkhole shaft, or it is the entrance chamber of a mouth higher than 55% of the world's peak height; sunsteel needs that and the mid/deep band (or the near-peak entrance).
- Mining Interact kinds 40 (vein) and 41 (pickup), next to WP12's 10/11 in the `contracts/events.md` registry (1-9 are core).
- Pickaxe 25 damage per swing, >= 250 ms apart server-side (the client swings every 300 ms), reach 4.5 m from the eye; carve radius 1.5 m (spec).
- Respawn delay seeded from (world seed, vein id, edit id); `edit_id` = mix64(vein id ^ break time) >> 1.
- Shared files touched: `worldgen/src/abi.rs` (4 helpers `pub(crate)`), `worldgen/src/lib.rs` (`pub mod cave`), `worldgen/README.md`, `worldgen/js/src/index.ts` (1 export line), `worldgen/js/src/golden.ts` (cave hook), `worldgen/golden/{generate.ts,golden.json,README.md}`, `worldgen/harness/{browser/worker.ts,browser/run.mjs,go/golden_test.go}`, `worldgen/tests/golden_native.rs`, `server/pkg/movement/{movement.go (3 lines), caves.go (new), README.md}`, `server/pkg/worldhost/caves.go` (new file), `server/pkg/README.md`, `server/zone/{main.go,README.md}`, `server/zone/internal/game/{server.go,session.go,entity.go,terrain.go,traversal.go (1 line),explore.go (1 line),metrics.go,game_test.go}`, `client/src/sim/{ground.ts,movement.ts (3 lines),caves.ts (new),README.md}`, `client/src/net/{zoneSession.ts (WorldEdits hook, event listeners),NetPlay.tsx (forwards atLevel/waterAt)}`, `client/src/world/{WorldScene.tsx,README.md}`, `client/src/world/core/{runtime.ts,protocol.ts,chunk.worker.ts,workerPool.ts,cacheDb.ts}`, `client/src/world/terrain/{tileMesh.ts,terrainManager.ts}`, `tools/bots/terrain.go`, `contracts/events.md` (rows in WP09's range + Interact/WorldEdits conventions), `docs/cr/006-cave-exports.md`. No migration (world_edits exists since 0001). No protocol.yaml, constants.yaml or migration change.

## WP18: Deploy and observability ✅ (rehearsed locally; NOT deployed to a real cloud)

Branch `wp/18-deploy`. Details: `infra/README.md`, decisions `docs/decisions/0018-deploy-and-observability.md`, director wake docs in `server/director/README.md`, cost notes `infra/cost/README.md`.

Works:
- **One deploy command**: `LODESTONE_GCP_PROJECT=<id> npm run deploy` (= `node infra/deploy.mjs --target gcp --yes`). Idempotent bootstrap and redeploy: APIs, Artifact Registry, service accounts (VM, Cloud Run, director) and a custom VM-power role bound on the one instance, static external + internal IPs, firewall (public: Caddy 80/443, WebTransport udp 4433, LiveKit 7881/7882; VPC-only via the Cloud Run network tag: Postgres, Redis, zone/social health+metrics; IAP SSH), Secret Manager (Ed25519 ticket/session/save pairs, Postgres password + URL, LiveKit key/secret, wake cron token, metrics token, creator token; generated once, passed on stdin, never printed), backup bucket (60-day lifecycle, `latest.sql.gz` kept), public worldgen archive (`worldgen-<sha256:16>.wasm` + manifest, `--no-clobber`, `immutable`, no lifecycle), images pushed, VM created or updated through instance metadata (refreshed over IAP when awake), Cloud Run director/account/creator (min instances 0, Direct VPC egress, secrets as env), Cloud Scheduler idle tick, client + creator on Netlify (`_headers`; creator `_redirects` proxies `/v1` to Cloud Run). Unavoidable console steps (Firebase Google sign-in, the Anthropic key with `--creator-llm`, an org policy blocking public buckets) print as `!! MANUAL STEP` with exactly what to click. Never changes gcloud config (`--project` on every command).
- **Dry run**: `--dry-run` executes nothing, not even read-only probes. Full 76-step plan for an empty project: `docs/evidence/wp18-deploy-dry-run.txt`.
- **Containers**: `infra/docker/<service>.Dockerfile` for director, account, zone, social, creator (multi-stage, distroless nonroot, env-only config, shared whitelist build context). Zone/account/creator carry the host-built `worldgen.wasm` (byte-identical to the client's); zone also carries `migrate` and `mintworld`; social carries `social-admin`.
- **Game VM**: `infra/vm/docker-compose.vm.yml` (Postgres, Redis with AOF, migrate, mint, zone, social, LiveKit, Caddy; optional Prometheus profile); Caddy TLS for `<ip>.sslip.io` or `LODESTONE_GAME_HOST` with path routing `/zone/ws`, `/social/ws`, `/livekit/*`; `startup.sh` (data disk as Docker data-root, Docker install, metadata + Secret Manager -> `.env`, pull + up, nightly backup timer), `shutdown.sh` (stops zone/social first so the write-behind flushes, dumps to Cloud Storage, stops the rest), `pg-backup.sh`.
- **Wake on demand** (`server/director/internal/wake`): `Provider` interface with GCE (plain REST + metadata token, no SDK), Fake, and none (the default: always awake, so dev and all existing tests are unchanged). `POST /v1/wake`; a 503 `waking_world` gate on connect/social-ticket before any Postgres access; readiness via zone/social `/healthz`; idle stop after 15 min with last activity in Redis and the VM's start time as the floor; `POST /internal/wake/tick` for Cloud Scheduler. Unit tests with a fake clock and a fake Compute API, plus Redis store tests. Cloud Run services start with `LODESTONE_LAZY_DEPS=1` (Postgres/Redis asleep is a warning, not an exit; account mints the world once reachable).
- **Client**: `client/src/wake/` "waking the world" screen before the account screen (invisible for the first 400 ms so an awake world and local dev never flash it; re-pings every 2 min while open; "Continue anyway" when the director is unreachable). 7 vitest tests. The debug HUD already had fps, transport, RTT, bandwidth and chunk queue (WP02/05/06); verified end to end in the rehearsal.
- **Observability**: every service via `server/pkg/svc`: `lodestone_build_info{version}`, uptime, goroutines, heap, GC cycles (runtime/metrics, no stop-the-world), `http_requests_total{code}` + latency histogram (WebSocket upgrades untouched), optional `LODESTONE_METRICS_TOKEN`; JSON logs (slog) as before. Zone gained monotonic `zone_bytes_in_total` / `zone_bytes_out_total` (tick time, players, per-client kbps, AOI sizes and write-behind depth already existed). Director: `director_wake_*`. Local Prometheus (9390) + Grafana (3390): `npm run infra:obs` / `infra:obs:down` (compose project `lodestone-obs`, 127.0.0.1 only); dashboards "Lodestone overview" and "Lodestone cost per player", generated by `infra/observability/gen-dashboards.mjs`.
- **CI**: `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` on push to main / manual. A gate job skips everything (green, with a notice) unless `secrets.GCP_SA_KEY`, `secrets.NETLIFY_AUTH_TOKEN` and `vars.LODESTONE_GCP_PROJECT` exist; then lint, all tests with `LODESTONE_REQUIRE_WASM=1`, `npm run test:golden`, a dry run, and the real deploy in the `production` environment (transcript uploaded). `ci.yml` untouched.
- **Local rehearsal** (`npm run deploy:local`): builds wasm, the five production images and the static builds; writes a production `.env` (fresh keys, `LODESTONE_ENV=production`, no dev auth); runs the VM compose file unchanged plus the Cloud Run/Netlify overlay as compose project `lodestone-rehearsal` on 127.0.0.1:28xxx; smoke-tests; tears down only itself. `docs/evidence/wp18-rehearsal.txt`: **ALL PASS** (28 checks): healthz on director/account/creator/zone/social and the Caddy edge; the edge hides `/metrics`; hashed client assets are `immutable`; creator `/v1` proxy; every `/metrics` refuses a request without the token and serves one with it; headless Chromium on the production client build sees "waking the world" (fake provider, 8.5 s) -> account screen -> local profile -> character -> director ticket -> zone (`phase playing`); HUD `net websocket rtt 2 ms ↓2.4 kbps ↑1.7 kbps`, `chunk queue`; zone metrics see the player; the director woke the VM exactly once. Screenshots `wp18-rehearsal-waking.png`, `wp18-rehearsal-playing.png`; metric dumps `wp18-rehearsal-*-metrics*.prom`.
- **Metrics in Prometheus/Grafana**: `docs/evidence/wp18-prometheus-query.txt` (targets up; queries with two browser players on the rehearsal: players 2, tick p99 0.16 ms, downlink ~18 kbps/client, AOI near 1, wake starts 1, tickets 3, build info for all five services), `wp18-prometheus-graph.png`, `wp18-grafana-overview.png`, `wp18-grafana-cost.png`.

Known gaps:
- **Not deployed to GCP or Netlify** (forbidden in this run). The plan is reviewed and the topology rehearsed in Docker, but real-cloud behaviour is unproven: org policies, quotas, Cloud Run Direct VPC egress with network tags, Let's Encrypt on sslip.io, the guest scripts on a real Debian image (`startup.sh`, `shutdown.sh`, `pg-backup.sh` were never executed), IAP SSH refresh, Cloud Scheduler auth, Netlify `--site-name` creation. Watch the first real deploy step by step.
- In the rehearsal the browser used WebSocket: WebTransport through Docker Desktop's UDP port proxy did not connect within the client's 2.5 s window (WP06's native acceptance covers WebTransport). Production WebTransport keeps WP06's self-signed 10-day certificate pinned by hash; a VM awake for more than 10 days straight needs a restart.
- Redis has no password (reachable only from the VM and tagged Cloud Run egress); Postgres over the VPC uses `sslmode=disable`.
- `POST /v1/wake` is unauthenticated (it must work before sign-in): anyone can keep the VM awake by polling it.
- Resources are created when missing, not reconciled when changed (e.g. firewall rules).
- Idle cost is roughly $12/month, dominated by the static IP (`infra/cost/README.md`); the cost dashboard needs the real bill (WP19).
- On this Windows host, Docker ports published on 0.0.0.0 in the 28xxx/9390 ranges did not answer, so the rehearsal and the obs stack bind 127.0.0.1 only (the VM file keeps 0.0.0.0 by default via `LODESTONE_PUBLIC_BIND`). Docker Desktop sometimes still holds a just-released port; both scripts retry `up` once.
- The dockerized zone showed max 17 ms (mean ~5 ms) for its first chunk generations, above the 8 ms browser-worker line drawn on the dashboard; not a server budget, noted for WP19.
- The client loads worldgen.wasm from its own hashed asset; fetching an older genVersion from the archive bucket is future work (only genVersion 1 exists).
- Spot zone servers and region failover (spec 13 rule 3) are not built.

Assumptions:
- "Wake when the first player signs in" = the client asks the director to wake the world before the account screen, because the account database sleeps with the VM.
- The rehearsal's fake provider simulates only the VM boot delay; its "VM" containers are already running.
- A static external IP over an ephemeral one (stable host and certificate) despite the idle cost; revisit in WP19.
- Shared files touched (outside `infra/`, `.github/` and new files):
  - `server/pkg/svc/svc.go` (scrape hook, metrics token, `instrument` in `RunContext`); new `observe.go` + `observe_test.go`.
  - `server/director/main.go` (`CheckDep`, `withWake`); new `server/director/internal/wake/`; `server/director/README.md` (appended section).
  - `server/account/main.go` (`CheckDep`, background world seeding when lazy).
  - `tools/creator/server/main.go` (`CheckDep`).
  - `server/zone/internal/game/metrics.go` (byte totals + help); `server/zone/zone_integration_test.go` (two metric names added to the expected list).
  - `client/src/screens/AccountSlot.tsx` (`<WakeGate>` around `AccountScreen`); new `client/src/wake/`.

## WP13: Combat, weapons, magic and enemies ✅

Branch `wp/13-combat` (main merged in, incl. WP09/WP11/WP18). Decisions: `docs/decisions/0013-combat.md`. Contract changes: `docs/cr/009-wp13-combat.md`. Spell names: `docs/proposals/spell-names.md` (**PENDING NICOLE'S APPROVAL**). Package docs: `server/zone/internal/combat/README.md`, `client/src/combat/README.md`, `server/pkg/combatdefs/README.md`, `server/zone/README.md` "Combat (WP13)".

Works:
- **One combat table** `contracts/combat.yaml` -> `npm run gen` -> client `COMBAT` + zone `server/pkg/combatdefs` (embedded JSON). The generator refuses a light move slower than 100 ms to start or 250 ms to recover, or an enemy wind-up outside 300-600 ms. All player-facing names come from its `names:` table (rename = one line + `npm run gen`); the 47 proposed names are listed under `pending:`.
- **Weapons**: sword (3-hit combo, sprint/air/plunge cuts, parry + riposte), spear (thrust/sweep, charged thrust), bow (quick shot, draw-to-charge 1 s, arrow rain), staff and wand (bolts, bonk; spell power 1.25 / 1.15). Two loadouts swapped instantly (X). Six use-based skills 1-100 (sword, spear, archery, spellcraft + mining/smithing tracks for WP09/WP14) with techniques every 10 levels (parry riposte, dash cut, elemental form, whirling cut; sweep, impale; quick-draw, double shot, charged pierce, arrow rain; mana flow, quick cast, overflow). Eight **elemental forms** by the socketed gem (Q), the wind one an 8 m dash cut.
- **Magic**: Fire, Ice, Dark and Earth (Druid + Crystalmancer) ported from wizard-wars with their names and numbers (PvP-only mechanics adapted, marked `ported:`); new Lightning, Wind, Light and Void trees, each with a mobility spell, 3-6 core spells, passives, a domain and a pinnacle. 121 spells and passives (92 castable, 29 passives) in 8 trees, prereqs OR'd, one skill point per node, four slots per loadout from any trees, mana, cooldowns. Generic mechanics by type: projectile, arc, hitscan (+chains), beam, aoe (self, aimed, cone, line, lingering), domain, direct, rune, mobility (dash variants), defensive (shields, heal over time).
- **Dodge in the shared movement step** (TS `client/src/sim/dash.ts`, Go `server/pkg/movement/dash.go`): 12 m/s for 0.2 s, 150 ms i-frames, 12 stamina, cancels attack recovery; mobility spells are dash variants. Counters in `SelfState.moveExt` bits 24-31; a cross-language golden (every variant, flat + plane) replays in Go exactly; WP02/WP10 goldens unchanged.
- **Client** (`client/src/combat`): step-synchronous timeline (150 ms input buffer, combos, cancels, charge, parry), predicted hits on the enemies as drawn (melee arcs on the hit frames, local projectiles, beams, chains, areas, runes) with instant spark + 60 ms local hitstop, HitClaims, numbers on HitResult; enemies (procedural bodies by family/size/element, elite/boss auras, health bars, wind-up glow), telegraph decals that fill over the wind-up, enemy projectiles, area/beam FX, floating numbers, first-person weapon with its swing; HUD (health, mana, weapon + 4 slots with cooldown sweeps, draughts, boss bar with phase, toasts, downed overlay). The input-to-hit timing probe (`probe.ts`, `window.__lodestoneCombat.probe()`).
- **Zone** (`server/zone/internal/combat` + glue `internal/game/combat.go`): every input frame passes the combat layer before the step (attack/cast/form log, start legality, illegal dashes stripped); lenient HitClaim validation (any sample of the last HIT_HISTORY_MS + interpolation delay, reach + 1 m, terrain LOS for ranged); damage with skills, spell power, passives, elements (strong 1.5 / resist 0.5 / void 1.2), statuses (burn, slow, freeze, stun, petrify, airborne, curse, shock, pull, knockback, taunt), crits; enemy AI state machines on the heightfield (sleep beyond 96 m, alert, chase, telegraphed wind-up, strike, recover, hit-and-run, evade hop, return/reset, at most two wind-ups on one player); enemy strikes settle after the player's grace frames (dodge i-frames, parry or being outside in any frame evades); enemy projectiles; player health, regen, downed + respawn at RespawnPoint (WP12), drinks, shields, XP into WP12's progress.
- **Enemies**: 22 commons in biome/element/site/depth tables + 2 boss archetypes; elites with fast/warded/sundering affixes and an element; camps at atlas enemy camps, bandit camps, guardians at ruins/watchtowers/shrines/mineshafts/craters, seeded wild packs (enemyDensity), towns safe; bosses (Warden, Archon) titled by element with 3 phases, invulnerable transitions, party-scaled adds, resets; arenas at wizard towers, sunken temples, demon lairs and tier-2 ruins (13 on the official world), `combat.ArenaProvider`/`AddArena` for WP09 cave chambers. Party scaling (+75 % health, +10 % damage per extra engaged player, cap 8). Replicated as `EntityKind enemy` on the existing snapshot/AOI path (typeId = id | element | affix; casting bit while winding up), spawned only within 160 m of a player.
- **PvP never**: claims naming a player are refused (code 1) before validation; the only function that lowers a player's health takes an enemy source (structural test).
- **Persistence** with no migration: `character_progress.skill_tree` (loadouts, weapon skills), `spells_known`, `spells_equipped`; local profiles carry the same fields in their signed save.
- **Contracts** (CR 009): `combat.yaml` (new), constants v3 (combat group), protocol v4 (combat flag semantics, moveExt dash bits, HitClaim/HitResult docs), Event kinds 200-222, Interact kind 50.

Evidence:
- **Feel targets, real browser** (`npm run combat:browser -w client`: real client in headless Chromium on the RTX 3070 via ANGLE/D3D11 at ~60 fps, real mouse/keyboard events, real zone binary on the official world): `docs/evidence/wp13-feel.md` (table), `wp13-feel.json`, `wp13-feel.txt`, screenshots `wp13-browser-*.png`. Press -> first hit frame p95 67 ms / max 68 ms (target 100); recovery 200-250 ms and dodge-cancel 100-117 ms (target 250); i-frames 150 ms; buffered presses fire 49-67 ms later, 52 presses -> 52 starts; hitstop 60 ms requested, 66 ms on screen (40-80); draught 309-316 ms while walking at full speed (400); wind-ups 400-550 ms; a level-1 boar died in 4 sword hits; 0 prediction corrections.
- **Fight lengths** (in-process zone combat world with the bot brain, `docs/evidence/wp13-sim.txt`): every one of the 22 commons dies in 2-5 hits to sword, spear and charged bow, fights 3-10 s (means 4.9 / 4.1 / 4.3 s); elites of every affix 11-29 s; Warden 175 / 182 / 165 s and Archon 179 / 156 / 149 s for 1 / 2 / 4 players, all 3 phases.
- **Party of two clears a camp and a boss against a real zone process** (`node server/zone/cmd/combatbots/acceptance.mjs`: zone binary on the official world's generated terrain, two `cmd/combatbots` over WebSocket with dev tickets): `docs/evidence/wp13-party-clear.txt|json`, zone log and metrics. **PASS**: a level-2 camp (cutthroat, archer, cutthroat, brute) placed with the dev knob near the spawn settlement cleared in 19 s; the party regrouped outside the arena and pulled "The Mossbound Warden" (level 2, scaled to 16,170 health for two): 3 phases, defeated after a 201 s fight, 1 down (walked back from the respawn point and rejoined), 689 claims accepted / 52 refused (mostly kills already confirmed and out-of-reach swings), 0 hits on a player by a player.
- **PvP immunity** (`docs/evidence/wp13-pvp-immunity.txt`): every weapon move and every spell started and claimed at another player is refused (189 claims in the combat package; 142 over the real WebSocket session path, all HitResult code 1, the target's health stays full), plus the structural `hurtPlayer` check.
- Tests: Go `combatdefs` (feel limits, names, trees, effectiveness, typeId), `movement` dash goldens + bounds, `internal/combat` (validation, PvP, strikes/dodge, scaling, profiles, simulations), `internal/game` session test; TS `client/src/sim/dash.test.ts`, `client/src/combat/timeline.test.ts`.

Known gaps:
- Spell, technique, form and enemy names in `docs/proposals/spell-names.md` await Nicole's approval (one-line renames).
- Some passives are data only: Phoenix and Undying (cheat death), Twin Cast, Pole Vault (a movement technique: needs the shared step), Double Shot/Charged Pierce/Quick-Draw are unlocked and shown but the bow does not fire two arrows or draw faster yet. Defensive barriers (Ice Wall, Crystal Wall) are shields, not obstacles. Summon Airbud was not ported (PvP joke).
- Status effects apply to enemies only; enemies never move or slow players (that would leave the shared step). Knockback/pull on enemies are server-side position pushes.
- Enemies steer straight over the heightfield with slope/water avoidance (no nav grid): a cliff between an enemy and its target can strand it; underground (WP09) they should use the cave graph (exposed by WP09, not consumed yet). Cave chamber arenas: the `ArenaProvider` hook exists, nothing calls it yet.
- Ranged enemies and the bot fighter are simple: archers and wisps kite, nobody uses cover. The networked acceptance places its camp and Warden (level 2) with the dev knob on open ground by the spawn settlement; a run against generated groups (`--generated`) picks the nearest arena (a level-4 Archon on a hilltop for this world) and the two fresh bots did not finish it (log kept: `docs/evidence/wp13-party-generated-attempt.txt`).
- WP14 hooks only: loot (`World.OnKill` with eligible players), boss weekly lockout, tomes (`Profile.Grant`), sockets (`SetGem`, dev Interact), ore arrows/gem tips (data), the healing draught is a stand-in (3 charges, refilled on respawn).
- WP15 hooks only: `killsDemons`/`demonMult` data on Light, sunsteel arrows and forms; burn does not ignite grass yet.
- Other players' attacks are drawn from Event 214 only as data (the 3D layer doesn't animate remote swings yet).
- Feel numbers are from one desktop with a real GPU (RTX 3070). Headless SwiftShader runs are not representative (the frame period dominates).
- `go test -race` not run (no cgo toolchain on this machine).

Assumptions:
- "Dies in 2-5 hits" is measured with the light combo for sword/spear and full-charge shots for the bow (staff/wand bolts are filler between spells by design); "the fight lasts 3-10 s" from the moment the enemy notices you to its death, with an optimal bot (humans are slower).
- The 150 ms input buffer keeps presses made within 150 ms of the end of a move; earlier presses during a long move are dropped (that is what a 150 ms buffer means).
- Party = players engaged with the group (near it when the fight starts or hitting it), not the social party; XP and loot eligibility are everyone who damaged it plus their party members within 80 m.
- Boss respawn 1 h after a kill (spec), camps 10 min; enemies despawn 30 s after the last player leaves 260 m.
- Character level health: 100 + 10 per level; mana 100 + 7 per level (wizard-wars); skill points = level + 1, start kit free (Ember Flick, Frost Bite, Magma Dash; sword + staff loadouts).
- Shared files touched: `contracts/{constants,protocol}.yaml`, `contracts/events.md`, `contracts/codegen/gen.mjs` (+ new `lib/combat.mjs`, `combat.yaml`), `contracts/ts/src/index.ts`, generated code + golden vectors, `worldgen/src/gen/constants.rs` (generated), `server/pkg/movement/{movement,wire}.go` (+ new `dash.go`), `server/pkg/README.md` (row), `server/zone/main.go` (3 lines), `server/zone/internal/game/{server,entity,session,explore,traversal}.go` (hooks, a few lines each), `server/zone/README.md` (section), `client/src/sim/{movement,moveWire,localSim}.ts` (+ new `dash.ts`), `client/src/sim/golden/{generate,io}.ts`, `client/src/net/{zoneSession,interpolation,NetPlay}.ts(x)`, `client/src/scene/{PlayerController,FirstPersonArms}.tsx`, `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `client/package.json` (script). No migration (nothing to apply after merge).

## WP20: Towns, roads and structures ✅

Branch `wp/20-towns`. Contract/generator notes: `docs/cr/007-towns.md`. Decisions: `docs/decisions/0020-towns.md`. Docs: `worldgen/README.md` "Towns, roads and structures" (export, generation, `LDTW` layout, town collision), `client/src/world/buildings/README.md`, `server/pkg/towns/README.md`, `server/zone/internal/npc/README.md`, `server/zone/README.md` "Towns and NPCs", `contracts/events.md` (Interact 30-33, Events 800-807).

Works:
- **Worldgen** (`worldgen/src/town/`): new export `towns_build(atlas)`, a pure function of the atlas returning the towns buffer (`LDTW`): settlements, buildings (kit parameters), oriented collision boxes, props (31 kinds, stable ids for doors/chests/stations), NPCs (roles, homes, work spots, names), roads + town streets, bridges, signposts, jigsaw structures + pieces, starmetal veins. **genVersion stays 1**: no existing atlas/chunk/cave hash changed; the official world needs no re-mint.
  - Roads: MST + nearest-neighbour edges between settlements, Dijkstra on the macro grid (slope, water, biome costs, reuse of laid roads makes forks), smoothed and clipped at settlement edges where streets continue; bridges (deck, railings, ramps) where roads cross water; signposts at forks pointing at the nearest settlement per arm; lanterns near towns.
  - Layouts: square around the waypoint stone, streets continuing the roads, ring street (villages/towns), frontage plots refined on the real 1 m terrain (dry, slope tolerance relaxing in passes, back-lane row), required buildings first (inn, forge, ward garden; board, stalls, well on the square in priority order, clear of doorways), then houses/farms/workshops/storehouses filling outward from the square; walls with gate towers hugging the built extent in towns.
  - Kits: six biome styles (cottage, timber, stone, log, adobe, stilt) via a shape grammar (footprint, floors, roof + ridge + pitch, door, porch, chimney, stilts, jetty) and per-role interiors (beds, tables, hearth, chests; inn counter; forge, anvil, quench tub; workbenches; crates).
  - Structures: jigsaw on a 6 m grid with typed edge connectors and per-kind weighted pools/budgets (by tier and `structureDensity`) for all nine v1 kinds; mineshafts link the nearest cave mouth; meteor craters fill WP09's `crater_veins` starmetal hook.
  - NPCs: blacksmith, innkeeper, a merchant per stall, bounty keeper, farmers, villagers, guards in walled towns.
  - Invariants (`worldgen/tests/towns.rs`, 6 worlds): every region has a settlement; every settlement has inn, forge, stall, ward garden, board, well, forge + anvil props, merchant, blacksmith, innkeeper and bounty keeper and a building count inside the spec's range (hamlet 4-8, village 10-25, town 30-60); stable ids unique; boxes finite; floors above the ground.
- **Generation time** (`docs/evidence/wp20-generation-timing.txt`, wasm on Node, 10 worlds incl. both schema extremes): towns_build 0.04-1.3 s per world (official world ~0.2 s; worst in the golden run 3.1 s under Go/wazero on a loaded machine); atlas + towns far under the 60 s mint budget. Buffer ~0.25-0.54 MB raw.
- **Golden** (`docs/evidence/wp20-golden.txt`, `wp20-chromium-golden.json`): `npm run test:golden` PASS on Node, headless Chromium (Web Worker), Go/wazero (compiler + interpreter) and native Rust; every existing entry unchanged, a `towns` block per world added (buffer hash, size, section counts, every-region rule, 350 floor + 350 collide vectors per world).
- **Solid buildings on both sides, bit-identical**: TS `worldgen/js/src/town.ts` (`TownCollision`, `TownGroundCore`) and Go `server/pkg/towns` (line-for-line port) answer floor/collide on the f32 boxes; 1,750 + 1,750 golden vectors replay bit for bit in Go. Plugged into the movement step through WP09's level-ground hooks: floors/porches/ramps/decks/roofs within a step are ground, walls push the body out, ceilings stop a jump. Client: `WorldRuntime.ground` = `TownGround` over the cave ground (towns built in a worker at load); zone: `townCaveTerrain` (cave terrain promoted) in `server/zone/towns.go`; bots predict on the same.
  - **Walk-into-building acceptance** (`docs/evidence/wp20-walk-into-building.txt`; Node client predicting with TS vs the real zone binary on the official world): into the spawn town's inn, forge and a farmhouse through the door, pressing into the back wall/furniture, a jump under the ceiling, out again: **0 corrections in every walk, max divergence 0.83 cm** (cm quantization), walker stopped exactly at the wall limit. Short version runs in `npm test -w client` (`walkIntoBuilding.integration.test.ts`).
- **Zone NPCs** (`server/zone/internal/npc`, glue `internal/game/towns.go`): schedules (work 07-19, farmers 06-18, innkeepers/guards round the clock; out by day, indoors at night), walking along a street graph through doors, entities of kind npc only while a player is within 200 m of the settlement (dropped at 260 m), replicated through the ordinary snapshot/AOI path (`typeId` = role | variant << 8, `level` = merchant kind, camera-convention yaw like players). Interact 30 npc (talk -> 800 + 801 shop / 802 bounties; buy -> 807 via `Granter`; rest at the inn -> 804; accept), 31 board (read 802, accept 803), 32 station (forge/anvil -> `Config.Stations` hook for WP14, default 805), 33 shaft (teleport into a chamber of the linked cave below the shaft); refusals 806. Seeded daily bounties per settlement ("Clear the bandit camp to the north-east", "Bring 5 iron ingots", "Slay the demon that haunts ..."). Tests: official world, 217/217 off-duty NPCs indoors at 01:00, 174/174 walkers home at dusk, 0 of 15,206 walk samples inside another building; in-process zone tests for NPC entities, talk/shop/buy, board read/accept/duplicate, inn, forge station, night closing, mineshaft descent.
- **Client rendering** (`client/src/world/buildings/`): a worker assembles one shell mesh + one interior mesh per settlement from the kit (geometry on the collision boxes, tested per style), one procedural 33-layer texture array and one material; roads draped on the terrain, bridges, signposts, structures (all 15 piece kinds), all 31 prop kinds, lit windows/fires at night with a fixed 6-light pool, scatter hidden on plots/roads (2.4k ids); NPCs (online from snapshots, offline at their work spots) with labels; interaction HUD for Events 800-807; `window.__lodestoneTowns`.
  - **Draw calls** (`docs/evidence/wp20-drawcalls.txt|json`, headless Chromium on the RTX 3070, high tier, 1600x900, the largest town of the official world): whole town from above at an angle **214 draw calls** (162 with the towns layer hidden), top-down 167 (110), street level 292 (216), inside the inn 261, NPCs in the square 251, road + bridge 277, night overview 264: all under the 400 budget; a settlement costs 1 shell + 1 interior draw, all townsfolk 9 instanced draws; frame time mean 5.7-8.6 ms / p95 <= 10.9 ms uncapped; largest settlement mesh built in the worker in ~33 ms, max main-thread upload step 5 ms. Triangle counts in the file include shadow passes (1.09-1.88 M; the town adds ~0.3 M); a visible-only count is not separated (see gaps).
  - Screenshots `docs/evidence/wp20-town-overview.png`, `wp20-town-top.png`, `wp20-town-night.png`, `wp20-street.png`, `wp20-street-night.png`, `wp20-interior.png`, `wp20-interior-house.png`, `wp20-npcs.png`, `wp20-npc-talk-prompt.png`, `wp20-npc-talk-panel.png`, `wp20-forge.png`, `wp20-forge-panel.png`, `wp20-forge-street.png`, `wp20-road-bridge.png`, `wp20-signpost.png`, `wp20-structures-{ruin,watchtower,bandit-camp,wizard-tower,shrine,mineshaft,sunken-temple}.png`, `wp20-styles-{timber,stilt,stone}.png`.

Known gaps:
- Roads are drawn on the terrain, not cut into it (spec 5 "flattened"): cutting changes chunks (genVersion 2). Least-cost routing keeps them on gentle ground; a later genVersion can flatten them. WP05 grass blades grow through streets/floors (they have no ids; needs a mask in WP05's grass generator).
- Building walls are not climbable (not in the height function, like cave walls); WP10 climbing next to a building ignores its walls (collide is skipped while climbing). Decoration (porch posts, chimneys, merlons) has no collision. Stone hip roofs are drawn as gables to sit on the two roof boxes.
- NPC day/night runs on the zone clock (Unix s mod 3600 + `LODESTONE_ZONE_TIME_OFFSET_S`); the client's sky clock is local, so the sky and NPC schedules can disagree until WP15 owns world time. NPCs do not collide with players or walls (they follow door/street routes).
- Bounties: acceptance is per character in zone memory (lost on reconnect/restart, max 5); completion needs WP13/WP14/WP15 (`Server.AcceptedBounties` / `CompleteBounty` hooks). Shop purchases grant placeholder item ids through WP09's `Granter` with no currency (WP14). Forge/anvil open a placeholder (`Config.Stations` for WP14). No migration.
- Mineshafts teleport into their linked cave (no carved shaft: carving would change cave output). Starmetal crater veins are in the towns buffer but not yet registered with the zone's mining (WP09 registry) or the official world (it has no meteor crater; seeds 1234/2026 do).
- Triangle budget (spec 12: 1.5 M visible) is not proven: the bench counts all passes incl. shadow maps (street level 1.82 M with towns vs 1.52 M without); WP05's ratio (~0.7 visible) suggests ~1.3 M visible, but it is not measured separately.
- Settlements beyond 1.5 km are not drawn (horizon tiles don't include them); signposts carry no lettering (destinations show in the HUD).
- The towns layer is rebuilt by each client in a worker (~0.2-0.85 s in wasm, worker ready ~7 s after load as it competes with chunk generation); not cached in IndexedDB yet.
- Timings from one desktop (i7-11700K, RTX 3070, Windows 11) shared with other agents.

Assumptions:
- Every settlement, hamlets included, gets the full required set (inn, forge, stall, bounty board, ward garden, well), so the spawn settlement always has a forge and an inn; stalls and gardens don't count toward the spec's building numbers.
- NPCs are snapshot entities of the existing kind npc (no protocol change); Interact for NPCs targets the entity id, boards/stations the prop stable id, shafts the structure id.
- Furniture taller than a step and town props are solid-only (decision 0020: walkable hard edges let the V8/Go sin/cos ulp difference flip WP10's climb grab, measured as 8-10 corrections of up to 6 cm before the change, 0 after).
- Shared files touched: `contracts/events.md` (rows 800-807, Interact 30-33, NPC entity convention); `worldgen/src/lib.rs` (`pub mod town`), `worldgen/src/cave/veins.rs` (`crater_veins` body), `worldgen/README.md`, `worldgen/golden/{generate.ts,golden.json,README.md}`, `worldgen/harness/{browser/worker.ts,browser/run.mjs,go/golden_test.go}` (+ new `go/towns.go`), `worldgen/tests/golden_native.rs`, `worldgen/js/src/{index.ts (export + `towns_build?`),golden.ts}`, `worldgen/js/test/golden.test.ts`, `worldgen/js/README.md`; `server/pkg/worldhost/towns.go` (new file), `server/pkg/README.md`; `server/zone/main.go` (1 line), `server/zone/internal/game/{server.go (Config.Towns/Stations/TimeOffsetS, one Server field, one tick line), session.go (1 line), entity.go (TypeID field + quantize)}`, `server/zone/README.md`; `tools/bots/terrain.go`; `client/src/world/core/runtime.ts` (towns worker, TownGround, `caveGround`), `client/src/world/caves/caveManager.ts` (uses `runtime.caveGround`), `client/src/world/WorldCanvas.tsx` (mounts `TownsLayer`), `client/src/world/README.md`. New: `worldgen/src/town/`, `worldgen/js/src/{town.ts,townGolden.ts}`, `worldgen/examples/town_stats.rs`, `worldgen/tests/towns.rs`, `worldgen/scripts/towns-timing.mjs`, `server/pkg/towns/`, `server/zone/{towns.go,internal/npc/,internal/game/towns.go,internal/game/towns_test.go}`, `client/src/world/buildings/`, `docs/cr/007-towns.md`, `docs/decisions/0020-towns.md`, `docs/evidence/wp20-*`. No protocol.yaml, constants.yaml or migration change (`npm run migrate` not needed for WP20).

## WP16: Multiple zones and handoff ✅

Branch `wp/16-handoff` (main merged in, incl. the G3 fixes). Decisions: `docs/decisions/0016-handoff.md`. Contract change: `docs/cr/008-zone-handoff.md` (protocol v4: `ZoneTransfer` kind + wtEndpoint; zone-cluster Redis keys). Package docs: `server/zone/README.md` "Multiple zones and handoff", `client/src/net/README.md` "Multiple zones", `tools/bots/README.md` "Multiple zones", `server/director/README.md` "Region assignment".

Works:
- **Region ownership** (`server/pkg/zoneregistry`): roles `primary` (owns what nobody claims; the failover target) and `pool` (owns what the director assigns), or regions pinned by config. `Owner(live, region)` is the one deterministic owner (explicit claim, else the primary; ties go to the lowest id, never to load). Every zone, the director and the bots compute it from the same registry read. Cluster zones heartbeat every 1 s with their own 3 s TTL. The **director assigns regions** (`internal/director/assign.go`: `zoneregistry.Plan`, sticky, contiguous stripes, a dead pool zone's regions go back to the primary, Redis lease per world) and routes logins with `Owner`.
- **Pre-connect and seamless transfer** (`server/zone/internal/game/handoff.go`): within 64 m of a foreign region, the owner sends `ZoneTransfer` (kind 0) with a single-use Redis token, and the client opens a standby connection to the neighbor. Once the player is 2 m inside the new owner's territory, the old owner transfers the exact state: movement state bit-exact (incl. stamina/moveExt), input seq and last frame, observers' view, health, level, exploration progress, local-save state, party, cave system (for the G3 earshot cave rule), WP13 combat profile and runtime state (HP, mana, cooldowns in input seqs, tick timers carried as time remaining), checkpoint epoch and registered `HandoffField`s. The new owner admits the standby with the same entity id, and its Welcome is the client's signal to switch. The old connection is kept as a standby for the way back, so walking back needs no pre-connect. A player whose client can't pre-connect stays with the old owner (simulated past the border) and is never dropped.
- **Ghost band and far ring** (`ghosts.go`): entities within 64 m of a foreign region are mirrored at 30 Hz, and within 200 m at 10 Hz (`ghost:{worldId}` pub/sub; the 200 m tier is my addition so mid-ring observers get mid-ring rates). The world presence feed `worldpos:{worldId}` runs at 1 Hz (spec 11) for the far ring and the cross-zone party ring. Every entity kind except personal pickups and projectiles is mirrored: players, WP13 enemies and WP20 townsfolk. Mirrors never enter `Server.ents`, so AI, checkpoints, exploration, mining and earshot see only owned entities. Mirrors are extrapolated to the receiver's tick time. Entity ids are world-stable (`entid:{worldId}`), so observers keep one track through a handoff.
- **Checkpoint ordering**: every cluster write carries the owner's epoch in `characters.position.e`, and Postgres refuses older epochs (position and progress saves). The old owner's late queue therefore never overwrites the new owner. The old owner saves on transfer (spec 11), and progress is marked dirty again on admission. No migration.
- **Duplicate login across zones**: `owner:{worldId}:{charId}` is swapped on login. A live previous owner releases the live state (and kicks its session with `duplicate_login`); a dead one is skipped.
- **Failover** (spec 13): a killed pool zone's regions go to the primary about 3 s after its last heartbeat. Clients (`ZoneSession` with a `reconnect` callback from NetPlay) and bots reconnect on their own through the director/registry to the new owner and snap to the last checkpoint. No overlay, no click.
- **Client** (`client/src/net/zoneSession.ts`): standby connections; switch on Welcome (decoder reset, unacked frames resent, prediction, interpolation and clock kept); cancel; failover with a forced correction. `netDebug()` shows `zoneId`, `standby` and `transfers`. Single-zone dev is unchanged: no ZoneTransfer ever arrives, and reconnect only replaces the old "connection lost" overlay when the zone dies (the overlay comes back after 60 s of failed attempts).
- **Cluster mode is opt-in** (`LODESTONE_ZONE_CLUSTER=1` / `_ROLE` / `_REGIONS`). Without it a zone behaves exactly as before.
- **Bots** (`tools/bots`): `-cluster` follows handoffs exactly like the client and fails over. `-mode cross` makes a share of the bots keep crossing borders. An ownership sampler feeds the scenario-2 verdict. `seedbots` puts bot characters in a scratch DB. `handoff-soak.mjs` runs everything: scratch DB, director, N zones, netsim per zone and an optional hard kill.

Evidence:
- **Soak scenario 2: 1,000 bots across the world with continuous zone crossings** (`node tools/bots/handoff-soak.mjs --bots 1000 --zones 4 --duration 10m`; `docs/evidence/wp16-soak-1000-4z-clean.txt|json`). Setup: real director (assigner), 4 zone processes (primary + 3 pool; the director split the world into four 1024 m stripes), shared Redis, scratch Postgres with one character row per bot, WebSocket. 40 % of the bots keep crossing the nearest border; the rest walk the world. 626 s. Results:
  - **1000/1000 bots connected at the end** (no dropped player).
  - **6,353 handoffs by 625 bots**, **0 handoff states lost**, 0 entity id changes.
  - Ownership sampled every 2 s: **0 characters owned by two zones**. Characters listed by two zones across an in-flight handoff were told apart by admission epoch, and no playing bot was unowned for two samples running.
  - **0 duplicated discovery grants** (1,135 grants).
  - Switch gap p50 56 ms, p95 67 ms. Downlink p95 101 kbps, max 116 kbps (budget 160); uplink 11 kbps.
  - The file says FAIL only because, at the time, the verdict gated the zone tick on spec 9's 12 ms. Zone tick p99 was 8.5 / 16.8 / 20.0 / 25.6 ms with 158 / 216 / 315 / 310 players (see gaps). That run also predates the reliable post-switch resend.
- **Scenario 2 under "thailand" (scenario 4) plus a spot-zone kill** (`--bots 1000 --zones 4 --duration 6m --profile thailand --kill-after 3m`; `docs/evidence/wp16-soak-1000-4z-thailand-failover.txt|json`). One netsim proxy per zone (RTT p50 200 ms). After 3 min a pool zone owning 306 players is hard-killed. **PASS**:
  - 1000/1000 connected at the end.
  - The dead zone's regions belonged to the primary 3.7 s after the kill.
  - **306 of 306 bots failed over on their own** (gave up 0; reconnect p50 5.2 s, max 5.5 s; rewind to the checkpoint p50 68 m).
  - 3,122 handoffs, 0 lost states, 0 id changes, **0 characters owned twice**, 0 duplicated discoveries. 5 discoveries were re-granted after the failover: they were granted on the dead zone, whose write-behind queue died with it, so each is granted once in durable state.
- **200 bots, thailand, kill** (`docs/evidence/wp16-soak-200-3z-thailand-failover.txt|json`, the machine not overloaded): PASS.
  - 86/86 failovers, 363 handoffs.
  - **0 prediction mismatches introduced by a switch** over 1,448 post-switch checks (every post-switch mismatch already existed against the old zone: netsim TCP-retransmit stalls).
  - Zone tick p99 2.3-3.5 ms.
- **The same 200-bot thailand + kill run after merging WP13/WP20** (enemies and townsfolk live; `docs/evidence/wp16-soak-200-3z-thailand-failover-postmerge.txt|json`): PASS.
  - 200/200 connected; 88/88 failovers (p50 4.8 s); 363 handoffs.
  - 0 owned twice, 0 duplicated grants.
  - 1 prediction mismatch introduced by a switch (of 1,446 post-switch checks).
  - Zone tick p99 3.7-6.6 ms.
- First 1000-bot run (`wp16-soak-1000-3z-first-run.txt`, 3 zones, every bot a crosser) found three bugs, all fixed:
  - the ownership sampler counted in-flight handoffs as duplicates;
  - re-admitted standby sessions used a fake ack baseline;
  - the unreliable post-switch resend was shed under load (the latest-wins input queue).
- **CPU profile** of a 329-player zone under the 1000-bot thailand run (`wp16-soak-1000-4z-thailand-failover-cpu-zone-pool-2.txt`): the tick goes to the existing snapshot/AOI path (`sendSnapshot` 30 %, `Grid.Query` 8 %) and Windows socket syscalls (`cgocall` 16 %). WP16 code (ghost frames, mirrors, interest sort) is about 2 %.
- **Two headless browsers across a border** (`node client/src/net/dev/handoff-acceptance.mjs --profile thailand`; `docs/evidence/wp16-border-browsers.txt|json`, `wp16-border-*.png`). Setup: real account, director and two zone processes (primary + pool; the director gave the pool x < 2048), one netsim "thailand" proxy per zone (RTT 135-168 ms), two Chromium processes on the GPU. Re-run after merging WP13/WP20. Both players walk side by side east across x = 2048 and back. Results:
  - Each browser switched zones twice (west → east → west), keeping the same entity id.
  - **0 local reconciliation corrections, 0 remote teleports.**
  - Each player saw the other in **96/96** samples.
  - Switch gaps (old zone's last snapshot → new zone's first): 41-72 ms.
  - Eased remote corrections within 1.5 s of a switch: at most 26 cm, under WP06's 30 cm "visible" line. A lurch away from the switches is described under gaps.
  - ALL PASS. Before the merge, two runs also had 0 eased discontinuities.
- **Failover in the browsers** (`--failover`; `docs/evidence/wp16-failover-browsers.txt|json`, `wp16-failover-a.png`): the same walk (again 0 corrections, 0 teleports, 95/95 visibility), then zone-west (which owns both players) is hard-killed. **Both browsers reconnected on their own to zone-east in 5.0 s** (failover gap ~4.7 s) and rewound 13-14 m to the transfer checkpoint. No "connection lost" overlay appeared, they see each other again, and walking afterwards gives 0 corrections. ALL PASS.
- Tests:
  - Go `internal/game/cluster_test.go`: two or three zones in one process on an in-memory cluster, with real WebSocket clients following the protocol. Covers: handoff with prediction agreeing to the cm and the same id both ways; combat state carried; enemies and NPCs mirrored; standby reuse on the way back; an observer on the old zone never gets a removal; no pre-connect means the player stays; duplicate login across zones takes the live state; the presence feed fills the far ring; a stale checkpoint is fenced; re-admission takes the handed-over progress; transfer along a line between two regions of one zone.
  - `internal/cluster` (Redis): single-use records, owner swap, ids, presence, pub/sub, heartbeat and ownership.
  - `zoneregistry`: Owner, TTL, Plan.
  - director `assign_test.go`: split, routing, failover.
  - vitest `client/src/net/handoff.test.ts`: switch, cancel, failover snap, terminal kick.

Known gaps:
- **Zone tick p99 over spec 9's 12 ms in the 1000-bot runs.** Pool zones held 300-460 players, about 100 in view each, on one shared 16-core Windows box that was also running 1,000 bots, 4 netsim proxies and other agents' work: p99 14-27 ms. The profile puts the cost in the existing snapshot/AOI path and socket I/O, not in handoff code. The cluster verdict reports this as a warning (scenario 2 is about drops and duplicates; the tick budget is scenario 1's, WP19). Mirroring the whole world's players into every zone's far ring (presence feed) is what a single 1,000-player zone would hold too, so `Grid.Query` grows with world population.
- **Bot prediction under load or thailand.** Bots resync (more than 5 cm) a few percent of checks: their own latest-wins input queue sheds packets when the bots process is starved, and netsim stalls make the zone fill input gaps by guessing. The handoff adds none at 200 bots (0 introduced). At 1,000 bots on the overloaded box, 56 of 3,122 switches introduced one. The browser client shows 0 corrections across every switch.
- **Browsers, post-merge:** each run shows one eased remote lurch of 0.6-2.8 m a few seconds after the harness's screenshot stall, while both players stand on one zone, away from any switch. Eased jumps within 1.5 s of a switch are at most 26 cm, under WP06's 30 cm "visible" line. The lurch first appeared after merging WP13/WP20 (two earlier runs had 0) and is not on the handoff path. Worth a look by WP06/WP13: the interpolation delay recovering after a render stall?
- **Failover rewinds to the last checkpoint** (spec 13): p50 14-68 m in the runs, up to `SAVE_INTERVAL_S` of walking. Progress granted on the dead zone in its last seconds can be re-granted (its write-behind queue dies with it).
- **Earshot (WP11) is still per zone.** Players on two sides of a border see each other (ghost band) but are not in each other's earshot. Fix: feed mirrored players to the earshot runner as speakers only.
- **WP13 combat profile saves are not epoch-fenced** (WP13's `SaveCombat`). A skill change on the new zone within the old zone's queue lag could be overwritten by the old owner's transfer save. Position and progress are fenced. Combat runtime state crosses handoffs, with tick timers moved as time remaining. The action log and the frame ring do not cross (a hit claim racing the switch is refused as stale).
- **Not browser-tested over WebTransport.** The border runs use WebSocket through netsim. Standby connections use the same `connectTransport` (WebTransport with fallback), and the WT endpoint rides in `ZoneTransfer.wtEndpoint`.
- **The director's assigner never rebalances live zones by load** (sticky by design). A director scaled to zero (Cloud Run) assigns nothing new, though routing and failover still work. The plan splits regions evenly only for pool zones seen in the same run (the soak starts the director after the zones).
- **Costs:** a player near a border holds one standby connection, an idle WebSocket answering nothing but kicks. Every handoff adds a position checkpoint and, when progress changed, a progress save on the old owner. Shared Postgres saw write-behind backlogs of about 300 under 1,000 bots.
- **One standby per player.** Near a corner of three zones only the nearest foreign zone is pre-connected. A transfer to the other one waits for its pre-connect, and the old owner simulates the player in the meantime.
- **Bots near the world edge or on islands** may never cross (cross mode aims at the nearest foreign region regardless of water). `-cross-frac` spawns crossers within 150 m of a border.
- **The 1000-bot soaks ran on code from before the WP13/WP20 merge.** After the merge, the browser border and failover runs and the 200-bot thailand failover soak were re-run (`*-postmerge`). All Go and TS suites pass on the merged tree.
- **CPU profiling** (`LODESTONE_ZONE_PPROF`, soak `--cpu-profile`) crashed a zone once on Windows: Go's profiler unwinding wazero JIT frames. It is opt-in, for diagnostics only.

Assumptions:
- "No duplicated items": WP14 isn't built, so the soak checks the preconditions and the grants that exist today:
  - no character owned by two zones at once (that would mean two authoritative inventories);
  - handoff states consumed exactly once (GETDEL);
  - WP12 discovery grants (once per character per site) never repeated.

  WP14 should read items on admission (spec 11: "reads happen on login and zone transfer") or register a `HandoffField`.
- The ghost band is extended with a 200 m / 10 Hz tier. The control channel is `zone:{worldId}:{zoneId}` (spec: `zone:{zoneId}`), so concurrent worlds on one Redis never collide.
- Failover position is the last Postgres checkpoint (spec 13), not the fresher presence feed.
- Shared files touched:
  - Contracts: `contracts/protocol.yaml` plus generated `contracts/ts/src/gen/protocol.ts`, `server/pkg/proto/protocol_gen.go` and `contracts/golden/protocol_vectors.json`.
  - `server/pkg`: `zoneregistry/{zoneregistry.go,_test.go}` (additive); `README.md` (row).
  - `server/director`: `main.go` (assigner start), `internal/director/director.go` (pickZone = Owner), `README.md`.
  - `server/zone/main.go` (cluster wiring, ~20 lines).
  - `server/zone/internal/game`:
    - `server.go`: Config.Cluster/IDBase, hooks in New/Start/Stop/onTick/join/leave, sorted insert, checkpoint epoch.
    - `session.go`: session.ho/joinOnce/ackFloor, verifyHello + clusterAccept in accept, remote entities in sendSnapshot.
    - `entity.go` (2 fields), `mining.go` (sorted insert, 1 line), `explore.go` (ProgressSnapshot.Epoch, 2 lines).
    - `persist.go` (fenced writes appended), `persist_progress.go` (fenced progress save).
    - `combat.go` and `towns.go` (WP13/WP20): one line each, sorted insert of enemies/NPCs.
  - `server/zone/internal/combat/handoff.go`: new file in WP13's package (Export/Restore of a player's combat state).
  - `server/zone/README.md` (section).
  - `client/src/net`: `zoneSession.ts`, `prediction.ts` (force/awaitingForced, beside main's G3 ground snap), `NetPlay.tsx` (zoneId + reconnect, social zoneId), `netStore.ts` (3 debug fields), `README.md`.
  - `tools/bots`: `bot.go`, `main.go`, `terrain.go`, `bots_test.go`, `README.md`.
- New files:
  - `server/zone/{cluster.go, internal/cluster/}`, `server/zone/internal/game/{cluster,handoff,ghosts}.go` + tests.
  - `server/director/internal/director/assign*.go`.
  - `tools/bots/{handoff.go, cluster_summary.go, handoff-soak.mjs, cmd/seedbots/}`.
  - `client/src/net/{handoff.test.ts, dev/handoff-acceptance.mjs}`.
  - `docs/cr/008-zone-handoff.md`, `docs/decisions/0016-handoff.md`, `docs/evidence/wp16-*`.

  No migration, no worldgen change.

## WP14: Loot, rarities, forging and drops ✅

Branch `wp/14-loot` (main merged in, incl. WP16). Decisions: `docs/decisions/0014-loot.md`. Contract changes: `docs/cr/010-loot.md` (new `contracts/items.yaml`, migration **0014** — run `npm run migrate` after merge —, Events 400-411, Interact 20-22 + 41 for dropped items). Package docs: `server/zone/internal/items/README.md`, `server/pkg/itemdefs/README.md`, `server/zone/README.md` "Items and loot (WP14)", `client/src/ui/inventory/README.md`.

Works:
- **One item table** `contracts/items.yaml` → `npm run gen` → zone `server/pkg/itemdefs` + client `ITEMS`: the eight rarity tiers with spec 8's colors and exact base odds (Bitchin' fixed at 1e-6 and announced), materials (sunsteel `killsDemons`, silver `demonStagger` for WP15), 10 gems × 3 cuts, 6 ores/ingots, 24 weapons over the WP13 weapon types, 15 armor pieces, arrows, consumables, ingredients, tools, tomes (`tome:<spell>` for every castable spell), gold, 15 element/theme-keyed affixes, 8 uniques, generated names, 25 recipes (smelting, forging incl. **`forge_sunsteel_blade`: 2 sunsteel ingots + the blueprint; sunsteel ingot = 2 sunsteel ore**), starter kit, loot tables. The generator refuses unknown references.
- **Rolls** (`server/zone/internal/items`), all seeded and logged: rarity with luck (elite, boss, deep cave, chest, Opal/loot find; rare+ capped at 60 %), Primordial onto starmetal/voidglass bases, affixes weighted by element and loot theme, uniques, mythic+ names, 0-3 sockets. Random loot never rolls sunsteel (forge-only). 2 M rolls land on the base odds (unit test).
- **Personal loot**: combat `OnKill` → every eligible player rolls their own (gold, equipment, family drops: meat/hides/gems/ore/blueprints/consumables, boss tomes), owner-only pickups auto-taken on touch; bosses give loot once per ISO week per character (`boss:<group>:<week>`); world containers (worldgen chunk anchors; kind and loot theme from the record, item level from region danger) listed to clients within 96 m and opened once per character straight into the bag (seed = world/container/character, so a lost roll rolls the same); WP09 vein drops go through the same grant path.
- **Inventory**: 40 slots + gem pouch + ore pouch + purse + equipment (two loadout weapons, head, body, legs), stacking with provenance merge, equip/unequip/move, socketing (free, anywhere), unsocketing (gem kept within 8 m of a town forge/anvil, destroyed elsewhere), combining 3 gems into the next cut at a waypoint, tomes teach spells, arrows set the loadout's arrowheads.
- **Gear into combat** (WP13 hooks): `combat.Player.Gear` (weapon material × level, damage/crit/spell power, armor reduction, max health, gem cut for elemental forms, WP15 `KillsDemons`/`Stagger`); the loadout's weapon type and **socketed gem element follow the equipped item** (WP13 forms read it); picking a training weapon through WP13's combat Interact unequips the item.
- **Consumables replace the stand-in draught**: the drink key spends the chosen (else strongest) consumable from the inventory with its heal; new characters get 3 healing draughts and 20 gold once (exactly-once starter grant). **Cooking** at inn hearths and campfires (Interact 22/2), recipes from items.yaml.
- **Forging at town forges**: WP20's `Config.Stations` hook records the open forge/anvil (805 still goes out and opens the client panel); smelting and forging within 7 m of it; forged items roll rarity with Smithing luck and carry the recipe's guaranteed socket; Smithing XP. **Gold**: WP20 purchases cost gold; blacksmiths also sell blueprints (`blueprint:sunsteel_blade` 40 gold).
- **Dropping** (Interact 21/2, drag out of the inventory or G) and taking (Interact 41) through **`TransferItem(from, to, reason)`** (character → world, world → character, character → character for trades later): public pickups anyone near can take, despawn after 10 minutes, a world row in Postgres restored after a zone restart; `tradeable` + `provenance` (rolled/starter/crafted/purchased/dropped/picked_up…) on every item; **local-profile drops are unverified and untradeable**.
- **Exactly once**: every item change is one transaction on an ordered per-zone journal (`internal/game/items_journal.go`), retried until it commits, absolute row state (idempotent), rolls and takes guarded by `loot_grants` markers (own-op retry = success, another op = refused); an impossible op quarantines the character (kick, reload from the DB). Local profiles: a drop's world row only appears after a blob without the item was issued; a take only lands in the blob after the world row's delete committed.
- **WP16 handoff**: the live inventory crosses zones in the handoff state (`HandoffField "items"`); item ops carry the ownership epoch and `item_epochs` fences out a stale zone; the new owner's first op is a full sync. `Server.ItemsPending(char)` for handoff flushing.
- **Client** (`client/src/ui/inventory/`): inventory screen (I) with rarity-colored tiles, tooltips (affixes, uniques, sockets, unverified/not tradeable, provenance count), equip/use/drop/socket/unsocket/combine/set-drink, drag-out to drop; crafting panel on 805/404 with have/need and blueprint state; loot toasts, refusals and the animated holographic Bitchin' banner; 3D layer for dropped items (rarity beams) and containers; E to open chests / take dropped items; `window.__lodestoneInventory`.

Evidence:
- **G4 loop** against the real zone binary on the default world (`docs/evidence/wp14-g4-loop.txt`, `TestG4LoopForgeSunsteelBlade`): a camp of two boars killed with the starting sword (2 kills, personal loot walked over and taken), a chest opened (+40 gold, items), two daylight sunsteel veins mined (4 ore) and a topaz vein (cut topaz), the Sunsteel Blade blueprint bought from the spawn town's blacksmith (66 → 26 gold), 2 sunsteel ingots smelted and the **Sunsteel Blade forged at the town forge** (1 socket), topaz socketed, blade equipped (combat_sync: socketed gem element = lightning), the respawned camp killed with it: mean hit 27.0 vs 21.3 with the starting sword; Postgres holds the blade, blueprint and loot. **PASS.**
- **Kill mid-grant never duplicates** (`docs/evidence/wp14-kill-mid-grant.txt`, `TestZoneProcessKillMidGrantNeverDuplicates`): the zone process is hard-killed (TerminateProcess) with the starter grant's transaction open, then with a container grant's transaction open, then twice right after a container grant's COMMIT (test hooks hold the transaction / the post-commit moment). After each restart the same container is opened again: the uncommitted roll is re-granted (same seed, same items), the committed one refused (already opened). Final Postgres units = starter + each container exactly once; `loot_grants` starter = 1, containers = 2; the in-game inventory matches. **PASS.**
- **Drop / pickup / despawn** (`docs/evidence/wp14-drop-pickup-despawn.txt`, `TestZoneProcessDropPickupDespawn`, zone binary + Postgres, despawn timer shortened to 8 s): a dropped potion becomes a world row (owner NULL), another player takes it (Postgres: theirs, `take:` marker, provenance starter → dropped → picked_up); dropped gold survives a zone kill + restart ("dropped items restored") and despawns on the timer with its row deleted. **Real 10-minute timer**: `TestDropPickupByAnotherPlayerAndTenMinuteDespawn` (in process, zone clock injected): present at 9:59, gone at 10:01, and another player picks up the first drop.
- Screenshots (headless Chromium, solo client with `__lodestoneInventory.mock()` sample data): `docs/evidence/wp14-inventory.png`, `wp14-inventory-tooltip.png` (red mythic "Riftreaver", unverified), `wp14-forge-panel.png`, `wp14-bitchin.png` (`node client/src/ui/inventory/dev/shots.mjs`).
- Tests: Go `internal/items` (odds, 2 M-roll distribution, every tier's shape, element/theme weighting, stacking/capacity, delta semantics, smelt → forge → socket → gear, combine, loot tables), `internal/game/items_test.go` (drop/take/10-minute despawn, local-profile unverified drop, personal kill loot + boss lockout, smelt/forge/socket/equip into combat + drink spends a potion, gold purchases, **items survive a WP16 handoff**), `items_store_test.go` (Postgres: grant + retried commit + duplicate refused, epoch fence + sync, world rows, take twice refused, expired rows deleted), process tests above (`bash server/zone/testdata_evidence_wp14.sh` re-runs them); vitest `client/src/ui/inventory/inventory.test.ts` (16).

Known gaps:
- Screenshots of the inventory/forge UI use the solo client with mock data; the live wire path is covered by the Go process tests, not by a browser run against a zone. `LootLayer` (3D dropped items/containers) is untested on screen.
- Foraging is not built: ingredients come from kills, chests and merchants. Selling to merchants and trading (UI) are not built (`TransferItem` has the character → character path).
- Arrows are not consumed (WP13 v1 fires without consuming); using an arrow stack sets the arrowheads. Moonstone's stamina bonus is data only (stamina lives in the shared movement step; night sight is WP15). `spellPowerPct` etc. apply; no other stats exist.
- Starmetal ore cannot be mined yet (WP20's crater veins are not registered with mining); starmetal comes from loot.
- Bounty completion for "deliver items" is not hooked (WP20 `CompleteBounty` exists).
- Dropped items are not mirrored across WP16 ghost bands (WP16 skips pickups); a drop near a border is visible to the owning zone only. WP13's combat profile save (loadout weapon/gem) is still not epoch-fenced (WP16 gap); items are.
- A local profile's drop appears in the world only after its next blob issue (normally immediately; the issue rides the write-behind queue). Local profiles taking a dropped item get it after the world row's delete commits (milliseconds).
- The Bitchin' global-chat line is published on the social bus (`social:{world}`, kind chat, "Lodestone") but was not exercised end to end with the social service (no Bitchin' roll happens in a test run; the in-zone Event 402 and the banner are covered).
- The client offers "[E] Take" for any pickup within 2.5 m (snapshots carry no owner); taking someone's personal pickup is refused server-side.
- `go test -race` not run (no cgo toolchain).

Assumptions:
- Pouch sizes 30 stacks each; gold is a purse stack with no slot (currency is not in the spec; WP20 prices exist).
- Armor has no sockets in v1; forged items guarantee the recipe's sockets; shop gear is plain (common).
- Smelting and forging happen at either a forge or an anvil prop; cooking at hearths (prop 4) and campfires (prop 22).
- Containers come from worldgen chunk container records only (town furniture chests are not loot).
- Item level of containers = 1 + region danger × 14; kill loot uses the enemy's level.

Shared files touched: `contracts/{events.md, README.md, codegen/gen.mjs (+ new lib/items.mjs, items.yaml), ts/src/index.ts (1 export), migrations/0014_loot.sql (new)}` and generated `contracts/ts/src/gen/items.ts`, `server/pkg/itemdefs/items_gen.json`; `server/pkg/localsave/state.go` (optional item fields, `Loot`), `server/pkg/accountapi/db.go` (upgrade copies qty/flags/provenance/name), `server/pkg/README.md` (row); `server/zone/main.go` (3 lines), `server/zone/README.md` (section); `server/zone/internal/game/{server.go (Config.Items, itm field, init/start/stop/tick/join lines), session.go (items field, loadItems, itemInteract, public pickups in snapshots), entity.go (drop field), mining.go (vein drop + takePickup hook, 5 lines), towns.go (purchases through gold), explore.go (issueLocalSave split into localState + itemsIntoLocal + local-drop flush)}`; `server/zone/internal/combat/{player.go (Gear, OnDrink, SetGear), ai.go (armor, drink heal, no refill with items), validate.go (gear in damage and crit)}`; `server/zone/internal/npc/talk.go` (blacksmith blueprints, `SmithWares`); `server/zone/internal/game/cluster_test.go` (WP16 test race fix: `ignoreTransfers` is now a flag checked in `handle`; the old channel swap let a ZoneTransfer slip past the filter, failing `TestPlayerWithoutPreconnectStaysWithOldOwner` about 1 run in 3); `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `client/src/net/NetPlay.tsx` (attach + `LootLayer`), `client/src/world/caves/caveManager.ts` (ore/gem pickups only), `client/src/world/buildings/townHud.ts` (805 → WP14 panel). New: `server/pkg/itemdefs/`, `server/zone/internal/items/`, `server/zone/internal/game/{items,items_journal,items_store,items_handoff,items_debug,loot}.go` + tests, `server/zone/{items.go, zone_items_integration_test.go, zone_g4loop_integration_test.go, testdata_evidence_wp14.sh}`, `client/src/ui/inventory/`, `docs/cr/010-loot.md`, `docs/decisions/0014-loot.md`, `docs/evidence/wp14-*`. Migration 0014 (additive): run `npm run migrate` after merge. No protocol.yaml or constants.yaml change.

## WP15: Night, demons, weather and chemistry ✅

Branch `wp/15-night` (main merged in, incl. WP16 and WP14). Decisions: `docs/decisions/0015-night-chemistry.md`. Contract changes: `docs/cr/011-wp15-night-chemistry.md`. Package docs: `server/zone/internal/chem/README.md`, `server/zone/internal/night/README.md`, `client/src/systems/README.md`, `server/pkg/weather/README.md` ("The world clock"), `server/zone/README.md` "Night, demons and chemistry (WP15)".

Works:
- **One world clock** (`server/pkg/weather/clock.go` = `client/src/systems/clock.ts`, shared vectors): world time = wall clock + the world's offset (`LODESTONE_ZONE_TIME_OFFSET_S`, same value on every zone: no per-process state, so WP16's zones agree), announced in Event 500. Night 18:30-06:00, night index, seeded 8-night moon (new moon = worst, lords walk), rain (WP10's function, now at world time on both sides), the chemistry wind (prevailing WorldSpec direction veering ±1 sector every 120 s, seeded strength). The zone's NPC schedules (WP20's `timeOfDay`/`gameDay`) and rain use it; the client's sky follows it (`worldClock.source`), the grass wind follows the chemistry wind (`setPrevailingWind`, never called before).
- **Demons** (combat.yaml v2; `internal/combat/demon.go`, `internal/night`, `internal/game/night.go`): enemies of family demon (WP13 AI, telegraphs, strikes, snapshots, drawing, bots all unchanged): Gloamling packs, seven greater demons each with one element ability, the Starless Sovereign lord (names pending approval). Health = max − seared − wounds: ordinary damage regenerates (35/25/15 % of max per s) and never takes the last point, ordinary hits stagger (≤ 1 per 1.2 s); **only killing hits sear**: a sunsteel weapon (WP14's equipped forged item: `Player.Gear.KillsDemons` from the item's material, merged and honored; or the loadout material key `sunsteel`, combat.yaml `materials.sunsteel.killsDemons`, which dev Interact 50/6 sets), sunsteel arrows, Light spells (tree `killsDemons`) and the Light form (×2). Sunrise burns demons in the open (no XP/loot), those near a lair or cave mouth flee. Spawning every 5 s at night around players in the open (moon × demonIntensity), a greater demon now and then, a lord per demon lair on new moons; demons stranded by rivers/cliffs shadow-step 6-20 m.
