Night groups never come back as camps.
- **Wards and town defense**: ward = settlement radius + 10 m (demons can't enter; players inside aren't hunted); road lanterns (WP20 props) keep lesser demons 5 m off. Seeded defense events per town per night (only if a player is near), 15 s warning, 3 waves from 2-3 gates to the ward garden (WP20's garden building), garden health 1000, ends held (XP to participants) / broken (demons roam the town until dawn) / abandoned. Events 510-514; HUD panel with the garden's health.
- **Chemistry** (`internal/chem` = `client/src/systems/chem`, line-for-line twins, Go vectors replayed bit for bit in vitest): 2 m material grid derived from terrain/water (wasm heights), settlement flags and a seeded biome mix; fire spreads on the wind (u32 hashing, integer maths, order-independent), burns ~20 s, regrows in an in-game day, makes updrafts (fed to WP10's `UpdraftField` on both sides), melts nearby ice; ice freezes water into walkable sheets for 60 s and puts fires out; Earth (Bramble Burst, Crystal Spire) raises 6 m pillars with 80° walls for 2 minutes (climbable by WP10's rules); rain douses fires and cuts spread; lightning in rain chains across water. Fire/Ice/Earth spells and forms trigger it where they land (the zone marches the aim ray/arc itself), burns on enemies ignite the grass under them, burning cells set enemies alight. Ice and pillars are a ground overlay on both sides that is a pass-through when empty (every movement golden unchanged).
- **Sync events, not results, plus the fallback behind one interface**: ops scheduled 3 steps ahead and broadcast within 256 m (Events 520-523), clients 2 steps behind; chunk baselines for newcomers (524, applied exactly at their step); 2 Hz 2-bit bitmaps (525) per active chunk for clients on the fallback (`?chem=bitmap` / Interact 51/10, or `LODESTONE_ZONE_CHEM_SYNC=bitmap`). Client `ChemView` = `EventSync` | `BitmapSync`; renderers, ground and updrafts don't know which.
- **Client**: `client/src/systems` (night runtime, clock-driven sky, HUD clock chip with moon/rain/fire, notices, defense panel), `NightLayer` (instanced flame cards leaning with the wind + 4 flickering lights, scorch, ice, pillars rising, rain streaks, demon horns and burning eyes, rise/burn flashes), `window.__lodestoneNight` dev handle.

Evidence:
- **A demon survives ordinary damage and dies to sunsteel, Light and sunrise**: in-process (`go test ./internal/combat -run Demon -v`): 40 sword hits, 3x its health in damage, still alive (min 1 hp) and whole again 3 s later; sunsteel kills it in 4 hits; softening with steel doesn't help the sunsteel; Glint (Light) kills in 4 casts; the rule table (Light spells/form yes, fire no, sunsteel blade/arrows yes, silver no); sunrise kills with no XP. Over the real zone process after the WP14 merge (`node server/zone/cmd/nightbots/acceptance.mjs`, `docs/evidence/wp15-demon.txt|json`): one bot raises a pack of 3 Gloamlings outside Draedmore's ward at 22:00: **40 s of an ordinary sword: 25 hits, 497 damage (> the pack's full health), 25 "shrugged" events, lowest health seen 55 %, 0 deaths, 3/3 alive**; the bot takes a sunsteel blade (dev Interact 50/6, `Loadout.material`; WP14's forged blade reaches the same rule through its gear): **all 3 dead in 14 s (13 hits)**; a fresh pack, the clock set to 05:59:52: **dawn burns 3/3**. **PASS**
- **Town defense with two players against the real zone process** (`docs/evidence/wp15-defense.txt|json`, zone log `wp15-defense-zone.log`): two bots (Ana: sword, Bo: spear; both with dev sunsteel) at the ward garden of Draedmore on the official world at 22:00; the ward falters (the same `StartDefense` a seeded night uses), 3 waves (7, 9, 12 demons incl. a greater Cinder Fiend from 2-3 gates on the 140 m ward edge) walk to the garden, the bots kill all 28 (Ana 10, Bo 18), garden health never dropped, **ward HELD after 175 s** (re-run after the WP14 merge; the first run: 187 s, same verdict), both rewarded 300 XP, 0 downs, 0 prediction corrections (bots predict on the zone's ground incl. buildings). **PASS**
- **Two clients see the same fire cell for cell for 60 s** (`npm run fire:accept -w client`, `docs/evidence/wp15-fire-sync.txt|json`): real zone on the official world, a 73 %-fuel meadow lit by a dev ignite; clients A and B (event path) vs the zone: **311/311 steps with identical state digests (A = B = zone)**, final 2,257 cells identical entry for entry, peak 1,182 burning cells (re-run after the WP14 merge; the first run: 2,221 cells, same verdict), 0 late ops, 8 baselines each; client C on the bitmap fallback: 127/127 bitmap bursts identical to A's state at the same step.
- Screenshots (headless Chromium, real client, `npm run night:shots -w client`): `docs/evidence/wp15-night.png` (night sky, clock chip with the moon), `wp15-demons.png`, `wp15-demons-close.png` (a pack and a greater demon rising and closing in: horns, burning eyes, telegraphs), `wp15-dawn.png` (the clock set to 05:59: "Dawn breaks. The demons burn. (4 burned)"), `wp15-fire-night.png` (a meadow fire 14 s after a dev ignite, lit by its own flames, burning-cell counter in the chip), `wp15-pillar.png` (an earth pillar), `wp15-fire-day.png` (the same fire by day); log `wp15-shots.txt`. RTX 3070 via ANGLE/D3D11, 60 fps
- Tests: Go `weather` (clock vectors, night rules, wind), `internal/chem` (vectors, spread downwind/burn-out/regrow, rain/ice, overlay ground incl. 80° walls and caves, codecs), `internal/combat` (demon rules, wards, sunrise, shadow step), `internal/night` (defense plans, wards, waves), `internal/game` (clock event + NPC clock, chem ops + baseline resync over sessions, a defense start to finish, dawn, dev sunsteel and a sunsteel kill over the session path); vitest `clock.test.ts`, `chem/chem.test.ts` (Go vectors, two-client sync, ground overlay). Every existing client test incl. the WP10/WP20 zone-process integration tests passes (goldens unchanged).

Known gaps:
- Materials stand in for scatter with a seeded biome mix (decision 0015 §9); trees don't burn as objects and burnt scatter isn't written to the WorldEdits log (`object_burned` unused). Fires are not persisted (a zone restart forgets them).
- Fire never hurts players (no environmental player damage in v1); it burns enemies and demons (ordinary damage to demons).
- The client applies ops 2 steps (400 ms) after the zone, so a player standing exactly where a pillar rises, ice forms or an updraft starts may see one prediction correction. Bots (`tools/bots`, nightbots) predict without the chemistry overlay.
- Cells outside a client's 256 m interest can diverge until it comes back in range (a fresh baseline heals them); the acceptance keeps both clients inside the fire's range.
- Network Light-magic kill is tested in-process only (the combat bot casts its slot spell only at targets its weapon can't reach); sunsteel and sunrise are tested over the network.
- Enemies (and demons) still steer straight over the heightfield (WP13 gap); demons shadow-step when stranded. ~~They walk through buildings~~ (fixed in pt/server-polish: every solid town box, i.e. the players' building collision, and closed doors block them; a stranded demon still shadow-steps, never into a building).
- Lords exist only where the atlas has demon lairs (the official world has none, so no lord walks there; dev Interact 52/3 arg 2 raises one). Demon horn/eye decoration is a client overlay on WP13's capsule bodies, not a model.
- Two zones of a WP16 cluster each run night spawning for their own players and defense events for towns near their players; a town on a region border with players on both sides could run the event on both zones (not handled). The dev "set time" Interact is per zone.
- Rain visuals are streaks only (no wet materials: WP05's `uWet` not driven); no thunder/lightning flashes; the sky's moon disc does not show the phase (the HUD does).
- `TestPlayerWithoutPreconnectStaysWithOldOwner` (WP16, `internal/game/cluster_test.go`) is flaky: it failed on this branch and on an archive of pristine main (same machine, same hour), and passed in the final full `go test` run. Not touched here.
- Spell/enemy names in combat.yaml are proposals (CR 011) pending Nicole.

Assumptions:
- "Demons rise at dusk" = 18:30 (sun just below the horizon); "sunrise" = 06:00 when the sky's sun crosses the horizon.
- demonIntensity scales numbers linearly around 0.5 (spec 16 default); 0 = no demons and no defense events.
- A defense event needs a player within town radius + 160 m at its start; anyone inside that radius during it is a participant.
- Wards cover the whole settlement radius + 10 m (the ward garden is the source; the ward is the town).
- No protocol change and no migration (Loadout.material rides the existing skill_tree JSON). Nothing to `npm run migrate`.
- Shared files touched: `contracts/{constants,combat}.yaml`, `contracts/codegen/lib/combat.mjs`, `contracts/events.md`, generated `contracts/ts/src/gen/{constants,combat}.ts`, `server/pkg/constants/constants_gen.go`, `server/pkg/combatdefs/{combat_gen.json,combatdefs.go}`, `worldgen/src/gen/constants.rs`; `server/pkg/weather` (new clock.go + README); `server/zone/main.go` (3 lines), `server/zone/README.md` (section); `server/zone/internal/game/{server.go (Config.Night, Server.night, tick + join lines), session.go (Interact 51/52 dispatch), combat.go (nightAction/nightCombatEvent hooks, dev Interact 50/6), traversal.go (world-clock rain, fire updrafts), towns.go (world clock for NPCs)}`; `server/zone/internal/combat/{world.go (GroupDemon, DemonHooks, demon state at spawn), enemy.go (Demon field, killing statuses), ai.go (regen/goal/leash/ward hooks), validate.go (KillsDemons, demonMult, applyDamage killing flag, wet chain), profile.go (Loadout.Material, SetMaterial), events.go (ActSetMaterial)}`; client `net/NetPlay.tsx` (attach + layer + HUD), `world/core/runtime.ts` (ground = ChemGround), `world/WorldScene.tsx` (clock source), `state/live.ts` (worldClock.source), `sim/stepEnv.ts` (world-clock rain, fire updrafts), `client/package.json` (scripts). New: `server/zone/{night.go, internal/chem/, internal/night/, internal/game/night.go, internal/combat/demon.go, cmd/nightbots/}`, `client/src/systems/`.

## WP19: Scale and performance hardening ⚠️ (scenarios 2, 3 and 4-voice/handoff PASS; scenario 1 tick p99 borderline)

Branch `wp/19-scale` (main merged in, incl. WP14 and WP15). Results, players per zone, bandwidth, cost and next steps: `docs/decisions/v1-capacity.md`. Evidence: `docs/evidence/wp19-*`. Package notes: `server/zone/README.md` "Scale (WP19)", `server/zone/internal/combat/README.md`, `server/pkg/README.md` (snapshot row), `tools/bots/README.md` "Scale soaks", `server/social/voice/voicebots/README.md` "Scenario 4", `client/src/net/README.md` "Diagnostics", `client/README.md` (qualityStore row).

Results (one shared Windows desktop, bots on the same machine; zone at `GOMAXPROCS=4`):
- **Scenario 1** (300 bots in region (1,1) + 2,000 awake enemies, `node tools/bots/scale-soak.mjs`): before tick p50 ~250 ms / worst 60 s p99 811 ms (zone at ~4 Hz); after p50 8.5-10 ms, 60 s p99 11.3-11.4 ms in quiet windows and 13-31 ms in noisy ones, downlink max 158.3 kbps (budget 160), 2,006 awake. Thailand: p50 10 ms, p99 14-17 ms, downlink max 159.2 kbps. In-process benchmark (no sockets): p50 5.5 ms, p99 7.9-9.4 ms (before: p50 136-146 ms). **FAIL on the strict p99**, PASS on downlink.
- **Scenario 2** (1,000 bots, 4 zones, crossings, 5 min): PASS before and after; busiest zone p99 53 ms -> 13.4 ms at ~430 players, 52 -> 10.5 ms at ~350.
- **Scenario 3** (50 voice bots): PASS before (864/864, p99 271 ms, 0 flaps) and after (868/868, p99 277 ms, 0 flaps).
- **Scenario 4**: voice under thailand PASS (864/864, p99 286 ms); scenario 2 under thailand + hard kill of a pool zone PASS (1000/1000, 71/71 failovers p50 4.9 s, 0 owned twice, 0 duplicated grants); scenario 1 under thailand FAIL (p99 above).
- **Remote "lurch"** (WP16 gap): real; caused by auto quality stepping high -> medium and recompiling every shader synchronously (~4.7 s freeze on ANGLE/D3D11, both headless pages at once). Fixed with `shaderTier` (auto steps change only cheap knobs): 0 programs compiled, eased remote discontinuities 0 cm / 0 cm (before 90-211 cm, 5/5 runs). `docs/evidence/wp19-lurch-investigation.txt`.
- **Client render benchmarks**: traversal high: draw calls 303, visible tris 1.19 M, heap 78 MB, chunk gen p95 5.9 ms, download 4.8 MB, first playable 6.7 s, **1 long task (347 ms)**; low: 132 calls, **2 long tasks (max 157 ms)**. Towns: 167-292 draw calls per view, unchanged from WP20.

Fixes (wire format unchanged; byte-equivalence tests: `snapshot/equiv_test.go` vs the pre-WP19 encoder, `TestGatherInterestMatchesReference`, `TestParallelSnapshotsMatchSerial`, combat `scale_test.go`):
- Zone: parallel snapshot phase; bitset interest in id order (mirrors sorted once per tick); second AOI grid for non-players (200 m); compact per-tick entity views; `Sqrt` distances; `/debug/combat` on demand; `zone_combat_awake`; tick phase timing in `/debug/tickstats` + slow-tick log; dev knob `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEBUG_HORDE`; benchmark `scale_bench_test.go`.
- Snapshot encoder: lazy diffs, heap selection, compact pointer-free baseline frames with interned names (the GC scanned ~700 MB of rings before), `Known`/`Knows`.
- Combat: `windupsOn` index; player grid during `Step` (acquire, playersNear, wake).
- Client: `qualityStore.shaderTier`; interpolation jump diagnostics; `window.__lodestone.quality()`.
- Tools: `tools/bots/scale-soak.mjs` (scenario 1, affinity pinning, phases, `--pprof`), `handoff-soak.mjs` (`--zone-bin`, `--evidence-prefix`, unique binary names), bots read `/debug/zone` via `-metrics` (netsim runs), voicebots `-netsim`, `handoff-acceptance.mjs` (`--prefix`, `--cpu-profile`).

Known gaps:
- **Scenario 1 tick p99** meets 12 ms only in quiet windows on this shared desktop; the serial tick (~5 ms: combat AI 2-3 ms, input + movement 1.5-2 ms) does not parallelize yet. Next: Linux VM with dedicated cores, then move movement steps onto the workers (needs the combat frame hooks split) and partition combat AI (`v1-capacity.md` "what to do").
- **Downlink headroom**: reliable events ride on top of the 150 kbps snapshot budget; crowds reached 158-159 kbps of 160. Charge reliable bytes to the budget or lower the default.
- **Uneven zone split after failover**: the director's sticky plan left one pool zone with 612 players (p99 49 ms) in the thailand failover run; no load rebalancing (WP16 gap).
- **Traversal long tasks** (347 ms high, 157 ms low) and first playable 6.7 s at high are regressions since WP05, most likely first-use shader compiles of newer materials; spec 12 shader warmup per tier at load is still not built (also removes the freeze of an explicit quality change).
- Auto quality on a weak GPU now adapts only with resolution, view distance, scatter, grass and terrain detail; shadows/post change only with an explicit setting.
- Snapshot baselines for a 300-client crowd are ~660 MB per zone (pointer-free now); size zone VMs accordingly.
- Cost table in `v1-capacity.md` is list-price estimates plus measured traffic, not a real bill (none exists: nothing is deployed).
- `go test -race` not run (no cgo toolchain); all numbers from one shared machine.

Assumptions:
- "4 vCPU" = `GOMAXPROCS=4` for the zone (also measured pinned to 2 physical cores and at 8 procs); scenario 1's "one region" = a 300 m disc centred at (1700, 1700) in region (1,1), enemies placed as camps of four by the horde knob (they wake by the normal 96 m rule; 2,006 awake throughout).
- The scenario 1 verdict uses the worst 60 s window p99 after all bots joined (t >= 60 s), sampled every 5 s.
- Baselines were built from the merge-base `da7e882` (main before WP14/WP15) plus the horde knob; the after runs include WP14 and WP15.

Files touched: `server/pkg/snapshot/{codec.go, compact.go (new), equiv_test.go (new)}`, `server/pkg/README.md`; `server/zone/internal/aoi/aoi.go`; `server/zone/internal/combat/{ai.go, enemy.go, world.go, scale_test.go (new), README.md}`; `server/zone/internal/game/{server.go, session.go (old sendSnapshot removed), ghosts.go, handoff.go (1 field), entity.go (2 fields), explore.go (1 line), combat.go (horde knob, debug on demand, awake metric), snapshots.go (new), phases.go (new), scale_bench_test.go (new), snapshots_test.go (new)}`, `server/zone/main.go` (phases in /debug/tickstats), `server/zone/README.md`; `server/social/voice/voicebots/{main.go, README.md}`; `tools/bots/{main.go, handoff-soak.mjs, scale-soak.mjs (new), README.md}`; `client/src/{devHandle.ts, net/interpolation.ts, net/dev/handoff-acceptance.mjs, net/README.md, scene/GameCanvas.tsx, scene/SkyAndSun.tsx, stores/qualityStore.ts, stores/stores.test.ts, world/WorldScene.tsx, world/buildings/TownsLayer.tsx}`, `client/README.md`; `infra/cost/README.md` (pointer); `docs/decisions/v1-capacity.md` (new), `docs/evidence/wp19-*` (new), this section. No contract, protocol, migration or worldgen change.

### Second pass (WP19b, branch `wp/19b-scale`, main merged in)

Fixes for the four G5 failures the first pass left, then every scenario re-run. Details, A/B tables and remaining gaps: `docs/decisions/v1-capacity.md` "Second pass". Evidence: `docs/evidence/wp19b-*`. The desktop was far busier than on 2026-11-08 (other agents' headless Chromium at up to 14 cores, their soaks), so live numbers are same-day A/B against the main-branch zone binary (`--zone-bin`), with the in-process benchmark as the noise-free reference.

Results:
- **Scenario 1** (300 bots + 2,000 awake enemies, 4 procs): baseline today p50 8.9-9.3 ms, every 60 s window p99 12.4-13.7 ms, downlink max 159.3 kbps; after (4 quiet runs, one on the code merged with main's playtest work): p50 6.5-8.2 ms, p99 8.5-10.7 ms in most windows, but one or two bursts per run lift a window to 14.5-17.7 ms (28-50 ms while another agent's browsers ran); downlink max 148.2-150.6 kbps. **FAIL on the strict p99** (bursts of slow parallel phases on the shared desktop), downlink PASS with headroom. Thailand: PASS once on a quiet machine (every window <= 10.4 ms, downlink 151.8 kbps); the merged run 10-10.5 ms between two bursts (24.8 ms windows); in the busy hour both builds ran 2-3x slower.
- **Scenario 2** (1,000 bots, 4 zones): **PASS**, 1000/1000, 0 owned twice, 0 duplicated; the director made 4 load moves and the zones ended at 263/254/251/231 players (baseline 440/370/103/86).
- **Scenario 4, S2 + thailand + hard kill**: failover works (216/216 failovers, 0 owned twice, 0 duplicated), loads after failover 370/305/305 (first pass 612/284/99), but the run **FAILs** the "playing but unowned for two samples" check (4 bots; run 1: 11) at zone tick p99 270-390 ms. The main-branch zone binary fails the same check in the same conditions (6 bots, p99 266-290 ms), so this is the overloaded desktop, not WP19b; not proven on a quiet machine.
- **Scenario 3 / 4-voice**: **PASS**: 872/872 earshot changes reflected, p99 268 ms, 0 flaps; under thailand 870/870, p99 270 ms, 0 flaps. (A run while another agent's Chromium used 14 cores missed the 500 ms p99: 557 ms, `wp19b-s3-after-50-busy`.)
- **Client**: traversal long tasks 1 (357 ms) / 2 (62, 175 ms) -> **0 / 0**, 0 shader programs linked during traversal; first playable high 7.2 -> 3.7-5.0 s, low 2.8 -> 2.0-2.5 s. Turn benchmark (main's `bench/turn.mjs`): 0 frames over 50 ms and 0 long tasks in 14/14 runs (high and low, before and after merging main's playtest work), worst frame 33.4 ms. After that merge: main's audio engine synthesized its noise and reverb IR in one go (67-86 ms long task on the first key press), now in slices; left on low: one 52 ms AudioContext creation on the first key press, which only `?autoplay=1` moves off the title screen.

Fixes (wire format and determinism unchanged; `npm run test:golden` PASS):
- Zone tick on workers (`internal/game/par.go`): inputs of plain-movement sessions (`inputs.go`, exact), combat AI partitioned by group (`internal/combat/par.go`, deterministic for any worker count), enemy mirror and combat event delivery per session, view extrapolation + quantization per entity; the pool waits for items, not goroutines. Writers held during the tick (`transport.Holder`). `playerUpkeep` in id order. Tests: `TestPartitionedAIMatchesAcrossWorkers`, `TestParallelTickMatchesSerial`, `TestHoldDefersWakeups`.
- Downlink: reliable bytes charged to the per-client budget (`snapshot.Encoder.Charge`, `transport.Stats.ReliableQueued`; decision 0005 amendment).
- Director load moves (`zoneregistry.Rebalance`, `Entry.Load` per-region counts in the heartbeat, one move per 10 s, held two cooldowns after a Plan change; decision 0016 note; `LODESTONE_DIRECTOR_ONLY_WORLD` for soaks on shared Redis, where the user's long-running dev director otherwise took every soak world's assignment lease: first-pass S2/S4 runs were assigned by it).
- Client shader warm-up (`client/src/world/warmup.ts`, `warmExtras.ts`): `compileAsync` of the scene plus town/cave-rock/avatar/weapon/enemy-bar variants on a render target before the veil lifts and after an explicit shader-tier change; `?nowarmup` to measure without it. Traversal benchmark lists programs linked per phase with cache keys. Audio engine noise and reverb IR synthesized in slices (`client/src/audio/{ambience,sfx}.ts`).
- Tools: `scale-soak.mjs --prefix`, post-ramp window and GC column; `handoff-soak.mjs` dedicated director and load-move log; `turn.mjs`/`traversal.mjs` `LODESTONE_BENCH_QUERY`, `LODESTONE_BENCH_PREFIX`; scale benchmark on real terrain (`LODESTONE_BENCH_TERRAIN=1`) with per-phase means.

Known gaps:
- Scenario 1 strict p99 on this desktop: six parallel phases per tick now meet OS descheduling and GC bursts; needs a dedicated 4 vCPU (Linux) measurement, which this pass could not do.
- Thailand runs (S1, S2+kill, voice) could not be measured cleanly today; the main-branch binary fails the same way in the same conditions.
- Scenario 4 S2 + kill: "playing but unowned for two samples" under extreme tick times (baseline fails it too).
- `go test -race` still not run (no cgo); the parallel phases were designed so workers only write their own items.

Files touched: `server/zone/internal/game/{par.go (new), inputs.go (new), server.go, session.go, combat.go, snapshots.go, handoff.go, metrics.go, scale_bench_test.go, snapshots_test.go}`, `server/zone/internal/combat/{par.go (new), par_test.go (new), ai.go, enemy.go, world.go, README.md}`, `server/zone/{cluster.go, README.md}`, `server/zone/internal/cluster/cluster.go`, `server/pkg/{transport/{transport.go, websocket.go, webtransport.go, hold_test.go (new)}, snapshot/codec.go, zoneregistry/{zoneregistry.go, rebalance.go (new), rebalance_test.go (new)}, README.md}`, `server/director/{main.go, internal/director/{assign.go, director.go, assign_test.go}, README.md}`, `client/src/audio/{ambience.ts, sfx.ts, README.md}`, `client/src/world/{warmup.ts (new), warmExtras.ts (new), WorldScene.tsx, WorldCanvas.tsx, debug/worldDebug.ts, bench/traversal.mjs, bench/turn.mjs, README.md}`, `tools/bots/{scale-soak.mjs, handoff-soak.mjs, README.md}`, `docs/decisions/{0005, 0016, v1-capacity}.md`, `docs/evidence/wp19b-*`, this section. No contract, protocol, migration or worldgen change.

## Playtest 1: progression and difficulty ✅

Branch `pt/progression`. Nicole's playtest feedback: demons unbeatable early (no sunsteel or Light yet, many every night), "I am level 4 but idk what I can do with that, nor do I feel any stronger", and "what is the giant tower with the crystal? that should do something". Decisions: `docs/decisions/0021-discovery-difficulty.md`. Contract: `docs/cr/012-progression-difficulty.md` (combat.yaml v3, items.yaml slime loot, Events 900-903 in the new range 900-949, Interact 10 action 2, migration **0021**). Package docs: `server/zone/internal/difficulty/README.md`, `client/src/ui/progression/README.md`, `server/zone/README.md` "Progression and difficulty (playtest 1)".

Works:
- **Discovery-stamped difficulty.** The world is cut into 256 m areas (4 x 4 chunks, the combat group grid; 256 areas). The first player to come within 200 m of an unstamped area stamps its level from their character level, pulled halfway to the mean of the stamped neighbours and clamped within 3 levels of them, plus up to 2 from the generator's region danger (`difficulty.Propose`). Stamps live in Postgres `area_difficulty`, inserted first-writer-wins (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` + read back), so every zone of a world agrees; a stamp never changes. The **Haven** (areas within 420 m of the world spawn, 8 areas on the official world) is level 1 forever and never stored. On the tick every lookup is an array index; claims go to one worker goroutine over channels; a failed claim falls back to a provisional level retried after 30 s. Worldgen is untouched (golden hashes unchanged).
- **Tiers** (combat.yaml `difficulty.tiers`): Haven (slimes only, no elites, no bosses, no demons), Quiet Lands 1-3 (half slimes, no elites, no demons), The Wilds 4-7 (elites x0.5, demons x0.35 and slowed below walking speed, no greater demons), Perilous Lands 8-12, Dreadlands 13-19 (WP15's full night, lords), The Deadlands 20+. Atlas groups spawn at their area's level and tier; night spawns, greater demons, lords and town defense events follow the tier; players below level 6 are not hunted within 90 m of a ward. WP15's rule is intact (ordinary damage never kills a demon). New beginner family: `slime` (Dewdrop Slime) and `moss_slime` (Mossback Slime), slow, soft-hitting, 3-4 hits.
- **Leveling that matters** (combat.yaml `leveling`): +12 health, +10 % damage on every attack/spell/DOT (equal to enemy health growth, so at-level fights keep their hit counts), +2 stamina wheel, +1 spell skill point per level; spell tree tiers unlock with level (4-5 at 3, 6-7 at 6, 8-9 at 10, domains at 14, pinnacles at 18). A level-up refills health and stamina and sends Event 900; the client shows a **fanfare** (Level N, the exact gains and unlocks, a synthesized chime) and the **character panel (C)**: level, XP bar, stats, the area you stand in and who shaped it, the next levels' gains and unlocks, spells a skill point can learn now (Light first, "kills demons": Glint is learnable from level 1 with the starting 2 points), weapon skills and their next technique.
- **The crystal tower**: within 45 m of an unclimbed tower the HUD says "Climb to the crystal to reveal this land"; on the top "[E] Attune to the crystal" with the boon. Attuning (Interact 10/2, server-validated on the top) once per tower gives +8 stamina, +5 health, +1 skill point and +100 XP permanently (`character_progress.attuned`), and Event **902 `tower_attuned`** (site, region, crystal position, region name): the hook for `pt/ux`'s discovery intro.

Evidence:
- **Process-level** (`go test ./server/zone -run TestZoneProcessDiscoveryDifficulty`, zone binaries + scratch Postgres, 22:00 world time; `docs/evidence/pt-progression-process.txt`): a fresh level-1 character at a Haven guardian site 205 m from the spawn: area level 1 / Haven, the group spawned 3 Mossback Slimes at level 1, 0 demons within 300 m after 12 s of night; a level-15 character at a pack 2.4 km out stamps area level 17 (15 + danger bonus) in Postgres, the pack spawns at level >= 15 with no slimes, 4 demons hunt them; a second zone process reads the same stamp; both zones race for one fresh area with a level-9 and a level-1 player: one row, both see the same level, exactly one "stamped by you". **PASS**.
- In process (`internal/game/progression_test.go`): Haven at night (slimes only, 0 demons over 7 s), level-15 stamp (harder enemies, demons hunt, stamp stored), quiet lands next to the Haven (no demons), two zones on one store agree, the wilds at night (at most the tier's count, all lesser, slower than walking; none for a level-1 player beside a town), level-up stats (+health/+stamina/+damage, full refill in SelfState), attunement once (refused off the top and the second time, saved), wire vectors shared with vitest.
- Feel (`internal/combat/sim_test.go`): `TestLevelCurveKeepsFightsSnappy` (boar, wolf, cutthroat, brute, sentinel, ember imp with the training sword: 4/4/4/5/4/4 hits at levels 1, 10, 20 and 30; a level-12 character needs 3 (brute 4) against level-4 ones), `TestBeginnerTimeToKill` (slimes 3-4 hits, 2.5-5 s, hits <= 7), every WP13 common/elite test unchanged.
- Screenshots (headless Chromium, solo client + the dev handle feeding the zone's Events; `node client/src/ui/progression/dev/shots.mjs`): `docs/evidence/pt-progression-levelup.png`, `pt-progression-character-panel.png`, `pt-progression-tower-prompt.png`, `pt-progression-tower-attune.png`, `pt-progression-tower-attuned.png`, log `pt-progression-shots.txt`.

Known gaps:
- Container item levels (WP14 chests) still follow region danger; kill XP is not reduced for enemies far below the player.
- The G4 browser acceptance (`tools/acceptance/g4.mjs`) waits 90 s for natural demons outside the spawn town before raising them with the dev Interact: the spawn town is now the Haven, so it will always take the dev path (by design).
- The client shows the attunement as a notice only; the cinematic is `pt/ux`'s (Event 902).
- Bots (`tools/bots`, combat/night bots) predict with the default 100-point stamina wheel; a levelled bot character gets one correction when its wheel grows.
- Checks (after merging main incl. G4): gen:check, build:wasm, test:golden, go vet, typecheck, eslint, `npm test -w client` (31 files), `npm run lint` all green; `go test` every module green except three timing/socket tests that failed once each in full runs on the shared, loaded machine and pass alone, untouched by this branch: `gameloop.TestLoopTicksAtRate`, `social.TestTwoProcessesAcrossZones`, `zone.TestZoneProcessWebTransport`.
- The tier names, slime names and boon numbers are proposals for Nicole.
- `go test -race` not run (no cgo toolchain).

Assumptions:
- "Discovered" = within 200 m (beyond the 160 m enemy spawn radius), not "entered", so enemies never pop in at an area border.
- Party members discovering together: whoever's claim reaches the database first stamps it (usually the one in front).
- Level-ups refill health and stamina (the "moment"); attunement boons are derived from the attuned set, never stored separately.
- Migration to apply: **`npm run migrate` (0021) before deploying this zone**: the zone's progress load/save reads and writes `character_progress.attuned`, and stamps need `area_difficulty`. Without it progress saves fail and retry (logged) and claims fall back to provisional levels.
- Shared files touched: `contracts/{combat.yaml, items.yaml, events.md, codegen/lib/combat.mjs}` + generated `contracts/ts/src/gen/{combat,items}.ts`, `server/pkg/{combatdefs/combat_gen.json, combatdefs/combatdefs.go, itemdefs/items_gen.json, localsave/state.go (optional attuned), accountapi/db.go (upgrade copies attuned)}`; `server/zone/main.go` (2 lines); `server/zone/internal/combat/{world.go (Areas hook, areaReady, tier in composition), demon.go (LevelAt), player.go (Boons, SetLevel), profile.go (LearnWith, tier gate), validate.go (level damage), events.go (skill points), sim_test.go}`; `server/zone/internal/explore/{explore.go (Attune), progress.go (Attuned)}`; `server/zone/internal/night/night.go` (NearWard); `server/zone/internal/game/{server.go (Config.Difficulty, init/start/tick/join lines), explore.go (levelChanged, attune action, attuned persistence), combat.go (levelChanged, LearnWith), night.go (tier hooks, caveSys fix), persist_progress.go (attuned column), explore_test.go (fake store copies attuned)}`; `server/zone/README.md`; client `src/net/prediction.ts` (stamina wheel in the agreement; a replay that only adopts a grown/refilled wheel with motion in agreement counts as `wheelResyncs`, not a correction: a level-up mid-walk would otherwise fail WP20's 0-correction walk-into-building test), `src/net/net.test.ts` (one test), `src/net/NetPlay.tsx` (attach), `src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx` (mount), `src/input/useKeyboard.ts` (KeyC no longer crouches). New: `contracts/migrations/0021_progression_difficulty.sql`, `server/zone/internal/difficulty/`, `server/zone/internal/game/{difficulty,progression}.go` + `progression_test.go`, `server/zone/{difficulty.go, zone_progression_integration_test.go}`, `client/src/ui/progression/`, `docs/cr/012-progression-difficulty.md`, `docs/decisions/0021-discovery-difficulty.md`, `docs/evidence/pt-progression-*`.

## Playtest 1: UX, quests and presentation ✅

Branch `pt/ux` (main merged in, incl. G4 and the mouse-look fix; sub-branches `pt/ux-doors`, `pt/ux-weapons`, `pt/ux-intros` merged). Contract change: `docs/cr/013-playtest-ux-quests.md` (Events 950-955 and 970-972, Interact 34 and 60, player `typeId` weapon look, Event 212 tail, migration **0023**: run `npm run migrate` after merge). Package docs: `server/zone/README.md` "Quests", `client/src/ui/quests/README.md`, `client/src/combat/README.md` (Controls, health bars, Weapons), `client/src/ui/intro/README.md`, `client/src/world/buildings/README.md` and `server/pkg/towns/README.md` (doors), `client/README.md` (`hud/layout.ts`), `client/src/ui/map/README.md` (quest markers). Screenshot harness: `cd client && npx vite-node src/ui/quests/dev/pt-ux-shots.ts [--before]` (real client + zone binary, official world, private ports, no DB).

Nicole's feedback, item by item:

- **"Map markers for quests you take on, and the option to see where a quest will take place, should you accept."** Quests are now a server-authoritative, persisted log (`server/zone/internal/quests` + `internal/game/quests.go`): accepted bounties (board or bounty keeper; WP20 kept them in zone memory only) and the starter story quest. Every step carries map markers (points or area circles). Client (`client/src/ui/quests`): a tracker under the compass (step, count, "Sunsteel ore: 500 m south-west"), notes for new quests/steps/completions, the journal (J: track, show on map, hand in, abandon); markers on the map (M; tracked in gold, areas as circles), on the compass (gold diamond, nearest labelled with its distance) and in the world (a soft gold beam over each marker of the tracked quest). Before accepting: an offer card lists every step with distance/direction and area size, a small map of all the places, and "Show on map" (the full map framed on a cyan dashed preview); every bounty notice says where it takes you and has its own "Show on map". Persistence: `character_quests` (migration 0023, epoch-fenced like WP16 checkpoints), local profiles' save blob, a `quests` HandoffField across zones. Completion for every bounty kind: clear (the site's group wiped with you eligible, or arriving while it is quiet), slay (a demon kill within 250 m of the site), explore (reach it), deliver (handed in at that town's board or keeper, automatically when you read it). Evidence: `docs/evidence/pt-ux-quest-tracker.png`, `pt-ux-quest-offer.png`, `pt-ux-quest-offer-map.png`, `pt-ux-quest-step.png`, `pt-ux-quest-map.png`, `pt-ux-quest-compass.png` (compass diamond + beams), `pt-ux-bounty-offer.png`, `pt-ux-bounty-preview-map.png`, `pt-ux-quest-journal.png`, `pt-ux-quest-map-bounties.png`, log `pt-ux-shots.txt`.
- **"Demons are really hard early on because you don't know where to get sunsteel or light magic."** Starter quest **"Ward against the night"** starts on a character's first login at the nearest town: talk to the blacksmith (25 gold and a line explaining that only sunsteel, Light or sunrise kill demons, and where the seam is) → mine 4 sunsteel ore (marked: the nearest cave whose sunlit chambers hold sunsteel veins, e.g. "Faeldstoich Deep, 490 m south-west of Draedmore", plus its mouth) → buy the Sunsteel Blade blueprint → forge it (forge marked) → seek Light magic at the nearest shrine ("The Forgotten Shrine, 200 m north-east"; arriving hands over a Tome of Glint) → read it (Glint drops into the first empty spell slot). 30 gold + 200 XP. A blacksmith re-offers it (with the preview) if dropped. An in-process test on the official world walks it end to end.
- **"The number keys are hard to use for spells. Scroll to a spell and right click, or the number keys."** The mouse wheel cycles what the right button uses: the weapon's secondary (the default, so WP13's feel is unchanged) or a filled spell slot; the selected box is raised with a gold "RMB" badge; right click casts it; 1-4 still cast directly (and select). Parry/draw/charge/heavy also on **hold Z** at any time. Hint line: "LMB attack · Wheel select · RMB use selected · Z parry/draw/charge · 1-4 cast · F dodge · Q form". Selection is client-only (the same input bits reach the zone), so WP13's timeline tests pass unchanged. Evidence: `pt-ux-spell-wheel.png`, `pt-ux-spell-wheel-cast.png`; tests `client/src/combat/spellSelect.test.ts`.
- **"Should be able to close doors."** (`pt/ux-doors`.) Building doors (inns, houses, farms, workshops...; forges have open fronts) open/close with E ("[E] Open door" / "[E] Close door"), replicated to everyone within 260 m of the town (Event 970), baselines for newcomers (971), refusals (972: e.g. someone in the doorway). Door boxes come straight from the towns buffer, identical f32s in TS (`worldgen/js/src/doors.ts`) and Go (`server/pkg/towns/doors.go`), 1,092 push vectors replayed bit for bit; closed doors are an overlay after the WP20 town collision, and doors start open (WP20 goldens and its walk-in acceptance untouched). Each change is stamped with the movement step it applies at, per recipient, so prediction and the zone switch on the same step. NPCs open doors on their route, walk through and close them behind. **Walk-through agreement** (`docs/evidence/pt-ux-door-walkthrough.txt`; Node client predicting with TS vs the zone binary on the official world: close, walk into it, open, walk in, close behind, push, open, out; the inn and two houses direct and the inn under ~190 ms RTT netsim): **0 corrections, max divergence 0.81 cm**, stopped exactly at the 0.55 m limit on both sides. Screenshots `pt-ux-door-closed.png`, `pt-ux-door-open.png`.
- **"Health bars are fixed to a player's head; rotate 180 degrees and see no health bar."** Enemy health bars (and elite/boss name labels, new) sit in a plate turned to face the camera every frame (`faceCamera`, double-sided); they used to turn with the body. Player nameplates were already sprites. Evidence: the same idle camp from four sides at 17 m, before (main: bars missing or edge-on from some sides) and after: `pt-ux-healthbars-{before,after}-{north,south,east,west}.png`; test `billboard.test.ts`.
- **"Take the location intros from 3d-portfolio-site."** (`pt/ux-intros`.) A grand title card ported from the portfolio's region reveal (`HudToasts.tsx` `botw-title` / `gem-label` / `botw-rule`, Fraunces with a Georgia fallback, its 3.4 s ease-in-out timing stretched per size, blur-in, letter-spacing settle, a glowing diamond rule) plus a synthesized WebAudio sting (the reference is silent). Region (tower reveal 600, first entry this session), major (602 discoveries: towns, towers, caves, structures, landmarks), minor (601 waypoints), quiet name cards when re-entering known towns; queued, never overlapping, pointer-events none. **902 `tower_attuned`** (CR 012, pt/progression) is wired too. Credited in `CREDITS.md` and `client/src/ui/intro/README.md`. Evidence: `pt-ux-intro-discovery.png` (mid-animation), `pt-ux-intro-frames-{1,2,3}.png`, `-town`, `-tower`, `-region`, `-region-entry`, `-1080`, `-800`, `-reference.png`.
- **"The lower part of the HUD is vertically crowded, with UI elements overlapping."** One centre stack at the bottom (hotbar, health bar with the numbers inside, mana, key hints) measured into CSS variables (`client/src/hud/layout.ts`); the lock hint sits above it instead of across it; chat and voice stay in the corners at a width that never reaches the centre column and lift above the stack below 1080 px wide; the party frame and loot toasts follow. Before/after: `pt-ux-hud-{before,after}-{1280x720,1920x1080,800x700}.png`.
- **"Weapons don't look like weapons."** (`pt/ux-weapons`.) Procedural sword (fullered, bevelled blade, curved guard, wrapped grip, pommel), spear (ash shaft, leaf head, binding, butt spike), recurve bow (string pulls back, arrow nocked while drawing), staff (gnarled shaft, tines cradling the gem), wand (carved, ferrule, claw-held gem tip); one draw call each, ≤ 1.6k triangles in first person, ≤ 0.6k third person with a far LOD; the **sunsteel blade** is warm gold-white with glowing edges and a shimmer; the socketed gem shows in its element colour. Other players hold their weapon: the player entity's `typeId` carries weapon | material | gem (set on change only). Evidence: `pt-ux-weapon-{sword,spear,bow,staff,wand,sunsteel,sunsteel-night,remote}.png` and `pt-ux-weapon-before-*.png`.

Gates on the merged branch (one shared desktop, other agents' soaks running): `gen:check`, `build:wasm`, `go vet`, `typecheck`, `eslint .`, `npm run lint` green; `npm test -w client` 313/313 (one parallel-run failure of the door walk-through process test passed alone and on the full rerun); `go test ./...` green except load-timing process tests that pass when rerun alone (`TestZoneProcessDropPickupDespawn` waits 5 s for a restart log line, `TestZoneProcessTerrain`/`WebTransport` "never became healthy", `TestG4LoopForgeSunsteelBlade` lost hits under load, social `TestTwoProcessesAcrossZones`, known flaky); `test:golden` has **0 hash failures** on every host but Chromium's p95 chunk-time budget (8 ms) read 8.8-24 ms under the machine's load (worldgen and the wasm are untouched by this branch).

New controls: mouse wheel (select what the right button uses), right mouse (use it: cast the selected spell, or parry/draw/charge/heavy with the weapon selected), hold Z (parry/draw/charge/heavy), J (journal), E on a door (open/close).

Tests: Go `internal/quests` (every objective kind, limits, wire, round trip), `internal/game/quests_test.go` (the Ward end to end; every bounty kind on today's board accepted, persisted over a reconnect, delivered, explored; abandon; the handoff field), door tests (`server/pkg/towns`, `internal/game/doors_test.go`), `weaponlook_test.go`; vitest `ui/quests/quests.test.ts`, `combat/spellSelect.test.ts`, `combat/billboard.test.ts`, `ui/intro/intro.test.ts`, `world/buildings/doors.test.ts` + `doorWalk.integration.test.ts`, `combat/weapons/weapons.test.ts`, `net/avatar.test.ts`.

Known gaps:
- Quest texts and the Ward's step order are proposals (one place: `server/zone/internal/quests/content.go`).
- No spellbook/skill-tree screen yet: the Ward teaches Light with a tome that slots itself; other trees still need tomes.
- NPC requests other than bounties don't exist yet (bounty keeper jobs are the NPC requests); slay bounties need a night and a demon near the site; a clear bounty at a site with no enemy group completes on arrival.
- The account upgrade (local profile -> account) does not copy the quest log; a dead zone's unsaved quest changes are lost like other progress (WP16 gap).
- Doors: bots predict without doors; the zone's view extrapolation ignores doors. (~~State in zone memory, per zone in a cluster~~: persisted and owned by the region's zone since pt/server-polish.)
- Remote avatars hold their weapon at rest only (remote swings aren't replicated); the intro sting was never heard in headless runs.
- There is still no shader warm-up list on main (WP19 second pass): the new weapon material, quest beam and doors compile on first use.

Assumptions:
- Quests are their owner's only (no shared party quests); the starter quest auto-starts as a tutorial (the offer card is for re-offers and future NPCs); quest proximity checks run on the authoritative position at 2 Hz.
- Shared files touched: `contracts/events.md` (rows, ranges), `server/pkg/localsave/state.go` (`Quests`), `server/zone/internal/game/{server.go (field, init, tick, join, leave lines), session.go (field, load, Interact 60, door frame ground), towns.go (bounties through the log, talk hook, doors), items.go (inventory recheck, tome -> slot, weapon look), combat.go (spell recheck, weapon look), explore.go (local blob)}`, `server/zone/internal/npc/{talk.go (Bounty.Item, EncodeBountiesState), npc.go (door gate)}`, `client/src/net/NetPlay.tsx` (quest hook + layer), `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx` (QuestHud), `client/src/ui/map/{MapScreen,Compass,store,ExplorationHud}.tsx`, `client/src/world/buildings/{townHud,townEvents,interact,kit,townsManager,townsDebug}.ts`, `client/src/world/core/runtime.ts` (door ground), `client/src/net/prediction.ts` (replay from a door step), `client/src/sim/localSim.ts`, `client/src/combat/{runtime,store,CombatHud,CombatLayer,events,timeline}.ts(x)`, `client/src/net/avatar.ts`, `client/src/scene/FirstPersonArms.tsx`, `client/src/hud/Overlays.tsx`, `client/src/social/ui/{ChatPanel,PartyFrame}.tsx`, `client/src/voice/ui/VoiceHud.tsx`, `client/src/ui/inventory/LootHud.tsx`, `CREDITS.md`, READMEs. Migration: `contracts/migrations/0023_quests.sql`. No protocol.yaml, constants, combat/items tables or worldgen output change.

## Sound design and music ✅ (branch `audio/sound-design`)

Nicole's tracks are the music of the world; sound effects are cut, layered and processed from the Novation Anniversary packs; a few textures are synthesized. Cue sheet, trigger map and not-yet-wired hooks: `client/src/audio/README.md`. Credits per file: `CREDITS.md` "Audio".

- **Music director** (`client/src/audio/music/`): a state machine (unit-tested) over the world clock and game state, crossfading between states. Day: *future land 1* / *future land 2*, alternating. Town by day: future land, warmer. Night (18:30-06:00): the night theme slot (see gaps). Caves (underground, latched with hysteresis): *void realm*, filtered; Void domains: *void realm*, open. Combat (recent hits, telegraphs within 18 m, a boss bar): a 16-bar synthwave loop from the Novation Analog Synthwave pack. Timed events (town defense warning and waves, a boss's last phase): *time crunch intro* -> *loop* -> *5 sec left* (5 s before the first wave, ward under 30 %, boss under 15 %), the three phase-locked so switches are seamless. Long tracks stream through media elements; short loops are decoded and looped sample-exact.
- **Level-ups**: *level up sound 1/2/3* by size (ordinary / every 5th or 2 at once / every 10th or 3+). Sound 3 also crowns boss kills, held town defenses and the Bitchin' loot sting; sound 2 the tower attunement and quest completion.
- **SFX**: 111 cues, 161 files: UI, footsteps (sliced from the acoustic drum loops), jump/land, swim, glide, climb, per-weapon swings, bow draw/release, cast + impact for each of the 8 elements, domains, hits/crits/parries, hurt/downed/respawn, enemy telegraphs and shots, demon and boss stingers (pitched, reversed vocal chops and the FX pack's "Robot Scream"), mining, loot by rarity with a Bitchin' special, coins, chests, forge/smelt/craft, NPCs, doors (synthesized creak), dusk/dawn, fire/freeze/pillar, ward falter/hit, a synthesized war horn for waves, defense held/broken, discovery, tower attunement, waypoints, skill-ups. Wired to the zone Events on main through one trigger map (`client/src/audio/triggers.ts`), to local prediction (combat runtime: attack, cast, bow draw, impact, explosion), to movement state, to CaveAudio (mining) and to UI stores (map, inventory, crafting, whispers).
- **Engine**: one AudioContext for the game, voice chat included (WP11's mixer now plays into the voice bus of the shared context and no longer closes it), created and resumed on the first gesture (Play). Buses master (limiter) / music / sfx / ambience / ui / voice; a settings panel (K or the "Sound" button) with a slider per bus and mute, persisted in localStorage. Equal-power 3D for world sounds with distance culling, a 40-voice cap, per-cue caps and cooldowns. Cave reverb sized by the chamber radius (moved from WP09's placeholder cave audio, which is now a bridge). Music ducks to 35 % while anyone in voice speaks. Synthesized ambience: wind (altitude, rain, glide speed), rain, fire crackle by burning cells, night insects, underwater, cave drips.
- **Budgets**: nothing audio before the first playable frame. First-frame JS +1.4 KB gzip (index 31.15 -> 32.26 KB gzip; PlayScreen 267.17 -> 267.49 KB gzip); the engine is a lazy chunk (66 KB, 20.2 KB gzip) loaded on idle after the first playable frame, then the instant cues are decoded (UI, steps, swings, hits; ~8 MB of PCM), then the rest downloads in the background and stays compressed until first played (all decoded would be 46 MB). Assets are Vite-hashed `/assets/` files (immutable cache; never base64-inlined). Committed audio: **17.6 MB** (7.8 MB Ogg Opus + 9.8 MB AAC; each browser fetches one codec: 1.0 MB of Opus SFX, music streamed per track, 6.8 MB for all 8 tracks).
- **Tests**: `npx vitest run src/audio` (director, trigger map incl. every cue built, movement, settings). Headless Chromium `node client/src/audio/dev/audio-browser.mjs` (solo production build served by `vite preview`, random port): **25/25 checks** (context created inside the Play click, no audio request until 7 s after the first playable frame, day -> night -> town at night -> cave -> combat -> timed intro -> 5 s left all audible, footsteps per stride and jumps from real input, SFX for triggers and zone events, K panel, persisted volumes, mute to -180 dBFS, no page errors), `docs/evidence/audio-headless.txt`, `audio-settings.png`.
- **Pipeline**: `node tools/audio/build-audio.mjs` (ffmpeg; the design is data in `tools/audio/cues.config.mjs`; sources are read-only and never committed).

Gaps:
- **"night overworld theme" is silent**: both exports (.aif and .mp3) are 2.4 s at about -90 dBFS. Night plays a nocturne cut of *future land 2* (its quiet middle, two semitones down, darker, roomier) until it is re-exported; swapping it in is one line in `tools/audio/cues.config.mjs` + `--only night`.
- The Novation packs ship no license text (only a description .docx); they are Novation's royalty-free sample packs, used here processed into new sounds. The Gentleman Library (encrypted Kontakt .nkx/.nkc, NI EULA) is not usable as raw audio and was skipped.
- Not wired yet (hooks defined and tested, one line each at merge): level-up toast, tower attunement, quests accept/complete, doors open/close, discovery intros (pt/progression, pt/ux); a client-side chest lid; snow footsteps (needs a cheap biome query); a beast aggro growl (no aggro event).
- No per-biome day soundscapes beyond the synthesized beds; remote footsteps are not voiced (crowds).
- Sounds were chosen by onset/spectral analysis and spectrograms, not by ear in the game: a listening pass by Nicole is the real acceptance (every level is one number in `cues.ts`).

Shared files touched (surgical): `client/src/main.tsx` (install the gesture unlock), `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx` (mount `AudioRoot`), `client/src/combat/runtime.ts` (`emitSound` lines: attack/cast, bow draw, impact, explosion), `client/src/voice/{mixer.ts,runtime.ts}` (shared context, voice bus, never close a shared context), `client/src/world/caves/caveAudio.ts` (now a bridge to the engine), `client/vite.config.ts` (never inline audio), `CREDITS.md` (audio block), this section.

## Playtest 1: towns on natural terrain and density ✅

Branch `pt/towns-density`. Nicole: "terrain in non-castle-towns shouldnt be flat. it should be more disheveled and houses built on foundations and paths to get everywhere. also the game feels a little too spread out and spacious, even in the towns". Contract notes: `docs/cr/014-towns-terrain-density.md`. Decisions: `docs/decisions/0022-towns-terrain-density.md`. Docs: `worldgen/README.md` (towns section header, "How the world is made" step 6, "Versioning"), `worldgen/archive/README.md`, `worldgen/golden/README.md`, `client/src/world/buildings/README.md`, `server/pkg/towns/README.md`.

**Worldgen genVersion 2** (generator output changes, spec 5 rule 4; ABI and buffer layouts unchanged):
- **Natural ground** in hamlets and villages (no more levelled footprints; walled towns keep theirs). Golden review before `golden:update` (`worldgen/golden/review.ts`, `docs/evidence/pt-towns-golden-review.txt`): on all 5 golden worlds every genVersion 1 site kept its index and id, spawns unchanged, **0 golden chunks away from hamlets/villages changed height**.
- **Foundations**: stone plinth, **stepped plinth** (building flag 64, relief >= 0.9 m) or **timber posts and cribbing** (flag 128, >= 2.2 m); stilt houses may stand in shallow water; a porch deck (now solid) where the door is > 1.3 m above the ground; door ramps above a 0.55 m rise are drawn as **steps**. Official world: 224 plinth, 154 stepped, 6 posts, 5 stilts.
- **Footpaths** (ROADS class 2, `worldgen/src/town/paths.rs`): Dijkstra on a 1 m grid from every door step foot, garden gate and stall to the streets/square (76 on the official world; most front-row doors open straight onto their street), plus paths from the nearest street end to every landmark, shrine, ruin and watchtower within 320 m of a settlement (75, ~16 km). Drawn on the terrain (no levelling: decision 0022). NPCs walk them to back-lane doors.
- **Tighter towns**: layout radius 0.8 / 0.7 / 0.85 of the atlas footprint, squares 7 / 9 / 12 m (was 9 / 13 / 18), streets 3.0 / 3.4 / 4.6 m (was 4 / 4 / 5), lots 0.5 m off the street, 1.2 m alleys (was 1.6), back lanes 7.5 m behind (was 10), trees up to the houses. Hamlets: mean building distance from the centre 18.8 -> 15.6 m, farthest 38 -> 28 m; villages 28.2 -> 25.6 m (`docs/evidence/pt-towns-towns-{before,after}.txt`).
- **Denser world** (atlas step 6, append-only): extra hamlets and minor POIs; **landmarks and enemy camps are built** (structure kinds 9 / 10, landmark pieces 15-22). Official world: sites 194 -> 290 (19.2 -> 28.7 per km2 of land), settlements 30 -> 42 (nearest-neighbour p50 407 -> 346 m), landmarks 14 -> 44, structures in the towns layer 58 -> 155; a walker on dry land to the nearest **built** thing: p50 95 -> 63 m, p90 179 -> 110 m, max 326 -> 227 m; within 150 m: 80.3 % -> 99.3 % (`docs/evidence/pt-towns-poi-{before,after}.txt`). 800 m coverage 99.99 %, every region has a tower and a settlement; no new camp within 750 m of the spawn (the Haven).

**Old worlds keep working**: the genVersion 1 build is archived (`worldgen/archive/worldgen-42a66cc191bee49d.wasm` + manifest, table `worldgen/golden/golden-v1.json`). `worldhost.LoadForGenVersion` (zone, bots) and `client/src/world/core/wasmFor.ts` (client, by Welcome `genVersion`) load the generator of the world's pin; `TestArchiveOpensGenVersion1World` opens the official world at its genVersion 1 pin `50ae4562cfd25265`. World renewal: `mintdb.RenewOfficial` / `go run ./server/pkg/cmd/mintworld -official -renew` (same row id, seed and spec; new gen_version/atlas_hash; refused unless every old site keeps its index and id, so progress carries over; characters saved near a hamlet or village restart at the spawn), and outside production the account service renews the dev official world by itself on startup (`LODESTONE_WORLD_RENEW=0` turns it off). `TestRenewOfficial` (scratch Postgres). Official world (seed 20261108) on genVersion 2: atlas `5b0cbaaec16d87b7`.

Evidence:
- **Golden** (`docs/evidence/pt-towns-golden.txt`): `npm run test:golden` PASS on Node, Chromium (Web Worker), Go/wazero (compiler + interpreter), native Rust, **and the archived genVersion 1 build against its own table in Node and Go/wazero**. Chunk in a Chromium worker mean 2.65 / p95 4.54 / max 6.0 ms (budget 8).
- **Sloped-lot agreement** (`client/src/world/buildings/dev/sloped-acceptance.ts`, `docs/evidence/pt-towns-sloped-walk.txt`): WP20's walk-into-a-building and pt/ux's door walk-through (close, blocked, open, in, close behind, blocked, out) on the six most demanding sloped buildings of the official world (timber posts, stepped plinths with porches, steps up to 3 m), Node client predicting on TS `town.ts`/`doors.ts` vs the zone binary: **12 walks, 0 corrections, max divergence 0.80 cm**, every closed door stops the body at the limit on both sides. The WP20 and pt/ux integration tests (`walkIntoBuilding`, `doorWalk`) pass on genVersion 2; door vectors regenerated.
- **Town bench** (`townBench.mjs --prefix pt-towns-bench`, RTX 3070, high tier, 1600x900, the largest town): max **305 draw calls** (street level; 292 in WP20), overview 277 (budget 400); triangles incl. shadow passes 1.17-1.87 M (WP20: 1.09-1.88 M); largest settlement/structure mesh 26.6 ms in the worker, max main-thread upload step 3.2 ms (`docs/evidence/pt-towns-bench-drawcalls.txt|json`, `pt-towns-bench-*.png`).
- **Generation timing** (`docs/evidence/pt-towns-generation-timing.txt`, wasm on Node, 10 worlds): atlas <= 0.59 s, towns_build <= 0.78 s per world (60 s mint budget). The towns pass memoizes the lattice per settlement (`Terrain::around`, bit-identical to fresh sampling), which took a wetland walled town from 4.2 s to 0.29 s native.
- **G4** (`tools/acceptance/g4.mjs --stop-after 3 --no-bots`): see `docs/evidence/pt-towns-g4.txt`.
- **Screenshots** (same cameras before/after, `townsTerrainShots.mjs --at 736,3552 --look 180`): `docs/evidence/pt-towns-{before,after}-{town-above,paths-above,street,foundation,door-steps,landscape}.png`.
- **Tests**: `worldgen/tests/towns.rs` (6 worlds: every door has a way to the street, paths end on the network and never cross a solid box, foundations reach below the ground, building counts, required buildings), worldhost archive/renewal tests, client kit step test; `npm test -w client`, `npm test -w @lodestone/worldgen`, `go test` in every module.

Known gaps:
- **Deployment (not done here; the cloud deployment was not touched):** the deployed official world is pinned to genVersion 1. Shipping this build alone is safe: zone, client and bots load the archived genVersion 1 build for it (the same world as today). To give it the new towns: (1) deploy this build (`npm run deploy`; the zone image now carries `worldgen/archive/` with `LODESTONE_WORLDGEN_ARCHIVE`, the wasm bucket gets the archived file too); (2) make sure a fresh Postgres dump exists (the VM's shutdown dump, or `pg_dump` in the postgres container); (3) renew: `gcloud compute ssh lodestone-game --tunnel-through-iap -- sudo docker compose -f /opt/lodestone/docker-compose.yml exec -T zone mintworld -official -renew` (prints before/after gen_version and atlas hash and the moved characters); (4) restart the zone (`... docker compose -f /opt/lodestone/docker-compose.yml restart zone`) so it opens the renewed row on genVersion 2; clients follow Welcome `genVersion`. Rollback: restore the dump (the archived build still serves genVersion 1).
- **Dev:** the shared dev database was not changed (Nicole's running dev stack and other branches run genVersion 1 builds). After this merges, restarting the dev stack renews the dev official world automatically (account log "official world renewed (dev)"), or run `go run ./server/pkg/cmd/mintworld -official -renew` once. Branches that have not merged this cannot serve the renewed world (pin refusal) until they merge main.
- Paths are draped, never levelled; a few in-town footpaths wade shallow water in wetland hamlets. Streets still run straight from the square to the edge (into a lake in one coastal hamlet, as before).
- Village compactness moved less than hamlets' (lots keep room for porches and steps on slopes); the tighter feel comes mostly from narrower streets and squares, smaller gaps and trees up to the houses.
- Local profiles (IndexedDB save blobs) keep their saved positions through a renewal; a local character standing in a village is lifted or pushed out on its next step.
- More sites mean more guardian/camp groups between towns and more discovery XP; left to the existing difficulty tiers, not re-balanced here.
- Timings from one shared desktop (i7-11700K, RTX 3070); no `-race` (no cgo).

Shared files touched: `worldgen/src/{lib.rs,chunk.rs,atlas/mod.rs,atlas/sites.rs,town/*}`, `worldgen/{README.md,golden/*,scripts/test-golden.mjs,harness/go/golden_test.go,js/test/wasm.ts,js/src/town.ts,examples/town_stats.rs,tests/towns.rs}`; `server/pkg/{towns/towns.go,towns/testdata/doors.json,worldhost/*,worldhost/mintdb/*,cmd/mintworld/main.go,cmd/heightcmp/main_test.go}`, `server/account/main.go`, `server/zone/{terrain.go,internal/npc/npc.go}`, `tools/bots/terrain.go`, `tools/creator/server` tests (golden hashes); `client/src/{net/NetPlay.tsx (1 prop),world/WorldCanvas.tsx,world/core/runtime.ts,world/buildings/{kit.ts,kit.test.ts,roads.ts,structures.ts,buildings.worker.ts,protocol.ts,townsManager.ts (1 line),townsDebug.ts,dev/*}}`, `client/src/sim/golden/*.json` (genVersion field only); `infra/docker/zone.Dockerfile` and the five identical `.dockerignore`s, `infra/lib/gcp.mjs` (archive upload). New: `worldgen/{archive/,src/town/paths.rs,examples/poi_density.rs,golden/review.ts,golden/golden-v1.json}`, `server/pkg/worldhost/{archive.go,archive_test.go,mintdb/renew.go,mintdb/renew_test.go}`, `client/src/world/core/wasmFor.ts`, `docs/cr/014-*`, `docs/decisions/0022-*`, `docs/evidence/pt-towns-*`. No protocol, constants, events or migration changes.

## G5: dedicated Linux soak ✅ (S1, S2, S3, S4-S1, S4-S2 + failover and S4-voice (fixed harness, local) PASS; S1 on a real 4 vCPU slice PASSES 5/5 on Linux after `g5-tick-vcpu4`, with 0.4-1.0 ms of p99 headroom; "deployed" not part of this)

Branch `g5-linux-soak` (from main `1d1f626`). Spec 14's soaks on two temporary GCP VMs in `sw-viz` (us-central1-a, 2026-11-09 15:48-16:56 UTC): `lodestone-soak-zone` c2d-standard-16 running only zone processes pinned with `taskset`, and `lodestone-soak-bots` c2d-standard-16 for bots, netsim, director and the Postgres/Redis/LiveKit containers. The two 16 vCPU VMs used the project's whole 32 vCPU quota and kept the live `lodestone-game` VM from waking (QUOTA_EXCEEDED); on the orchestrator's notice the bots VM was stopped and resized to c2d-standard-8 at 16:35 (24 vCPU total), which aborted one S4/S2 attempt (re-run). Everything was deleted afterwards (instances, boot disks, the `lodestone-soak-iap-ssh` rule; listings empty); cost ~$1.50. Details: `docs/decisions/v1-capacity.md` "Dedicated Linux measurement"; evidence `docs/evidence/g5-linux-*` (overview `g5-linux-summary.md`); how to reproduce: `tools/soak/linux/README.md`.

Results (zone `GOMAXPROCS=4`; vcpu4 = 2 cores + SMT siblings, what a 4 vCPU VM is; cores4 = 4 physical cores):
- **Scenario 1**: cores4 **PASS 3/3** (worst 60 s p99 10.20-10.36 ms, 0 windows over 12 ms, p50 6.5-7.5 ms, downlink max 150.5 kbps, 2,006 awake); vcpu4 **FAIL 3/3** (12.96-13.24 ms, 18-21 of 40 windows over 12). In-process benchmark on the same VM: 4 cores p50 5.4 / p99 8.3 ms (= the desktop), 4 vCPU 7.3 / 11.5 ms.
- **Scenario 2** (1,000 bots, 4 zones each on its own 4 vCPU slice): **PASS**, 1000/1000, 5,390 handoffs, 0 owned twice, 0 unowned, 0 duplicated, zones 262/241/258/240 after 4 load moves, zone p99 5.7-7.1 ms.
- **Scenario 3** (50 voice bots): **PASS**, 868/868, 0 flaps, p99 234 ms.
- **Scenario 4**: S1 under netsim thailand cores4 **PASS** (11.42 ms), vcpu4 FAIL (14.25 ms); S1 under real `tc netem` thailand between the VMs, cores4 **PASS** (11.67 ms); S2 + SIGKILL of a pool zone (253 players) **PASS**: 227/227 failovers, 0 owned twice, 0 unowned two samples running, 0 duplicated, survivors p99 7.0-8.7 ms; voice under thailand **FAIL 2/2** (8 and 26 decisions never reflected, 1 and 3 zone flaps; reflected latency p99 240 ms; not capacity: zone tick 0.8 ms).

Changes (drivers: flags, no logic): `tools/bots/remote.mjs` (new: start/kill a zone on another host over ssh), `tools/bots/scale-soak.mjs` (`--bin-dir`, `--zone-host`, `--remote-dir`, `--zone-cpus`, `--bots-cpus`, Linux machine line), `tools/bots/handoff-soak.mjs` (`--bin-dir`, `--zone-host`, `--remote-dir`, `--infra-host`, `--zone-cpus`; the hard kill is SIGKILL on Linux, where `killTree` sends a graceful SIGTERM), `server/social/voice/voicebots/main.go` (`-bin-dir`), `tools/soak/linux/` (new: build/stage, VM startup, run and queue scripts, netem, README).

Known gaps / decisions for G5:
- Scenario 1 at "4 vCPU": fails on a real 4 vCPU slice, passes on 4 cores. Either size zones at 4 cores (8 vCPU) for worst-case density or accept scenario 1 as beyond the 4 vCPU budget (decision for Nicole/orchestrator). **Follow-up below (branch `g5-tick-vcpu4`): passes 2/3 on a 4 vCPU slice of the desktop; confirmed on Linux below (PASS 5/5): 4 vCPU per zone holds.**
- Voice under thailand: earshot decisions flip under the netsim-impaired input stream (25-35 % more decisions than clean, some reversed within 2 s); reproducible without VMs; not investigated further. **Resolved below: night demons in the soak, not netsim.**
- S4/S2 used the netsim proxy (TCP-style stalls), like the desktop runs; only S1 was also run under kernel netem. Voice media was never impaired (LiveKit next to the voice bots).
- Bot-side prediction resyncs vary 12k-160k between identical S1 runs while the zone dropped/synthesized 0 input frames; not part of any verdict, not investigated.
- `npm run lint`: eslint, go vet, gofmt and clippy clean; `typecheck` fails in `tools/creator` (`ajv/dist/2020` not resolvable from this worktree, which has no node_modules of its own; code untouched here). Go tests: `tools/bots`, `voicebots` pass.
- "Deployed" (the other half of G5) is not part of this work.

### Voice under thailand: root cause and fix (branch `g5-voice-netsim`)

**Not netsim: night demons.** The voice soak's spawned zone runs the WP15 world clock (night every real hour from :46:15 to :15:00). At night demons killed the standing bots, the zone respawned them at the world spawn, and each bot kept simulating where it thought it was, so the zone's earshot followed positions up to 98 m off the scenario (extra decisions, zone flaps, reshuffles while standing). The Linux clean run ended 16:45:49 UTC (day), thailand run 1 straddled dusk (16:46:15), run 2 was all night. All 34 "missed" of the two Linux runs were decisions in the last second before "end", which the analysis could never match (an unsubscribe shows when the track ends ~1 s later). Reproduced locally (no cloud): same code and thailand profile by day 862/862 PASS, by night 1,280 decisions / 35 missed / 4 zone flaps FAIL; clean by night fails too (7 and 17 missed). Under thailand by day the zone's positions lag the bots by at most 2.2 m and never go backwards (8 m hysteresis band). The other hypotheses (bursty positions, checker vs ground truth, reconcile ordering, LiveKit races) were tested and refuted; details `docs/evidence/g5-voice-netsim-summary.md`.

Fix (harness only, `server/social/voice/voicebots`; no zone/earshot/social change): `-spawn` runs the zone with `LODESTONE_ZONE_NO_DEMONS=1` (`-demons` to keep them); bots follow the zone's teleport Event and any teleport makes the run INVALID; bots stand 2.5 s after "end" and listener events in that tail match decisions made before it; zone input-frame counters in the report. Regression tests: `voicebots/analyze_test.go` (tail, teleport), `zone/internal/voice/earshot_bursty_test.go` (soak through the netsim thailand model of the input stream: same decision count as smooth, 0 flaps; teleport changes sets once), `social/voice/reconcile_order_test.go` (bursts, reversal in flight, random batching = per message).

After, all at forced night, 3 runs each (desktop): **scenario 4 voice (thailand) 874/874, 866/866, 874/874; scenario 3 clean 868/868, 876/876, 868/868; 0 missed, 0 flaps, 0 teleports, p99 275-319 ms, 100 % within 500 ms: PASS 6/6.** Evidence `docs/evidence/g5-voice-netsim-*`. Not re-run on the Linux VMs (the bug and fix are machine-independent). Note: `tools/bots` scale/handoff drivers do not pin the clock or disable demons either; their verdicts did not depend on it, but bot deaths at night can add teleports to those runs too. (Since `g5-tick-vcpu4` they start their zones with `LODESTONE_ZONE_NO_DEMONS=1`.)

### Scenario 1 on 4 vCPU (2 cores + SMT): cheaper tick + GC pacing (branch `g5-tick-vcpu4`)

No cloud: the desktop pinned to two cores' hyperthread pairs (`start /affinity F000`, logical CPUs 12-15 = cores 6-7; topology checked) reproduces the Linux 4 vCPU case (in-process p50/p99 6.4-6.6 / 10.2-11.6 ms here vs 7.27 / 11.47 on the EPYC). Changes, all wire-identical (`TestWireDigest`: same SHA-256 of every byte sent in two deterministic scale runs on main and after) and documented in `docs/decisions/v1-capacity.md` "4 vCPU follow-up":
- Snapshot baselines are 24-byte references into a per-tick `snapshot.History` shared by every client's encoder (was a 40-byte copy per entity per client per tick); "changed since the baseline" from fingerprints and unchanged-since ticks; lock-free name lookups; per-entity due masks, grid queries straight into the bitset and without the 200 m square's corners, the newest frame searched only at the far ring's edge (`server/pkg/snapshot`, `internal/aoi`, `internal/game/snapshots.go`).
- Lock-free terrain cache reads with a sharded hit counter (`server/pkg/worldhost`); cave systems on the workers; no allocation per chem-ground query or per Windows QPC read; `spawnTick` without its once-a-second 150,000-`Hypot` / map spike (`internal/combat`).
- Zone GC pacing: GOGC 300 + 3 GiB soft limit by default (`server/zone/gc.go`, `LODESTONE_ZONE_GOGC` / `_MEMLIMIT_MB`; plain `GOGC`/`GOMEMLIMIT` win). Same build A/B: GOGC 100 22 of 28 windows over 12 ms, GOGC 400 0.
- Benchmark: CPU per tick and phase p99s in `BenchmarkScale`; `tools/soak/linux/build.sh` stages `bin/game.test`, `bench-inprocess.sh` runs it pinned on the VM. Soak drivers disable demons.

Results (evidence `docs/evidence/g5-tick-vcpu4-*`, overview `g5-tick-vcpu4-summary.md`): **in-process 2C/4T** p50 6.55 -> 6.14 ms, p99 10.48 -> 9.38 ms (5 alternating runs, medians), CPU per tick -8 %; **4 cores** p50 5.18 -> 4.51, p99 8.61 -> 7.35, CPU -15 %. **Live scenario 1 on 2C/4T**, interleaved: base FAIL 3/3 (worst p99 14.71 / 12.24 / 13.94 ms), after **PASS 2/3** (11.02 / 11.35 ms, 0 windows over 12; the third, 15.72, hit bursts from outside the zone from t = 85 s). Downlink max 150.6 kbps. No behavior change (no AI LOD: every enemy in scenario 1 is within wake range of a player).

Known gaps: the Linux confirmation run (expected in-process p99 ~10.3 ms on the EPYC, live worst ~11-11.5 ms: a pass with ~0.5-1 ms of headroom, less than the 4-core runs' 1.6 ms; **done below: in-process 9.3 ms, live 11.04-11.56 ms, PASS 5/5**); zone memory now peaks at ~4x live heap (>= 3 GB per zone process); allocation (~40 MB/s, mostly the WebSocket library's per-message contexts and buffers) is the next lever for the p99.

### Scenario 1 on 4 vCPU: Linux confirmation (branch `g5-linux-vcpu4-confirm`) ✅

Main @ `7ac9da4` (the `g5-tick-vcpu4` merge), no code change; the dedicated-Linux recipe of `tools/soak/linux/README.md` again (GCP sw-viz, us-central1-a, 2026-11-09 18:52-19:34 UTC): `lodestone-soak-zone` c2d-standard-16 (same type as the first G5 run) running only the zone, `lodestone-soak-bots` c2d-standard-8; 24 vCPU of the 32 quota (0 in use before; `lodestone-game` could wake and did). Zone `GOMAXPROCS=4`, demons off, GOGC 300 + 3 GiB (zone defaults).

- **Scenario 1, vcpu4 (`0,1,8,9`, 2 cores + SMT): PASS 5/5**: worst 60 s p99 11.30 / 11.40 / 11.06 / 11.56 / 11.04 ms (after the join ramp 10.56-10.83), **0 of 40 windows over 12 ms in every run** (first G5 run: 12.96-13.24 ms, 18-21 of 40 over), p50 7.7-9.3 ms, max tick 13.7-22.9 ms, downlink max 150.4 kbps, 2,006 awake, 300/300, 0 kicks/errors, 0 input frames dropped. GC cycles in the 200 s steady state 4 (was 10).
- **Scenario 4 / S1 under netsim thailand, vcpu4: PASS** (11.89 ms, 0 of 40 over, downlink max 151.9; first G5 run 14.25 ms, 31 over), with only 0.1 ms of headroom.
- **Control, cores4 (`0-3`): PASS 2/2**, 8.94 / 8.99 ms (first G5 run 10.20-10.36).
- **In-process on the VM** (3 alternating runs): 4 vCPU p50 6.79-6.82 / p99 **9.25-9.38 ms** (was 7.27 / 11.47), 4 cores 4.99-5.00 / 6.92-7.04 (was 5.43 / 8.27); CPU per tick 26 ms on 2C/4T vs 18.5 ms on 4 cores.
- Live phase means vcpu4 (was): snapshots 5.02-5.14 (5.33-5.46), combat 1.33-1.39 (1.68-1.72), inputs 0.64-0.66 (0.83-0.85), world 0.39-0.47 (0.70-0.72). The slowest tick per minute is still a 7.6-9.8 ms snapshot phase.

So spec 9's "4 vCPU per zone" holds for scenario 1's worst-case density on real cloud vCPUs, and G5's soak scenarios all have a PASS on dedicated hardware (voice under thailand on the desktop after the harness fix). Headroom on 2C/4T is thin (0.4-1.0 ms clean, 0.1 ms under thailand); anything that grows the tick (more per-entity work, a busier world phase) should be re-measured on this shape. Everything was deleted afterwards (instances, boot disks, the `lodestone-soak-iap-ssh` rule; listings empty); cost ~$0.80. Evidence `docs/evidence/g5-linux-vcpu4-confirm-*` (overview `g5-linux-vcpu4-confirm-summary.md`). Changes: `tools/soak/linux/run-s1.sh` takes `PREFIX` for the evidence names (README note); docs.

## Playtest 1: server polish ✅ (branch `pt/server-polish`)

Three server gaps from playtesting. Contract: `docs/cr/015-door-state.md` (migration **0024** `door_state`: run `npm run migrate` after merge). Docs: `server/zone/README.md` (Combat, Doors, Items), `server/pkg/towns/README.md` (`Blocker`), `internal/combat/README.md`.
- **Chest loot at the area's level**: containers roll at the discovery-stamped area level (the level the area's enemies have), so the Haven's chests give starter gear and harder stamped areas better gear; worldgen's region-danger curve is only the fallback while an area is unknown or with `LODESTONE_ZONE_NO_DIFFICULTY`. Seeds unchanged and stamps immutable, so a crash re-roll gives the same items (`internal/game/difficulty.go` `lootLevelAt`; `loot_level_test.go`). Kill loot already used the enemy's (area) level.
- **Doors persist and follow the region's zone**: the zone owning a door's region decides it; other zones forward requests (`door`) and mirror decisions (`doorset`, their own stamps), refusals come back (`doorno` -> 972), the owner's doorway check includes ghost-band players. Only the owner writes `door_state` (one batched multi-row upsert per second through the write-behind queue, newest `changed_at` wins, flushed on shutdown); every zone restores before ticking and newcomers get restored doors in the baseline (`doors_sync.go`, `doors_store.go`; tests `doors_persist_test.go` restart/batching/shutdown flush/two zones, `doors_store_test.go` Postgres).
- **Enemies respect buildings**: enemy steps and demon shadow steps are refused into any solid town box (the players' collision, incl. town walls and fences) or a closed door (`towns.Blocker`: 8 m bitmask broadphase, ~3 % of the official world marked; ~4 ns per query away from towns, ~100 ns hugging a building; an enemy already overlapping a box may move anywhere). `TestBlockerAgreesWithPlayerCollision` (Blocked == Collide pushes on ~1.1 M samples), combat `obstacles_test.go`, in-process `TestEnemiesDoNotWalkThroughBuildings` (official world; control without the hook walks through). Scale benchmark (`Scale/p300_e2000`, 600 ticks, -cpu 4, 3 alternating runs): p50 4.52/4.53/4.64 -> 4.51/4.51/4.55 ms, p99 7.02/6.94/7.52 -> 6.51/7.15/7.31 ms, combat 0.67-0.73 -> 0.67-0.69 ms: no change (the scale world has no towns, so the hook is a nil check). `TestWireDigest` unchanged on both runs for the same reason.

Known gaps: enemies still steer only ±69° around obstacles (no pathfinding): a chaser stops at a wall and returns home after 6 s; demons shadow-step over walls. NPCs of a border town on the non-owning zone ask the owner to open doors (every 2 s) and wait.

## Audio QoL pass ✅ (branch `audio/qol`)

Nicole's playtest ask: "rain and fire should be quieter if you are farther and rain should be quieter under a roof and sound muffled ... do a sound quality of life pass". All client-side, no contract change. Details and numbers: `client/src/audio/README.md` "Space and distance".
- **Distance everywhere**: every placed sound uses one curve (`spatial.ts`): full inside the cue's reference distance, inverse distance beyond, easing to exactly silent at its range (the Web Audio model used to stop attenuating at max, so sounds cut off at -23 dB), plus air absorption (far sounds lose their top end); panners only pan. Long sounds are re-placed as you move.
- **Positional loops**: every torch, brazier, hearth, campfire, forge and camp campfire in the towns buffer (383 fires and 42 forges on the official world, indexed in 7 ms), burning grass (chemistry cells clustered into fires that grow with the blaze), rivers and lake/sea shores (a water-surface ring probe) are emitters with per-category ranges (torch 12 m ... wildfire 60 m); only the 8 loudest get a voice (hysteresis, 0.3 s fade-in, fade-out then stop), textures rendered once through an OfflineAudioContext (no assets). Smiths hammer at forges within 36 m by day. Hearths heard from the street are muffled by the wall (occlusion by space: closed building / cave / outdoors).
- **Rain and weather**: rain intensity builds and fades (~15 s / ~10 s) instead of switching; thunder in heavy rain (delayed by distance at the speed of sound, darker and quieter further away). Wind, rain, thunder and insects run through one weather submix steered by a 5-point roof probe over the town collision boxes and a "inside closed walls" test (`shelter.ts`): under a porch or bridge a little quieter and duller; indoors x0.22 and low-passed to ~650 Hz, with a "rain on the roof" drumming layer rising in its place; walking through a doorway crossfades (~0.3 s). In caves surface weather fades with the distance to the nearest opening and the depth (CaveManager now reports the mouth distance) and darkens to ~300 Hz.
- **Interiors and caves**: small-room reverb indoors (sized by the room), the chamber reverb underground, and a gentle low-pass on the sfx and ambience buses (11 kHz indoors, 7.5 kHz in caves); UI, music and voice stay dry.
- **One-shot hygiene** (`voiceLimit.ts`): same cue at the same place within 70 ms is one sound; at most 3 starts of a cue per 45 ms (area hits); never the same variant twice running; +/-10 % gain on top of the pitch spread; 4 ms ramps on mid-file starts. Footsteps by surface: grass, stone (streets, caves, stone town boxes), wood (floors, decks, bridges: new `step_wood`), wading (`step_water`), snow (snowpeak biome; `step_snow` finally wired).
- **Mix**: ambience also ducks under big stings (72 %) besides music (50 %); voice chat still ducks music 35 % / ambience 60 % / sfx 80 %; a hidden tab fades music, ambience and effects out (voice stays) and back in. All of it is pure logic in tested modules (`spatial`, `acoustics`, `shelter`, `surface`, `voiceLimit`, `loopPool`, `emitters`), run by the engine at 10 Hz (listener/occlusion/loop selection), never per frame per source.
- **Budget**: the audio engine chunk grows to 88 KB / 28 KB gzip (lazy, loaded after the first playable frame; first-frame JS unchanged); the four loop textures are ~3.4 MB of PCM (6-9 s, 32 kHz mono); the 10 Hz pass is a few grid lookups, ~100 wasm water samples twice a second and at most 8 loop voices.
- **Tests**: `npx vitest run src/audio`: 9 files, 82 tests (incl. the shelter probe on the official world's first 400 closed buildings: roof over every middle, door step outside). Full client suite (main merged) 419 tests pass.
