Headless (`node client/src/audio/dev/audio-browser.mjs`, solo production build): **32/32** (the 25 earlier checks plus: environment pass, rain outdoors vs inside an inn (weather gain 1 -> 0.23, low-pass 16 kHz -> 664 Hz, roof rain 0 -> 0.36, room reverb 0.32, bus low-pass 11 kHz), wooden floor footsteps, town fires as capped loop voices, 9 of 11 burst hits suppressed, hidden tab -48 -> -180 dBFS, no page errors), `docs/evidence/audio-headless.txt`.

Gaps: occlusion is by space, not ray-cast, and ignores open doors; smiths hammer whether or not the blacksmith NPC is at the forge; no waterfalls (worldgen has none to find); remote players' footsteps still unvoiced; the loop textures and thunder are synthesized, so a listening pass by Nicole is the real acceptance (levels are single numbers in `spatial.ts` LOOP_PROFILES and `acoustics.ts`).

Shared files touched (surgical): `client/src/world/caves/caveAudio.ts` and one line of `caveManager.ts` (pass the cave-mouth distance), this section.

## Playtest 2: skill tree + build overview ✅ (branch `pt2/skill-tree`)

Nicole's playtest asks: "Skill tree you can open" and "Somewhere to see where all your spec'd items have accumulated". One screen (`client/src/ui/skills`, README there), all client-side: **no contract change, no CR** (the numbers come from Event 900, combat_sync, Event 400 and the generated `COMBAT`/`ITEMS` tables; gear is computed as the zone does).
- **Keys**: **N** opens the skill tree on the last spell tree (N again closes), **B** the build overview, **Esc** closes (C/I/M/J close it as their screen opens). Buttons "Skill tree" and "Build overview" in the character panel (C); the combat hint line ends "N skills · B build"; the level-up fanfare says "C character · N skill tree". Opening frees the pointer; `data-ui-panel` hides the look hint.
- **Spell trees**: a tab per tree (Fire, Ice, Lightning, Earth, Wind, Light, Dark, Void; element colours; known/total and a gold "+" when something is learnable). Each tree is a graph by tier (columns headed with the level a skill point needs), lanes from barycentre sweeps so chains run straight, prerequisite edges (OR'd: solid when both ends are learned, dashed from a learned prerequisite). Node states: learned, learnable now (gold, pulsing), no free point (dashed), needs level N, needs a prerequisite; passives are pills. Hover: type, damage (x hits), cooldown, mana, wind-up, range/radius, status, lifesteal, shields, dash, "kills demons" (Light), passive effects, "your hit" with the active loadout's build, which slots hold it, the name-pending flag (none pending today). Click: a card with **Learn** (the existing Interact 50/4; double-click also learns) or **Put in slot 1-4**; drag a learned castable spell onto a slot of either loadout, or pick it and click a slot (Interact 50/3); x clears a slot.
- **Weapon skills** tab: the six use-based skills (level, XP bar, what the level gives), techniques by level with what each does (the ones the zone does not apply yet say "not in effect yet": Sweeping Strike, Pole Vault, Impale, Quick-Draw, Double Shot, Charged Pierce, Twin Cast), the eight elemental forms with yours highlighted.
- **Build** tab: character totals each with its sources (health = base + level + crystal boons + every armor piece and affix; mana; stamina (Moonstone shown as not applied); level damage; armor with the 60 % cap; loot find and its luck; forge luck from Smithing); both loadouts side by side (main-hand item with material, item level, rarity, affixes, uniques, socketed gems and their element; the four spells; weapon-hit and spell multipliers broken into level, skill, material x item level, gear %, staff/wand spell power, Overflow; crit; mana regen; cooldowns (wand, Quick Cast); the elemental form and its damage or what it needs; what kills demons); the armor; skill points (base + level + crystals, spent, unspent, per tree); learned passives (tree multipliers; Thick Bark, Undergrowth, Crimson Hunger/Veil, Phoenix, Undying marked "not in effect yet", as the zone does not apply them); elements and statuses the build brings.
- **Tests**: `skills.test.ts` 29 tests (all eight layouts: every node once, columns by tier, no overlaps, edges never backwards, deterministic; node states agree with the character panel's `learnable` at six levels; tooltip facts; passive lines; every technique described; gear and every build number with its parts). The gear numbers are pinned on both sides: new Go test `server/zone/internal/items/gear_vector_test.go` computes the inventory mock's equipment with `Inventory.Gear` (health 77, armor 17.2 %, weapon x1.224, topaz form x1.25, sunsteel kills demons). Full client suite after merging main: 49 files, 448 tests pass.
- **Evidence**: solo headless (`node client/src/ui/skills/dev/shots.mjs`, 26 checks PASS, no page errors: learn spends a point, Immolate opens, drag to a slot, pick + click a slot, tooltip numbers, fits 1920x1080 and 1280x720 with no overlapping nodes, Esc closes): `docs/evidence/pt2-skill-tree-{fire,earth,weapon-skills,build}-1920x1080.png`, `pt2-skill-tree-{fire,dark,build}-1280x720.png`. Online against the local dev stack (`dev/online.mjs`: a fresh character learns Glint through the screen and drags it to slot 1; the zone's combat_sync confirms both): `pt2-skill-tree-online-{light,build}-1920x1080.png`, `pt2-skill-tree-online.txt`.

Gaps: the "your hit" estimate leaves out the enemy's element, statuses, crits and situational passives (low health, vs status); character totals use the active loadout's gear (as the zone does), so a health affix on the other loadout's weapon counts only after X. Shared files touched (one line or a few each): `client/src/combat/CombatHud.tsx` (hint), `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx` (mount), `client/src/ui/progression/ProgressionHud.tsx` (two buttons, fanfare line).

## Playtest 2: bugs ✅ (branch `pt2/bugs`)

Nicole's live-deploy reports, root causes and fixes. CR 017 (travel to a friend). No migration; no protocol/constants change.

- **"VC doesnt seem to work"**: LiveKit on the live VM is healthy (firewall tcp 7881 / udp 7882 open, `use_external_ip` advertises 34.171.234.208, ICE selected UDP for real players, `/livekit` through Caddy answers, social mints tokens). The cause was the design: WP11 made local profiles listen-only, and with no Firebase sign-in on live every player is a local profile, so every join token had `CanPublish: false` (`/debug/voice`: 3 participants, 0 subscriptions). Fix: local profiles may talk in proximity voice (earshot, `/mute`, `/block` and a moderator mute by profile id still apply; a moderator mute gives a listen-only token). Kill switch `LODESTONE_VOICE_LOCAL_LISTEN_ONLY=1` (social + zone) restores WP11's rule. The client reads listen-only from the token's grant, not from "is local profile"; the zone shows local profiles' speaking bit. Infra (VM compose): social reaches LiveKit's API at `http://livekit:7880` inside compose (it went out through the public IP and Caddy, which fails while Caddy boots); `startup.sh` raises the UDP socket buffers LiveKit asks for. This deviates from spec 10/11 ("local profiles hear but don't transmit") on purpose, until Google sign-in is live.
- **"Questing is bugged"**: three bugs. (1) For local profiles (everyone on live) the tracker, journal and markers stayed on a finished step: the step's reward (the smith's gold) issued the save blob, which marked the quest log saved before `questsAdvance` checked it for changes, so the new log (Event 950) was never sent (the toast came from Event 952). Now the change is read before the rewards are applied. (2) The Ward was impossible to finish: the official world has six sunsteel veins, one per cave, each ~140-150 m down at the bottom of a sunlit shaft, giving 2-3 ore (the step needs 4) and then mined for everyone for 6-24 h. A sunsteel vein now gives at least a blade's worth (4 ore) and regrows in 20-40 min. (3) The ore step said nothing about depth: it now says how far down the seam is, to walk in from the mouth or jump down the shaft (glider), and marks the shaft. Also the blacksmith's shop showed `blueprint:sunsteel_blade` as "sunsteel blade 40 g" (looked like the blade for sale) and the blueprint and the finished iron sword both as "iron sword": shops name items from items.yaml ("Blueprint: Sunsteel Blade", "Iron Sword"), say "N in stock" and that a blueprint teaches the recipe. Bounties go through the same log code (fixed by (1)); their existing tests pass.
- **"It says mine ore but you have no pickaxe"**: the pickaxe was implicit (anyone mines with E). The starter kit now carries a Pickaxe (items.yaml `starter`); older characters get one once on join (grant marker `starter:pickaxe`, loaded like `starter`; none if they already carry one; a granted one that is sold or dropped isn't replaced). The step reads "Mine 4 sunsteel ore with your pickaxe" and the smith says "You've a pickaxe in your pack".
- **"You cannot switch back to the default sword"**: the starter sword is the loadout's training weapon, not an item. Unequipping a weapon item kept the item's weapon type in the loadout (a spear stayed a training spear), and no UI could pick a training weapon. Now an empty main hand falls back to the loadout's default (combat.yaml start: Loadout 1 sword, Loadout 2 staff); the inventory shows "Training Sword (default)" in an empty main hand, "Unequip · use Training Sword" on an equipped weapon, and a "Use Training Sword" button when an older save left another training weapon there.
- **"When I added my friend, it teleported us to our respective spawns"**: adding a friend never reaches the zone (social only). What moved them was a reconnect at the same moment (a zone restart / redeploy or a dropped connection: the client fails over silently). Local profiles carry their position in the save blob they present on connect, and the zone only re-issued the blob when other progress changed, and never on its way down, so a player who had only walked came back at the position of their first blob, their spawn. Now a local profile who moved more than 2 m gets a new blob at the 30 s checkpoint, and a stopping zone sends every local profile a final blob (synchronously) before the shutdown kick.
- **Travel to a friend** (feature, CR 017): a Travel button per online friend in the Friends panel (O). Interact 61 (FNV-1a 64 of the friend's character id) → a 3 s channel (Event 980; the screen darkens over its last second and lifts after the move) that moving 1 m or taking damage cancels (981) → the zone checks the accepted friendship in `social_friends` off the tick, refuses if either is in combat (hurt/fighting in the last 10 s, or downed), 30 s cooldown → `Teleport` onto the friend (982). A friend on another zone (cluster) is found in the presence feed and the WP16 handoff takes over after the move.

Tests: Go `quests_playable_test.go` (the Ward on the official world with real actions: kit pickaxe, the step text, the marked seam's vein mined with swings, >= 4 ore, auto-pickup advances, respawn 20-40 min, blueprint bought, smelted twice and forged at the town forge), `localpos_test.go` (local profile: walk-only blob at the checkpoint and the spot survives a reconnect; final blob on Stop and the next zone spawns there; the quest log follows a talk step, fails without the fix), `playtest2_test.go` (unequip falls back to sword/staff, gem cleared; the starter pickaxe once, not twice, not over a carried one, not replaced), `friendtravel_test.go` (offline, self, not friends, moved, hurt, friend downed, the move, cooldown, a friend on another zone; FNV vectors shared with vitest), `earshot_test.go` (local profile speaking shown by default, hidden with the switch), social `voice_integration_test.go` (a local profile's token publishes the microphone; the switch restores listen-only). Vitest: `social/travel.test.ts`, `voice/voice.test.ts` (token grant), `ui/inventory/inventory.test.ts` (training weapon), `world/buildings/townEvents.test.ts` (item names).

Deploy (orchestrator): redeploy everything (`LODESTONE_GCP_PROJECT=sw-viz LODESTONE_NETLIFY_CLIENT_SITE=lodestone-rpg-game npm run deploy -- --creator-site lodestone-rpg-creator`): zone and social images, the VM compose file and `startup.sh` (voice: social's LiveKit API URL, UDP buffers), the client. No migration. Players' current saves: the first blob after the redeploy carries the position; an older character gets its pickaxe on the next join.

Gaps: the zone reads `social_friends` directly (the social service owns it); friend travel can't see a friend's combat state on another zone; voice for local profiles has no extra abuse safety beyond earshot, mute/block and moderator mutes (a mid-session moderator mute still takes effect at the next voice reconnect: social doesn't revoke the LiveKit grant live). `npm run typecheck` fails in `tools/creator` in this worktree only (it resolves the root's ajv 6 instead of the creator's ajv 8: no nested node_modules in the worktree), unrelated to this branch.

## Playtest 2: third person + animation ✅ (branch `pt2/third-person`)

Nicole: "Need third person mode" and "animations in the third person of other people doing things in the game. it feels a little lifeless looking at other players". Contract: `docs/cr/018-avatar-action-events.md` (two event-driven additions, no snapshot or struct change). Docs: `client/src/net/README.md` (avatar, animator, dev scripts), `contracts/events.md` (214, 707).

- **Third-person camera** (`client/src/scene/thirdPerson.ts`, `PlayerController.tsx`): **T** toggles first/third person (a free key: V is fly/push-to-talk, F dodge, G glider, N/B skill screens); persisted (`stores/viewStore.ts`, localStorage `lodestone_view_settings`; `?view=third` for one session). Over-the-shoulder boom (0.55 m to the side, 0.25 m up, 3.4 m back by default, 1.8-6 m), right or left shoulder. Mouse look is untouched (the same raw/unadjusted pointer-lock deltas and per-frame look; the camera only adds a position). Collision: both legs of the boom (eye -> shoulder, shoulder -> back along the view) are swept every 0.2 m and bisected against the movement ground's own queries (terrain + 0.3 m, building walls within 0.2 m via the player push-out, ceilings, cave rock); a blocked boom shortens in the same frame (never inside a wall or under the terrain) and grows back eased (0.2 s); the pivot height is smoothed over step-ups. Backed flat against a wall the boom collapses to the shoulder: the body hides and the first-person view model returns. Settings: the line under "Click to look around" (First person / Third person / shoulder / distance) and the debug panel. Your own body is the remote-player rig (`scene/LocalAvatar.tsx`) fed from local state and the combat runtime (every started action, the held secondary, mining swings, lost health); the first-person arms/weapon are hidden.
- **Aim stays meaningful**: the crosshair is the camera's ray; the client converges the combat aim from the eye (1.6 m) onto the first enemy or terrain that ray hits (up to 90 m, correction capped at 20 degrees) and uses it for projectiles, beams, hitscan and aimed areas (`combat/runtime.ts` `aimYaw/aimPitch`). Movement, the InputFrame yaw/pitch and the melee/form cones keep the plain view yaw, so the zone validates exactly what it did before (its projectile slack is 30 degrees, hitscan 20). Mining and town interactions reach from the eye, not the camera.
- **Avatar animation** (`client/src/net/avatarAnim.ts`, a pure state machine; `avatar.ts` rig): a jointed rig (hips, torso, neck, shoulders + elbows, hips + knees, darker legs, a skin-coloured head; the equipped weapon in the hand, angled so it reads from behind). Locomotion phase-locked to ground speed (idle breathing, walk, run, sprint lean and arm pump, jump tuck, fall, landing dip, glide, climb, swim); head and torso follow the look pitch; aimed actions raise the arms to it. Actions from Events already on the wire: 214 (sword slash forehand/backhand, whirlwind spin, plunge; spear thrust and sweep; bow draw/release; staff/wand bolt and jab; spells: off-hand cast, two-handed area slam, beam, shield, each with a hand glow in the spell colour; elemental forms; mobility dashes), 222 (hurt flinch), 203 + health 0 (falls onto the back until health returns). Derived client-side: the plain dodge (a ground-speed burst past 10.2 m/s: a roll in the direction of travel). **CR 018** adds the two things that were invisible: Event 214 targetId `0x4001`, once per press of the secondary on a sword/spear/bow (parry stance, spear charge, bow draw), and Event 707 `mine_swing` (pickaxe swings, to players within 64 m). Movement has no crouch; emotes not done.
- **LOD and cost**: LODs are switched by hand so only the figure in use is in the scene graph: the full rig within 25 m (capped at about 32 full rigs: a dense crowd shrinks the radius, `FullRigBudget`), capsule + weapon pivot with whole-body motion and the weapon arm at 25-80 m, a box with no animation beyond 80 m (the animator is not stepped). No per-frame allocations (one pose per avatar, cached action specs, the nameplate redrawn only on change). Main-thread cost (`npx vite-node client/src/net/dev/avatar-bench.ts 300`: avatar update + world-matrix pass, 1200 frames): 300 players spread out (10 % within 25 m) **0.36-0.66 ms/frame vs 0.91-1.03 ms before**; all 300 within 25 m (`NEAR=1`) **0.98 vs 0.93 ms**; heap growth ~0 vs 12-14 MB per 1200 frames before (the old per-frame nameplate key string). Draw calls per near avatar 13 (was 9), mid 4 (unchanged).
- **Benches** (RTX 3070, ANGLE D3D11, solo, high tier). Turn (`client/src/world/bench/turn.mjs`): before, pan p99 33.3 / max 50 ms, 2 frames over 33 ms, 1 long task (56 ms); after, first person pan p99 16.8 / max 16.8, 0 over 33 ms, 0 long tasks; third person pan p99 16.8 / max 33.3, 0 over 33.4 ms, 0 long tasks; flicks max 16.8 in every run. Traversal (`traversal.mjs high`; `docs/evidence/pt2-third-person-{before,after-1p,after-3p}-traversal-high.json`): walk p99 16.8 in all three (max 166.7 before from one 148 ms start-up long task, 50 after), fly p99 16.8; draw calls max 302 before, 297 after (1P), 328 (3P walk: your own body and its shadow); long tasks 1 before, 0 after.
- **Evidence** (`npx vite-node client/src/net/dev/third-person-shots.ts`: the real client and zone binary on private ports, two browsers; B photographs A only once B's own animator reports the action; `pt2-third-person-shots.json`): `pt2-third-person-self.png`, `-self-attack.png` (A's third-person view, sunsteel sword), `-remote-walk`, `-remote-sprint`, `-remote-attack`, `-remote-cast` (fire glow), `-remote-cast-area`, `-remote-bow-draw` (CR 018 guard), `-remote-spear`, `-remote-parry`, `-remote-dodge`, `-both` (B in third person, A slashing beyond), `-wall` / `-wall-against` (solo: one step from a house the boom is 1.9 m; backed against it, collapsed to the shoulder with the view model).
- **Tests**: vitest `net/avatarAnim.test.ts` (classification of every id family, idle, walk/sprint cadence and amplitude, jump/fall/landing, glide/climb/swim, look, slash, thrust, bow draw/release, cast glow, the derived dodge (not from sprinting or gliding), hurt, downed, zones without combat, no allocation), `scene/thirdPerson.test.ts` (open boom, wall pull-in in the same frame and eased return, a slope never under the terrain, a side wall, every boom sample free, bisection, the probe on a collide ground, crosshair convergence, the yaw seam), `net/avatar.test.ts` (Event 214 swings the held weapon, LOD bands, crowd budget); Go `combat/guard_test.go`, `game/mining_test.go` `TestMineSwingSeenByOthers`.

Gaps: other players' body yaw is their look yaw (strafing walks sideways, as before); remote bow strings don't bend (the weapon geometry is shared), the draw shows in the arms; a held remote parry/charge ends on a time-out (no release event); the derived dodge can't tell a plain dodge from an equally fast knock; the camera does not collide with props that have no player collision (waypoint stones, stall awnings); the voice/audio listener stays at the camera.

Shared files touched (surgical): `client/src/combat/runtime.ts` (converged aim for shots/beams/areas, `lastStarted`/`startedCount`, `selfId()`), `client/src/combat/weapons/FirstPersonWeapon.tsx` and `client/src/scene/FirstPersonArms.tsx` (hidden in third person), `client/src/scene/GameCanvas.tsx` (`LocalAvatar`), `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx` (`ViewHud`), `client/src/hud/{Overlays,DebugPanel}.tsx`, `client/src/state/live.ts` (`view`, `localActions`, aim), `client/src/devHandle.ts` (`setView`, `viewState`), `client/src/world/caves/caveManager.ts` (eye origin, swing counter), `client/src/world/buildings/townsManager.ts` (eye origin), `server/zone/internal/combat/{events,player,actions}.go`, `server/zone/internal/game/mining.go`, `contracts/events.md`, this section.

## Playtest 2: PvP + duels ✅ (branch `pt2/pvp-duels`)

Nicole: "Add pvp option if not in the same party and duels if you are which are a facilitated means". CR 019 (`docs/cr/019-pvp-duels.md`; amends spec section 2's "no PvP of any kind"). Contract: combat.yaml v4 `pvp` block, Interact 70 `pvp`, Events 990-997, HitResult refusals 16 (safe zone) / 17 (same party), player typeId bits 12 (flagged) and 13 (dueling). No protocol version change (Interact/Event are generic, HitClaim's doc only), no migration.

- **Open PvP (opt-in)**: a player hit lands only when both players are flagged, they are not in the same party, and neither stands in a safe zone (every settlement out to its radius + 20 m, and the Haven: the areas within `difficulty.havenRadiusM` of spawn, as Event 901 marks them). Claims on players go through the same server validation as PvE (`combat.World.ClaimPlayer`: the logged action, legality, age, hit limits, shape against the victim's last HIT_HISTORY_MS of positions, terrain LOS); damage = the same hit against a physical enemy x `pvp.damageMult` (0.5), then armor and shields; no statuses, skill XP, kill XP or loot from players. A killing hit downs the victim (the normal respawn, nothing dropped) and sends the kill feed (993) to both and to players within 64 m. Refused claims say why ("Safe zone: no PvP here.", "A party member: challenge them to a duel instead.").
- **Flag timers (anti-abuse)**: on at once; off after 10 s, restarted by any PvP hit given or taken meanwhile (no flagging off to escape); requests 5 s apart; refused within 6 s of dealing or taking any damage. Per session (a new login starts unflagged); it crosses zone borders (handoff field `pvp`).
- **Duels (party members)**: challenge within 30 m (party frame **Duel** button, or `/duel Name`) -> the challenged gets a prompt (**Y** accept / **U** decline, or `/duel accept|decline`; 20 s) -> 3-2-1 countdown -> a 25 m ring around the midpoint (a glowing band and curtain on the terrain; red while you stand outside it). Only the two duelists' hits on each other count, anywhere (towns and the Haven included: consensual, kept in place by the ring) and whatever their flags; enemies still hurt both normally. Ends: a duelist drops to 1 health (never down), outside the ring > 5 s (forfeit), `/yield` (`/duel yield`), logout or leaving the zone (the other wins), 3 minutes (draw); an enemy downing a duelist calls it off. Both get back the health the duel took (enemy damage stays) and their mana from the accept; result banner on both (Victory! / Defeat / Draw) and a party-chat line through the social bus to the party members the zone hosts.
- **UI**: character panel (C) PvP section with the toggle and the rules; `/pvp [on|off]`; HUD indicator top left (red crossed swords "PvP", "off in N s", green "safe zone · Town"), duel indicator with the clock, kill feed under it; nameplates: red crossed swords = flagged, gold = dueling (typeId bits, no new snapshot field). Sounds: trigger mappings for 990-997 and the countdown (`duel_count`) in `audio/triggers.ts`.
- **Wire**: the flag and dueling ride spare typeId bits (changed only on a toggle); everything else is reliable Events to the players involved, nothing to a player who never touches PvP. `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1 go test ./server/zone/internal/game -run WireDigest`: identical to main (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`, both builds), as expected: the scale scenario's bots never flag, duel or claim a player.
- **Tests**: Go `server/zone/internal/game/pvp_test.go` (in-process zone with fake connections and an exact tick clock: the rules matrix over flags x party (none/same/different) x either side safe x duel, 96 combinations; the matrix's corners through real claims and HitResult reasons; damage = 0.5 x PvE, kill feed, downed by the killer, respawn, inventory untouched; flag timers: cooldown, 10 s turn-off, restart by a hit, on-while-turning-off, combat lock; safe-zone state from an atlas (settlement radius + margin, Haven radius); duel: party/range/busy/decline/expiry, countdown without hits, only the duelists, 1-health floor with restore and party chat, enemies still hurt and a downed duelist calls it off, forfeit by leaving the ring (and coming back in time), time cap draw, yield, disconnect), `server/zone/internal/combat/pvp_test.go` (ClaimPlayer validation, scaling, one hit per swing, out of reach, duel floor, open-PvP down, zero-damage spells refused), the structural test now allows exactly two health-lowering paths (`hurtPlayer` from enemies, `hurtByPlayer` from ClaimPlayer). Client `client/src/ui/pvp/pvp.test.ts` (12 tests: state machine, decoders, kill feed, the hostile rule, player hit dimensions, nameplate marks, chat commands, the ring on the ground).
- **Evidence** (`node tools/acceptance/pt2-pvp.mjs`: private stack from this checkout, two headless Chromium players, dev auth, real UI and input; 26/26 PASS, `docs/evidence/pt2-pvp-acceptance.txt`): flagged from the panel and `/pvp on`, 3 claims in town refused with 16, rejoin on open ground 900 m from spawn, 9 hits accepted (10-17 damage at level 2) until the kill, kill feed on both, respawn at full health; then `/invite`, the party frame's Duel, prompt, countdown "3", ring 25 m, 9 duel hits, duel ends at 1 health (996 reason 1), health restored, "[Party] Duel Corin defeated Della in a duel (6 s)." in chat. Screenshots `docs/evidence/pt2-pvp-01..11-*.png`.

Gaps: no PvP across a zone border (a player on another zone is a ghost here; claims on it are refused as unknown targets) and duels end when a duelist changes zone; PvP uses the lenient PvE window (a strafing player can be hit a little behind where they are); duel results reach party members hosted by the duelists' zone only; challenging from a nameplate (3D picking) is not built (party frame and `/duel`). Seen while making the evidence, not from this branch: after merging third person, each headless browser's first session drew the 3D view black once its town discovery intro had played (canvas and WebGL context alive, labels drawn; after a rejoin it draws normally); the PvP shots were taken after the rejoin.

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/combat/{ai,validate,player,world}.go` (hurtPlayer's armor/shield/down steps shared, geometry check takes radius/height, per-tick position history), `server/zone/internal/game/{combat,session,server,entity,items}.go` (claim routing, Interact 70, pvpTick, the entity field, the weapon-look compare masks the PvP bits), `server/zone/main.go`, `client/src/combat/{runtime,events}.ts` (hostile players as hit targets, player hit dimensions), `client/src/net/{avatar.ts,NetPlay.tsx}`, `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `client/src/social/{commands,session}.ts`, `client/src/social/ui/PartyFrame.tsx`, `client/src/ui/progression/ProgressionHud.tsx`, `client/src/audio/triggers.ts`, `contracts/*`, `docs/SPEC.md`.

## Playtest 2: spell VFX ✅ (branch `pt2/spell-vfx`)

Nicole: "the spells as a whole are either not showing anything no visual effects or are quite underwhelming. I think we can stand to add some of the visual flair and polish as on wizard wars spells. and go all out if we assess the user for high graphics or they set high graphics". Docs: `client/src/combat/README.md` "Spell VFX" (files, the before/after table per spell type, quality, performance, readability). No contract, protocol or server change: everything rides Events already on the wire (214, 205, 206, 219, 200).

- **Audit (before)**: projectiles were unlit spheres flying through terrain and bodies; hitscan, beams and chains 1 px `THREE.Line`s (WebGL clamps the width: practically invisible); every area, rune and domain a flat translucent disc (floating or clipping on slopes, black for dark colours); direct spells a disc only when a target was found; **mobility and defensive spells showed nothing**; elemental forms a 0.3 s gold disc; **other players' casts (Event 214) were ignored entirely** (no client handler), as were statuses (206) and shields (219); enemy projectiles red spheres through the terrain; hits a 0.12 m octahedron.
- **The FX engine** (`client/src/combat/fx/`, ported and adapted from Wizard Wars' "spell vfx overhaul": GPU particles, pooled transients, recipes, shader warm-up): two GPU particle pools (closed-form motion in the vertex shader, ring buffer, 2 draw calls for every particle), fixed transient pools (shockwave rings, flashes, scorch / magic circle / zone decals laid on the terrain as small grids, domes, beams, shells, instanced spikes in six styles, lightning ribbons in one buffer, pooled point lights), and recipes per family (fire, ice, lightning, nature / stone / crystal for earth, wind, light, dark / blood / void). Every castable spell (92) resolves a look (tested). Your own casts leave your off hand (a glow that pulses on each cast and charges through windups; your avatar's hand in third person), projectiles have per-family cores, halos and trails and end where the runtime's shot really ends (ground, body, range: impact or explosion with shockwave, debris, light, scorch); beams are volumetric and re-aim while channelled; chains fork; areas burst in their shape (circle, cone from the caster, lines erupting spikes), lingering ones get animated floors that follow their caster; windups show a filling sigil where they will land (or under the target for Death Note / Unwritten); runes arm, idle and detonate on each enemy that enters; domains raise a dome with per-element weather; dashes leave trails; shields show a shell to others and an edge tint to you and burst when Event 219 says 0; statuses show on enemies (burn, freeze, shock, stun, slow, pull, curse, black flame...: particles plus a tinted rim shell); set pieces for the big ones (God Ray / Divine Judgement pillars, Lightning Strike / Act of God bolts from the sky, Eruption, Frost Nova, Let There Be Light, Collapse, Blood Nova, Thunderclap, Downburst, Monolith / Verdant Lance spikes). **Other players' casts** are simulated from Event 214 (windup, projectiles with visual ground/body collision, beams onto the nearest enemy in the cone, areas at the aim point, runes, dashes following the caster, shields, forms); enemy projectiles (205) take their attack's element. A screen effect (medium and up) merged into the world's existing EffectPass adds shockwave distortion, a few pixels of shake (UV offset: the camera rig is untouched) and capped flashes.
- **Quality**: FX tiers low / medium / high / ultra. An explicit `lodestone_fx_quality` override wins, then an explicit graphics setting (**high graphics = ultra, "go all out"**: 1.5x particles, hotter HDR cores for the bloom, full shake and distortion), else graphics auto capped by a light GPU assessment (renderer string + a 120-frame median; it only picks the default). Low stays readable: a third of the particles, no point lights, no screen effects, no status shells. Pooled lights per shader tier (0 / 2 / 4) fixed at mount. Settings hook: `fxSettings()` (`fx/SpellFxLayer.tsx`), documented for the graphics-settings agent.
- **Performance** (`client/src/combat/fx/dev/fx-bench.mjs`, `docs/evidence/pt2-spell-vfx-bench.{txt,json}`; production build, solo, 10 virtual casters each casting a random spell of all 92 about once a second): with vsync (60 Hz), high (FX ultra, 1920x1080) and low (1280x720): **60 fps locked idle and through the spam, 0 frames over 33 ms, 0 long tasks**; the turn bench (`world/bench/turn.mjs`) with the FX layer: pan and flick p99 16.8 ms, 0 long tasks on high and low (as without it). Uncapped (no vsync, the real cost): high idle p50 3.5-5.7 ms with the layer vs 3.3-4.2 ms without (the 4 pooled point lights and the screen effect; idle particle pools skip their draw), spam p50 4.1-6.9 / p99 8.8-14.8 ms for 130-142 casts in 12 s (peaks: 3,100-3,700 particles, 4 lights, ~150 spikes, ~30 live effects, 2 domes); low idle p50 1.8 vs 1.6 ms, spam p50 2.1 / p99 4.8 / max 10 ms, 0 long tasks. No program is linked during the spam (66 before / 66 after on high). Engine CPU: update <= 1.3 ms through load and idle, <= 5-6.5 ms in the worst spam frame. The GPU in these runs is shared with other agents' browsers, so absolute numbers vary run to run (the file keeps a repeat run). Shader warm-up: every FX program is in the WP19b compile; the first benches still showed 3-4 long tasks of 60-110 ms at the first casts (no new programs linked: ANGLE builds each draw's D3D variant on its first real draw; a pre-spam made them vanish), so the engine now draws every pooled object invisibly for a few frames once the world's warm-up is done (`prewarm`): what remains is an occasional 50-104 ms frame at the first cast of a spell on high (0 to 3 per run, none in the repeat run, none on low). Engine update max ~6 ms in the worst spam frame, ~1 ms typical; no per-frame allocation in the steady state.
- **Readability**: particles fade within ~2 m of the camera, your projectile's halo shrinks at the lens, beams fade near the camera, big flashes are dimmer per pixel, no spike grows within 1.5 m of the camera, sigils under your own feet stay faint, domes are a faint tint from inside, floors use deep tones and enemy telegraphs draw over them, status shells are rims, full-screen flashes are capped at 18 %, `prefers-reduced-motion` turns shake off.
- **Evidence**: gallery sheets (the real engine and post chain over test ground with dummies, `npx vite-node client/src/combat/fx/dev/fx-shots.ts`): `docs/evidence/pt2-spell-vfx-{fire,ice,lightning,earth,wind,light,dark,void}-{high,low}.png` (projectile, impact, area, beam/chain, rune, high-tier spell per element), `-seq-{high,low}.png` (frame sequences: Fireball, Lightning Strike, Let There Be Light, Inferno Domain, Chain Lightning, Frost Nova), `-firstperson-{high,low}.png` (your own casts with the crosshair: fireball, windup charge, beam, frost nova, blizzard, shield tint), `-status-{high,low}.png`; real client on a real zone with two browsers (`fx-zone-shots.ts`): `pt2-spell-vfx-zone.jpg` (Ember Flick, Frost Bite with its slow status, Magma Dash from the caster's view and from another player's, who before saw nothing).
- **Tests**: `client/src/combat/fx/fx.test.ts` (23: tier resolution and profiles, fxCount scaling and the frame budget, GPU classification and the frame sample, recipe selection for every spell, pools and caps, the light pool, spikes near the camera, bolts, the projectile cap, the runtime's routing of your casts and Events 214/206/219/205, remote-cast routing (dash variants, forms, spells, unseen casters), every castable spell / form / status played through the engine without errors).

Gaps: the gallery and bench run on a desktop GPU only (no mobile device measured; the GPU assessment's mobile/low classes are by renderer string); remote casts are simulated from one event (a remote channelled beam aims at the nearest enemy in its cone and does not follow the caster's later look; remote runes trigger on any enemy that enters, not on the zone's verdict); other players' melee swings have no FX beyond the avatar animation; statuses on players (yours) are not sent by the zone, so they show only on enemies; the "before" look is described, not screenshotted (it needed the old client against a zone).

Shared files touched (small): `client/src/combat/{runtime,CombatLayer,store}.ts(x)` (spell visuals to the FX engine, Events 214/206/219 handled, old spell meshes removed, telegraphs over spell floors), `client/src/world/post/postfx.ts` + `WorldScene.tsx` (the optional spell screen effect, medium and up), `client/src/world/warmExtras.ts` (FX programs in the warm-up), `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx` (debug solo mounts the FX layer; `?nofx`), this section.

## Playtest 2: new spell mechanics ✅ (branch `pt2/spell-mechanics`)

Nicole: "borrow from elden ring and other sources for interesting spell mechanics. like we could have spells that heat-seek towards the target, or spells that summon apparitions or whatever. anything." and "add spells with faster fire rates, full auto just hold it down spells. for example one could be sparks, or bubbles. or bullet rain." Contract: `docs/cr/020-spell-mechanics.md` (combat.yaml v5, `InputFlag.channel`, Events 223-225, two constants).

- **Mechanics** (generic blocks any spell can carry in combat.yaml, validated by the generator): **homing** (fly straight for a delay, lock the nearest target in a cone, turn-rate-limited steering, lose lock; client and zone run the same 1/60 s step, `spellsim.ts` / `spellsim.go`, pinned by golden vectors; the zone re-flies the path against the target's recorded positions before it accepts a hit), **hold-to-fire streams** (up to 15 shots/s while held, mana per shot, seeded jitter; one logged action per stream, each shot recorded and claimed by its own seq; the wire carries only the start with a seed and the stop: observers simulate the stream), **rain** (stream shots fall from above seeded points around the aim), **hold-to-empower charges** (release fires with damage, burst and speed scaled by the held fraction), **orbits** (orbs circle the caster; ring and rate checked by the zone), **delayed charges** and **chain-reaction brands** (the zone's own bursts), **summoned allies** (zone-side bodies owned by the caster on the enemies' steering, kept out of the enemy tables, drawn as EntityKind effect; they fight enemies, and players only under the PvP rules; capped per kind and 3 per player; leave on time-out, caster down/leaving/out of leash).
- **14 new spells** (names pending Nicole's sign-off): Cinder Spray (fire 1, stream of sparks), Pyre Brand (fire 4, brands that chain), Frost Bubbles (ice 2, stream of popping slowing bubbles), Hungering Souls (dark 3, four lifestealing seekers), Wraith Hound (dark 4, ally), Boulder Heave (earth 3, charge), Burrowing Seed (earth 4, delayed burst), Arc Sparks (lightning 2, 15/s stream), Storm Seekers (lightning 3, fan that curves in after 0.3 s), Whirling Blades (wind 3, orbit), Gale Phantom (wind 4, archer ally), Guiding Motes (light 1, two seekers), Sunlit Sentinel (light 5, turret ally), Starfall Hail (void 3, bullet rain). Cinder Spray, Frost Bubbles and Guiding Motes are learnable with a new character's first skill point (tier 1-2 nodes off the starting spells / a Light root). The skill tree shows them with tooltip lines per mechanic.
- **Client**: held slot keys (1-4, RMB with the slot selected) drive streams and charges (`timeline.ts`); per-shot prediction and claims, homing on whole 60 Hz steps, orbits (`runtime.ts`); FX engine: seekers steered on the shared step, remote streams from their seed, charge gathering, orbit blades, bursts, ally bolts and auras, small pops for stream bursts; spectral allies with a time-left bar (`CombatLayer.tsx`); remote stream/charge poses (`avatarAnim.classifyChannel`); audio cues (quieter repeats for streams, one cue per remote stream).
- **Found and fixed on the way**: the zone's HitClaim budget (30, 15/s) would have throttled a full-rate stream and counted toward a rate-limit kick: now 120 / 60 per second (`claims_rate_test.go`); the client claims at most 4 targets per bursting stream shot (the zone's cap).
- **Performance (G5)**: `BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000` 300 ticks x3, base main vs this branch on the same machine: p50 3.51-3.65 vs 3.14-3.28 ms, p99 5.6-9.9 vs 4.6-5.7 ms, CPU 91-93 vs 90-91 Mcycles/tick, 137.2 kbps/client both (noise; the bots never cast these spells). **Wire digest unchanged** for both scale scenarios (`docs/evidence/pt2-spell-mechanics-bench.txt`).
- **Evidence** (real client x2 on a real zone with training dummies, `npx vite-node client/src/combat/dev/mechanics-shots.ts`): `docs/evidence/pt2-spell-mechanics-{streams,homing,charge-orbit,bursts,summons}.jpg` (caster's view and another player's), `pt2-spell-mechanics-run.json` (claims per spell: 0 refused across the run; homing hits accepted through the re-flight; allies seen by the other player).
- **Tests**: Go `combat/spellsim_golden_test.go` (replays the client's vectors), `combat/mechanics_test.go` (stream rate, mana drain and five anti-cheat input patterns, per-shot claims, charge release / cancel, homing reach and per-orb limits, orbit ring and rate, delayed bursts, brand chains, ally lifecycle / caps / enemy targets / PvP rule), `game/summons_test.go` (ally entity kind and name, PvP rule end to end, leaves with its caster), `game/claims_rate_test.go`; vitest `combat/spellsim.test.ts`, `combat/mechanics.test.ts`, fx routing for streams, charges and Events 223-225, skills tree counts.

Gaps: allies take no damage and enemies ignore them (apparitions; a later CR could give them health and threat) and do not cross zone borders; a remote stream aims from the caster's 8-bit snapshot yaw/pitch (within ~1.4 deg); orbit and rain validation is ring/area based (lenient, like the other areas), homing is re-flown at three render lags; the evidence's charge gathering is a hand glow in first person (particles fade at the lens).
## Playtest 2: dash overhaul ✅ (branch `pt2/dash-overhaul`, merged into `pt2/spell-mechanics`)

Nicole: "make the dash attacks more useful. as it stands, you can sprint faster on your own ... last as long as you hold it down, draining either mana or health ... some of them like the fire one, propel you in the direction you are looking. it should feel like you are iron man." CR 021 (`docs/cr/021-dash-overhaul.md`). Contract: combat.yaml `dashes` gain `aim`, `exitSpeed`, `sustain`, `trail`; mobility spells cost 6 mana to start; Event 230 `dash_slam`. Holding uses CR 020's `InputFlag.channel` (no protocol version change, no new snapshot field, `State` unchanged).

- **Every dash is fast now**: bursts 30-60 m/s, held speeds 22-36 m/s with a 120 m/s blink pulse (sprint is 12.8); letting go keeps 14-18 m/s (was a clamp to 6 m/s, slower than walking).
- **Hold to sustain** (the spell's key 1-4, or RMB with the slot selected): the dash goes on while held and paid: 6-28 mana/s, Phase Slip 6 mana/s + 14 health/s (never below 1). Release: a 4-step carry, then the exit speed.
- **Magma Dash = jet flight**: starts and thrusts along the 3D look (look up to go straight up), steers, gravity cancelled while thrusting, burns enemies it passes, sets grass alight along the path (chem ignite), and diving into the ground slams (45 + knockback within 4.5 m, Event 230).
- **Each element**: Glacier Step skates on water (water is ground while held; chem freeze along the way), Static Step blinks (2 m teleport pulses, bolts and shock marks), Root Launch bursts through with rock spikes and knockback, Updraft glides long (fall held to 1 m/s), Sunstep leaves a radiant trail that heals you and your party nearby, Skip phases (i-frames kept up while held), Phase Slip is the blood dash with lifesteal. Trail hits: each enemy once per dash, scaled like your spells; PvE only.
- **Feel**: FOV kick up to +16 degrees, radial speed lines, shake on high/ultra FX, a held whoosh, per-element FX kept up while the dash runs (yours and other players'), the slam burst.
- **Authority**: the zone records the dash it allowed and pays every held step before the movement step (`Player.sustainDash`); a hold it did not allow or cannot charge is stripped and the shared step ends the dash on both sides. Validation accepts dash speeds only while a dash runs. Effects are the zone's (`World.dashTick`, `Server.dashEnvTick`).
- **Twins**: TS/Go movement bit-exact; golden version 4 (`movement_wp13_dash_{flat,plane,island}.json`) deliberately regenerated with held jet/blink/skate/glide/phase sections and an island-in-a-lake skate, replayed by Go at 1e-9 m; WP02/WP10 goldens unchanged.
- **G5**: wire digest identical (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...` on base and head; bots never dash); `BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000` median 112 vs 114 M cycles, 3.6 vs 3.5 ms max (noise).
- **Tests**: Go `server/pkg/movement/dash_hold_test.go`, `server/zone/internal/combat/dashes_test.go`, `server/zone/internal/game/dashes_test.go`; vitest `client/src/sim/dashHold.test.ts`, `client/src/combat/dashes.test.ts`.
- **Evidence**: `docs/evidence/pt2-dash-{magma,glacier,elements}.png` + `pt2-dash-shots.json` (`npx vite-node src/combat/dev/dash-shots.ts`: two headless clients on a real zone).

Gaps: other players' avatars show the short dash pose, not a held flight pose; the skate leaves no walkable ice for others (the frozen path is the chem freeze state).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/combat/{player,world,combat_test}.go` (dashBegin/sustainDash/noteDash hooks, dashTick, the payBlood allowance), `server/zone/internal/game/combat.go` (dashEnvTick, the dev spell grant), `client/src/combat/{timeline,runtime,link}.ts` (the hold hook, `castHeld` key state, Event 230), `client/src/combat/fx/{engine,SpellFxLayer}.ts(x)` (the dash() hook, the feel tick), `client/src/state/live.ts` + `scene/PlayerController.tsx` (`localPlayer.dash`), `client/src/audio/triggers.ts`, `contracts/{combat.yaml,events.md,codegen/lib/combat.mjs}`.

## Playtest 2: shared spell visuals ✅ (branch `pt2/shared-spell-fx`)

Nicole: "make spells graphics/animations shared client state". Read as: what you see of your own spells and casting animations is what everyone else sees of them (same effect, origin, timing, shape and held state), drawn from shared, replicated state instead of each client guessing. Contract: `docs/cr/022-shared-spell-fx.md` (the before/after audit per mechanic). No protocol version, struct or snapshot field change.

- **One state per caster** (`client/src/combat/castState.ts`): the newest action, what is held (stream, charge, beam, guard), the running dash and whether its key holds it, the exact aim. Your prediction fills yours (`runtime.ts`); replication fills everyone else's from Events 214 / 223 / 226 / 219 / 707 and their drawn Activity casting bit, applied when their drawn body reaches that moment (their interpolation delay; before, spells left a body ~100 ms before its arm moved). The avatar (`net/avatarAnim.ts followCaster`, the same call for your third-person body and everyone's) and the FX engine read only that record. Shared helpers instead of twin code paths: `combat/aim.ts` (aim walk, target pick, beam end), `fx/castOrigin.ts casterHand` (staff/wand tip or off hand for every body), one projectile launch (leaves the hand, eases onto the eye path) for every caster.
- **Closed gaps**: held dashes fly for others (jet: Iron Man along the velocity; skate; soar; rush) with their FX alive exactly while held; channelled beams follow the caster's aim; streams, rain and charges shoot along the caster's exact aim (third person included: aimed steps send the crosshair aim in the frame); a player arriving mid-stream starts it at the right shot; world-space swing arcs for everyone; others' arrows, staff bolts and slams (before: nothing), the drawn shot at its charged speed; spear charge / bow draw held exactly as long as held (before: a 2.5-3 s time-out); shields burst for observers when they break; forms burst on their first hit frame; the cast flourish for every caster; your own body now holds the stream and charge poses others saw. Statuses on players: the zone applies none (combat.yaml: enemies only), so nothing to replicate.
- **Wire** (event-driven; nothing for a player who holds nothing): the Activity casting bit on players while a held action runs (a byte when it flips); Event 226 `held` (exact aim of a held stream / charge / beam, <= 10 Hz on change, a settle update, a 1 s keepalive with the seed for late observers; 64 m, not the holder); 219 to observers on a shield break; 214 + u8 draw for charged weapon moves. Per holder per observer: 2.9 kbps while sweeping continuously, 0.24 kbps holding still.
- **G5**: wire digest identical (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`, base and head); `BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000` 100.3-103.6 vs 103.3-103.9 M cycles/tick, 137.2 kbps/client both (`docs/evidence/pt2-shared-fx-bench.txt`).
- **Evidence** (`npx vite-node src/combat/dev/shared-fx-shots.ts` from client/: a private zone, two headless clients, the caster in third person and an observer 11 m away, both screenshots at the same moment plus 1 s of parity samples per mechanic; the same script run on main for `-before`): `docs/evidence/pt2-shared-fx-{casts,channels,dashes,weapons}{,-before}.jpg`, `pt2-shared-fx-run{,-before}.json`. After: the caster's body and the observer's avatar of them agree on the action in 31/32 and on the held pose in 32/32 (before: held dashes showed `dodge`/`dash`/nothing, a 3.6 s bow draw nothing); held aims agree to 0.001-0.002 deg at rest (the 8-bit look: 0.23-0.33 deg, and 0.5-3.8 deg off in third person before).
- **Tests**: Go `server/zone/internal/combat/held_test.go` (idle sends nothing, rate limit and audience, exact aim, keepalives, settle, the dash bit only while thrusting, beam window, guards, shield break), `server/zone/internal/game/held_test.go` (near observer gets 226 and the bit, the caster and a player 110 m away get nothing, the bit drops on release); vitest `client/src/combat/castState.test.ts` (decoders and Go packing, drawn-time delay, the casting bit, aimOf, your pose vs theirs for a stream / jet / 3.6 s bow draw / mining, aim in the frame, beams and dash FX drawn from the record), fx and avatar suites updated.

Gaps: mining and forms are unit tested, not in the two-browser run; particle sprays still use `Math.random` (paths, seeds, timing and held state match, single sparks do not); an observer beyond 64 m of a dash's start learns its variant from the first keepalive (<= 1 s); several dash screenshots frame the fast-moving caster poorly (the parity samples measure it).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/combat/{player,dashes,actions,ai,world}.go`, `server/zone/internal/game/combat.go`, `client/src/combat/{runtime,timeline,events,link}.ts`, `client/src/combat/fx/{engine,api,dashFx,castOrigin}.ts`, `client/src/net/{avatarAnim,avatar}.ts`, `client/src/net/RemotePlayers.tsx`, `client/src/scene/{LocalAvatar,PlayerController}.tsx`, `client/src/sim/localSim.ts`, `contracts/events.md`.

## Playtest 2: new content ✅ (branch `pt2/content`)

Nicole: "add some other content to this game". CR 023 (`docs/cr/023-content.md`). One data file for all of it: `contracts/content.yaml` → `npm run gen` → `server/pkg/contentdefs` + client `CONTENT` (the generator checks every enemy, item, role, biome and feat stat, and that the codex covers every enemy). Migration **0025** `character_content` (run `npm run migrate`), Events **1000-1022** (new range 1000-1029), Interact **80** `content`, additive quest wire fields. Docs: `client/src/ui/content/README.md` (where to find each thing), `contracts/events.md`.

- **Townsfolk side quests**: 2-4 quest givers per settlement (farmers, villagers, merchants, innkeepers, guards; picked from the world seed and the settlement; never the smith or bounty keeper), each with an endless deterministic sequence of requests, a line of dialogue on the offer card ("Una took a wrong turn chasing a goat, the fool, somewhere near The Shattered Arch. Bring them back to Draedmore?") and five kinds: **hunt** (cull N creatures near a named place; kills counted and saved), **gather** (bring herbs, pelts, meat or ore: hand them over by talking to the giver), **deliver** (a sealed parcel to someone in one of the three nearest towns: travel), **explore** (go to a ruin, shrine, watchtower or landmark and report back), **rescue** (find a lost villager at a camp: they appear as an NPC, say hello and follow you home; then the giver thanks you). Never the same kind twice running. Rewards: gold, XP, and **renown** with the giver's town: Stranger → Known (20) → Trusted (60) → Honored (120); each tier adds stock to that town's merchants and smith (silver arms at Trusted, starmetal and the Dawnroot Elixir at Honored). The next request comes when one is done. At most 6 held.
- **Story quests** (handcrafted, places resolved around the town that offers them): **The Lodestone's Hum** (an innkeeper: a shrine's hum, its lore tablet, a tower crystal, a smith in the next town, the guardians of a ruin; 6 steps), **Embers of the Old Watch** (a guard or bounty keeper: a watchtower, a bandit camp's banner carried to a town in another region), **The Bell Beneath** (a farmer: open a sealed vault and defeat its keeper). Gold, XP, renown and a rolled rare-or-better item.
- **World events** (zone-scheduled every ~75 s per player without one, 160-380 m away on open ground the zone owns, never in towns; announced within 650 m with a toast, a tracker under the compass with its clock and progress, a red star on the compass and map, a beam in the world): **Starfall** (a Starfall Sentinel elite and wisps guard the shard: starmetal ore and an uncommon-or-better item each), **Caravan Ambush** (three bandit waves; the caravan's health drops while bandits stand at it), **The Wandering Behemoth** (a Thornhide Behemoth roaming leg by leg across the land, re-anchored as it goes), **The Wild Hunt** (night only: the Huntsman and four Gloamhounds), **The Glintfox** (a fox that never fights and runs from you; 45 s to catch it once spooked: gold and gems). Groups take the area's level (CR 012); rewards are personal (each participant rolls their own); finished groups are retired (never respawn). New combat behaviour `flee`.
- **Sealed vaults** (14 on the official world: one per region under the smaller ruin nearest the region's middle, else a shrine): found within 70 m (Event 1021, saved, a gold keyhole on the map); a seal puzzle chosen per vault: **The Three Levers** (pull sun / moon / star in the inscription's order; a wrong pull resets), **The Weighing Stones** (three pressure stones held at once, each stays down 14 s: two players, or one fast one), **The Cold Braziers** (light both with fire magic), **The Weeping Font** (still it with ice magic); two elite packs guard the yard; the opened seal wakes the **Vault Keeper** (a boss: a wide sweep and a 4.5 m burning sigil under everyone in the fight, phase 2 at half health with two adds); its fall clears the vault (a story step, a feat) and opens the chest: gold and a **guaranteed rare** per character every 20 h. A vault resets 15 min after the last player leaves.
- **Collectibles and codex**: **16 lore tablets** (standing stones with a blue rune at ruins, shrines and watchtowers, dealt over all 69 such sites; E to read; the Lore tab keeps them); the **creature codex** (a bestiary entry on the first kill of each of the 41 creatures: habitat, lore, health, speed, element, what it is weak to and resists, kills); **25 feats** (First Blood, Skyreader, Slimebane, World-Walker, Duelist, Folk Hero, Loremaster, Vaultbreaker, Keeper of the Song, Green Thumb...) with gold/XP and **titles**, chosen in the Feats tab and shown under your name on other players' nameplates (Event 1005, only for players who have one).
- **Foraging** (chosen over fishing): herb patches on a 48 m grid by biome (Heartleaf, Emberroot, Frostcap, Glowmoss, Duskpetal), E to gather 1-2, regrowing per character after 15 min; five new hearth recipes (Emberroot Skewer, Frostcap Broth, Glowmoss Tonic, Duskpetal Draught, Dawnroot Elixir).
- **Client**: offer card with the giver's line and renown; journal (J) tabs Quests / Bestiary / Lore / Feats / Renown; world event tracker, notes, tablet reader, vault panel, `[E]` prompts (arbitrated with the town, cave and loot prompts); in-world tablets, herb clumps (one instanced mesh), levers, pressure stones, braziers, the font, the vault door and chest, event beams, the caravan's wagon, the starfall's shard.
- **Persistence**: one jsonb per character (renown, each giver's next request, codex kills, tablets, feats and title, counters, regions walked, vaults found and chest times, herb patches regrowing), epoch-fenced like the quest log; local profiles in their save blob (`localsave.State.Content`); a WP16 handoff field. Changes are saved within seconds (coalesced per character by the write-behind queue).
- **Network**: event driven, nothing per tick; places (1007, binary) once per session per 256 m area; content is off in atlas-less worlds (the flat and scale worlds). `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1`: **identical** (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`). `BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000` (300 ticks, alternating base main / head x3): p50 3.47/3.72/3.00 vs 3.02/3.00/2.76 ms, p99 5.26/6.53/4.04 vs 5.09/4.51/4.52 ms, CPU 89.5-90.6 vs 87.8-89.7 Mcycles/tick, 137.2 kbps/client and 1983 allocs/op both: no change (`docs/evidence/pt2-content-bench.txt`). A first version changed the digest (the bots' kills sent codex and feat Events); content is gated on an atlas or towns since.
- **Ownership (WP16)**: the zone owning an event's or vault's region runs it; players walking there are handed to that zone. Kill switch `LODESTONE_ZONE_NO_WORLD_EVENTS=1`; `GET /debug/content` lists vault plans, live events, tablets and quest givers.

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/content/content_test.go` (givers and side quests deterministic per seed, every kind generated, no unfilled text, never the same kind twice; stories resolve on a synthetic world (the banner goes to another region); state round trip, feats once, titles, renown tiers; the codex covers every enemy; herb patches deterministic and never in two areas; vault plans deterministic, all four puzzles solvable, wrong lever resets, plates must be held together, wrong element; event picks deterministic, night only at night), `internal/quests/content_test.go` (kill counts saved over a reload, give needs the item and the right NPC, read/attune/escort/dungeon, at most 6 side quests), in-process on the official world `internal/game/content_game_test.go` + `content_rescue_test.go` (a gathering request offered on talk with its line, accepted, handed in: gold, renown, the giver's next request, the state saved and read back after a reconnect; a delivery's parcel carried to the other town; a rescue: the villager found, following, home, thanks, gone; world events: the Glintfox caught, the Starfall's ore and gold, an expired Wild Hunt pays nothing and is retired; a sealed vault: wrong lever, the right order, the keeper, the guaranteed rare once, the Vaultbreaker feat; a lore tablet read once, a herb picked then regrowing, the Duelist title seen by another player and cleared; the handoff field), `content_store_test.go` (Postgres: round trip, epoch fence, missing row). Vitest `client/src/ui/content/content.test.ts` (1007 decode, the reducer for sync / codex / feats / renown / titles / tablets / a world event to victory, the E target for tablets, herbs, levers and a sealed chest, weaknesses, reward text). Full suites: gen:check, `npm run migrate` (applied 0025 on the dev DB), go vet and go test for server/pkg and server/zone (all packages ok, incl. the 275 s game package and the process tests), client typecheck, `eslint .`, `npx vitest run` (61 files, 567 tests), test:golden PASS (wp01 evidence restored).

Evidence (`cd client && npx vite-node src/ui/content/dev/content-shots.ts`: the real client and zone binary on the official world, one headless browser, dev-started events; `docs/evidence/pt2-content-shots.txt`): `pt2-content-sidequest-dialogue.png` (Caelia, villager of Draedmore, offers a rescue with her line, the steps, the minimap and renown), `-sidequest-accepted.png`, `-event-caravan.png` (Caravan Ambush: tracker, wagon, bandits, compass star), `-event-starfall.png` (the Warded Starfall Sentinel at the shard), `-vault-puzzle.png`, `-vault-lever.png` (two of three levers down, the inscription), `-vault-keeper.png`, `-vault-keeper-2.png` (the keeper awake: boss bar, the guardians), `-lore-tablet-world.png`, `-lore-tablet.png`, `-codex.png` (the bestiary after the first kill), `-feats.png`.

Known gaps: the account upgrade does not copy the content state (like the quest log); world events and vaults stay on their zone (no fight across a border; ghost-band players hear of an event when they cross); rescued villagers walk without building collision (like WP20 NPCs) and catch up by teleport beyond 45 m; the boss bar says "phase 1/3" for the two-phase keeper until its first phase change (the client's first guess, `combat/runtime.ts`, left for the combat owner); the Behemoth's legs are short hops within open ground (it never enters towns or water); the evidence's keeper shot shows the fight's start, not a sigil mid-burn; all names and lines are proposals for Nicole (`contracts/content.yaml`). `npm run typecheck` fails only in `tools/creator` in a worktree (ajv resolution, as before).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/game/{server,session,quests,towns,progression,duel,combat,explore}.go` (fields, hooks), `server/zone/internal/combat/{ai,world}.go` (the flee hook, `Group.Retired`), `server/zone/internal/items/roll.go` (`Rolled.MinRarity`), `server/zone/internal/quests/{quests,wire}.go`, `server/pkg/localsave/state.go`, `server/zone/main.go`, `contracts/{combat.yaml,items.yaml,events.md,codegen/gen.mjs,codegen/lib/items.mjs,ts/src/index.ts}`, client `net/{NetPlay.tsx,avatar.ts}`, `screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `ui/quests/{QuestHud.tsx,events.ts}`, `ui/map/{Compass,MapScreen}.tsx`, `ui/inventory/LootHud.tsx`.

## Playtest 2: graphics settings + art direction ✅ (branch `pt2/graphics`)

Nicole: "add graphics options for different kinds of rpg styles and graphics quality too"; "take inspiration in general from queen's domain ... do not make the graphics exactly look like queen ... make it so you can get there in the graphics settings. the main graphics inspirations are valheim and breath of the wild". Decision note: `docs/decisions/0023-art-direction.md`.

- **Graphics screen** (`client/src/ui/graphics/GraphicsPanel.tsx`): a **Graphics** button in the top-right row (Sound at the corner, Graphics, then Friends; `hud/layout.ts`) or **F10**; Esc or F10 closes; `data-ui-panel` (frees the mouse, hides the look hint, the quest tracker hides under it); opening it closes the Sound panel and vice versa. `openGraphicsPanel()` is exported for the Esc menu (pt2/onboarding). Visual style cards, quality presets **Auto / Low / Medium / High / Ultra** ("Custom (on High)" once a control is changed, "Reset to preset"), and controls: render scale 50-100 %, pixel-ratio cap 1-2.5x, shadows Off/Low/High + distance, view distance (m and 64 m chunk rings), foliage (grass reach and density, 0-150 %), post effects (bloom, light shafts, FXAA; fog/tone/grade always run), anti-aliasing Off/FXAA/MSAA 2x/4x, max FPS (30-144 or unlimited: whole frames skipped in the world loop), spell effects Auto/Low/Medium/High/Ultra (shows what Auto runs now).
- **One source of truth** (`client/src/stores/qualityStore.ts`, key `lodestone_quality_settings`, now **version 1**): preset, overrides, style, spell effects and the auto-detect result. Version 0 (`{ setting }`, still written by the benches) and a bare legacy string migrate; persisted values are validated and clamped. `stores/graphics.ts` `resolveGraphics()` is the one place that turns preset + tier + overrides + style into renderer numbers (Low and High resolve to exactly the pre-settings numbers; cheap knobs follow the auto-stepped tier, shader-shaping ones the shader tier, the WP19 rule). The spell VFX FX setting (`combat/fx/quality.ts` `setFxSetting/getFxSetting`, `fxSettings()`) now reads and writes the store's `effects`; the old `lodestone_fx_quality` key is adopted once and removed; `fxLightCount('ultra')` = 4.
- **Auto-detect** (`stores/gpuDetect.ts`, `scene/GraphicsApplier.tsx`): first run with Auto reads the renderer string from a throwaway WebGL2 context *before* anything renders (RTX/GTX/Radeon RX/Arc/Apple GPU -> High, Intel/AMD integrated -> Medium, mobile/software -> Low, < 8192 textures caps at Medium), so the world builds at that tier with no recompile; once playable, 120 frames (after 90 skipped) refine it (median > 24 ms one step down, > 40 ms Low; never up). Auto never picks Ultra. An explicit choice always wins.
- **Styles** (`client/src/world/style/styles.ts`): **Lodestone** (new default: soft two-tone cel light + rim light on upright surfaces, brighter saturated gradient skies matched by the fog colour, 1.6x aerial haze, valley fog that thickens at dusk and stays at night with moonlit in-scatter, darker nights, 1.6x warm town/fire lights, wind-swept grass with a gust sheen, turquoise water with a stronger fresnel and a broken shore-foam band, soft bloom), **Painterly** (3 toon bands, ink outlines from depth edges, posterized flat colour, banded sky), **Grim** (desaturated, cold, fog floor in the valleys, 0.45x night lift, vignette), **Retro 32-bit** (240 internal rows upscaled nearest-neighbour, vertex snapping, affine texture wobble + texel-snapped sampling on the town atlas, 15-bit colour with 4x4 ordered dither, 45-230 m fog that also climbs the horizon; caps view distance 520 m, foliage, MSAA/FXAA, bloom, god rays and shadow size, so it is cheap). Nothing is named after another game (tested).
- **How**: `world/style/styleShader.ts` patches three.js chunks once (RE_Direct toon + rim for physical/lambert/phong; snap in `logdepthbuf_vertex`, not in shadow passes) and registers two uniforms on every ShaderLib entry with a shared `Float32Array` (cloned by reference), so a style switch is a write: no recompile for any lit material in the game, including other packages'. ShaderMaterials get the uniforms through the default `onBeforeCompile`. Screen side: `world/post/styleEffect.ts`, merged into the one EffectPass after the grade (outline/posterize/vignette/dither, all uniforms); the spell screen pass (shockwaves, the dash and impact shake, flashes, your shield's edge tint) stays first in the same pass, medium and up, in every style (Retro too: it is feedback, not decoration). Sky dome + fog share `SKY_STYLE_GLSL`; water, wind (`uWindSweep`), bloom, god-ray weight and torch light (`liveStyle.torch`) are per-frame uniforms. Opt-outs: `LS_NO_RIM` (terrain, horizon, cave rock).
- **Rain** now changes the world: wet darker terrain (`uWet`, never driven before), overcast clouds, the sun dimmed by 55 %, thicker fog and haze, lower saturation, eased in and out; the rain streaks moved from NightLayer to `world/sky/rainStreaks.ts` (owned by the world scene), so solo play shows them too (`?rain=1` or `__lodestone.setRain(true)`).
- **Shader warm-up (WP19b)**: the post chain's full-screen programs (main pass with the style and spell effects, FXAA) are compiled with the scene (`PostFX.warmObjects`, the last pass against the canvas); grass/scatter/town program keys bumped for the new code. Traversal: 0 programs linked after playable on High and Low. After merging main (third person, avatars, spell mechanics, dash overhaul, shared spell visuals, new content): switching to third person and 12 s of every castable spell from 8 virtual casters (~105 casts) links 0 programs after playable in every style on High and in Lodestone/Retro on Low, with 0 long tasks and 0 page errors (`warmCheck` in `pt2-graphics-turn-bench.json`).
- **Bug fixed on the way** (`world/sky/sunShadows.ts`): rebuilding the shadow rig with the same cascade count (a style or shadow-distance change; also explicit Medium <-> Retro) threw `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'needsUpdate')` every frame: `CSM.dispose()` deleted the cascade uniforms from live shader objects whose program three.js then reused. The rig now restores materials itself and adopts the live shader objects.
- **Benches** (RTX 3070, ANGLE D3D11, headless Chromium, solo Vite dev server; re-run after merging main `c2e2a6f`: `docs/evidence/pt2-graphics-turn-bench.json`; before = that main, after = this branch). Turn (`turn.mjs`, vsync): before and after, High (1920x1080) and Low (1280x720) pan and flick p99 16.8 / max 16.8 ms, 0 frames over 33 ms (so none over 50), 0 long tasks; Painterly High and Retro High the same. Uncapped (no vsync, the true frame cost), pan p50/p99/max: High before 4.3/7.0/8.8 ms, after Lodestone 4.5/6.6/8.9, Painterly 4.3/6.6/9.2, Grim 4.4/7.4/9.7, Retro 3.7/8.5/17.8 (flick max 7.7-10.4); Low before 2.5/5.6/10.4, after Lodestone 2.6/4.1/10.2, Retro 2.5/3.8/10.3; 0 long tasks in every run. Traversal (before the main merges, `pt2-graphics-{before,after}-traversal*`, `traversal.mjs high low`, no shots): High walk p99 16.8 max 83 ms (before 33.4 / 650), fly p99 16.8; long tasks 4, max 194 ms (before 7, max 642; streaming/town builds, present before); draw calls max 302 (296); Low walk max 50 ms, fly 16.8, 0 long tasks (before 6, max 228), draw calls 128 (128).
- **Evidence** (`node client/src/world/style/dev/graphics-shots.mjs`: solo, headless GPU, `pt2-graphics-shots.json` with frame times per shot): `docs/evidence/pt2-graphics-<style>-<scene>-high.png` for style in lodestone/painterly/grim/retro and scene in day-town, dusk-meadow, night-fire (town lanterns at 22:30), cave (gem chamber), rain, spell (an eruption, third person); `pt2-graphics-{lodestone,retro}-<scene>-low.png`; `pt2-graphics-panel.png`. Refreshed after merging main: the third-person spell shots show your avatar in the navy shirt and the new spell FX in each style.
- **Tests**: `client/src/stores/graphics.test.ts` (presets monotonic and equal to the pre-settings numbers, overrides and Custom, cheap vs shader knobs, post off, grass from foliage, spell screen pass, style completeness and naming, Retro caps without touching stored values and its pixel ratio, GPU classification, refine/sampler, the player's choice wins, v0/raw/junk migration, clamping, localStorage round trip, presets clearing overrides, auto capped at High, stored detection, the shader chunk patch and shared uniform). Existing `stores.test.ts` and `combat/fx/fx.test.ts` pass unchanged.

Gaps: toon/rim apply to three's built-in lit materials (and their patched versions); the impostor billboards and the grass blades' own colour ramp are untouched by the bands. Retro's affine wobble is on the town atlas only (terrain is triplanar in world space; its low resolution and snapping carry the look). Rain streaks and the sky do not stop at a roof. No mobile device was measured; the auto-detect's mobile/integrated classes are by renderer string. The screenshots are from the dev server (shot frame times are vsync-capped). Switching to Retro mid-game costs one long frame (~220-270 ms in the gallery run: the post chain, shadow rig and render targets rebuild at the new size and the view-distance cap drops chunks); the other styles switch as uniform writes. Retro caps bloom, so spell cores glow less there (the embers, smoke and spikes still read).

Shared files touched (surgical): `client/src/world/{WorldScene.tsx,config.ts,warmup.ts,wind.ts}`, `world/post/postfx.ts`, `world/sky/{atmosphere,skyDome,sunShadows}.ts`, `world/water/water.ts`, `world/terrain/{terrainMaterial,horizon}.ts`, `world/scatter/{scatterManager,scatterMaterials}.ts`, `world/buildings/{townMaterial,townsManager}.ts`, `world/caves/caveVisuals.ts`, `systems/NightLayer.tsx` (rain moved out, torch multiplier), `scene/GameCanvas.tsx` (pixel ratio, GraphicsApplier), `combat/fx/quality.ts` (FX setting in the store), `audio/ui/{AudioRoot,AudioSettingsPanel}.tsx` (Graphics button beside Sound), `hud/layout.ts`, `devHandle.ts` (`setStyle`, `setGraphics`, `graphics`, `setRain`), `world/bench/{turn,traversal}.mjs` (`LODESTONE_BENCH_STYLE`, turn `LODESTONE_BENCH_UNCAPPED`), this section.

## Playtest 2: new player onboarding ✅ (branch `pt2/onboarding`)

The first hour for a new player: an Esc menu, tips when they matter, a first quest close to home, a clear death, and a "What's new" changelog (Nicole: "put a what's new changelog based on the diff from what the user last saw on their last version"). CR 024 (`docs/cr/024-respawn-grace.md`: one constant, `PLAYER_RESPAWN_GRACE_S`; no protocol change, no migration).
- **Esc menu** (`client/src/ui/menu`): Esc with no panel open (or freeing the mouse with Esc) opens it: Resume, Controls (every binding, grouped, from the real handlers: `controls.ts`, and a test checks each listed key exists in the file it names), Graphics (main's `openGraphicsPanel`, F10), Sound, What's new, the camera settings, tips on/off and "show again", Leave to title. Not a pause (shared world). Esc still closes an open panel first; panel hotkeys wait while the menu is up. The lock hint is one line ("Click to look around · Esc: menu and controls").
- **Tips** (`client/src/ui/tips`): one short card at a time, once per character, when it is relevant (getting around, the tracked quest, `[E] Talk`, the first enemy, low health, night, a cliff, falling, a free skill point); they wait while a panel, the menu or the downed screen is up (a tip a panel interrupts at once comes back).
- **First quest "Slimes in the meadows"** (`server/zone/internal/quests/content.go`, game `quests.go`): a new character is sent to a farmer/villager in the starting town, then to kill 5 slimes at the marked Haven packs (kill-N objective: since merging main it is CR 023's `ObjKill`, `Objective.N` / `Quest.Count`, R 0 = anywhere; kills are signalled once, from `questsOnKill`, content on or off), then back for gold, XP, two draughts and a leather cap, then the Ward quest. Older characters (the Ward already started) never get it; dropping it starts the Ward.
- **Quest markers follow people** (`client/src/ui/quests/live.ts`): a talk step's marker (compass, beam, tracker) follows the person while they are drawn (townsfolk leave work in the evening; the marker led to an empty anvil).
- **Death and respawn** (CR 024): the downed screen says what did it and where/when you come back: "Downed / by a Bandit Brute / Back on your feet at Draedmore in 3 s / You keep everything you carry. Touch a waypoint to come back there next time." (`client/src/combat/{downed.ts,DownedOverlay.tsx}`: the killer from Event 203's target among the drawn entities; the place from the last waypoint, else Event 608's name, else the starting town). You lose nothing (spec 8: full health, mana, draughts, gear). After the respawn, for 5 s or until you land a hit, enemies don't target or hurt you and no PvP damage lands (claims refused, summoned allies too); a line says so.
- **What's new** (`client/src/ui/whatsnew`, README there): `changelog.json`, player-facing, newest first (9 entries: every playtest 2 change: fixes, world, skill tree, third person, PvP/duels, spells/dashes/shared visuals, content, graphics, onboarding). After login (once the arrival cards are done) a browser sees only the entries above the id it last closed the panel on (localStorage `lodestone_whatsnew_seen`); a first-time browser gets a three-line welcome instead (a browser with older Lodestone settings but no marker played before the panel existed and gets the list); Esc > What's new shows everything. Add an entry per deploy: `node scripts/whatsnew.mjs add --id <id> --title "..." "line" ...` (`check`, `list`). Fail-safe: invalid JSON stops the build (nothing ships); a malformed entry is dropped at runtime (never thrown) and fails `check` and the vitest; the panel sits in an error boundary.
- **Private stack for playtests**: `node tools/playtest/stack.mjs [--zone-env K=V ...]` builds the services from the checkout on random ports with a scratch database, starts Vite and prints `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL`; stops on a stop file / timeout and drops the database.

Evidence (`tools/playtest/sp9.mjs` on that stack with `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV_SPAWN=1 LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV_COMBAT=1 LODESTONE_ZONE_DEBUG_CAMP=2620,2273,8,brute brute brute`, after merging main `c4e2596`; 0 issues, `docs/evidence/pt2-onboarding-sp9.json`): the welcome on a new browser (`pt2-onboarding-whatsnew-welcome.png`), the look tip after it, Controls (34 rows, fits 1600x900), Esc closes inventory/map/journal/character/sound/friends without opening the menu, Sound and Graphics from the menu (`-esc-menu.png`, `-esc-graphics.png`), the starter quest tracked; a browser that last saw `pt2-world` gets the 7 newer entries only and nothing after closing (`-whatsnew-since.png`), the menu lists all 9; rejoined 700 m out next to the brutes: downed in ~4 s, "by a Bandit Brute", "at Draedmore in 3 s" (`-downed.png`), respawned 700 m away at full health with the grace line, gone after 5 s (`-respawned.png`). `sp1.mjs`: no HUD overlap, no stuck panel (its Esc step now only closes a panel that is open).
Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/game/respawn_test.go` (downed event names the brute and the wait, respawn point, full health/mana, draughts kept, brutes at the respawn point leave the player alone for the grace and fight after; a landed hit ends it), `meadows_test.go` (quests and game: the whole first quest in process, saved count, hand-off, old characters); vitest `combat/downed.test.ts`, `ui/whatsnew/news.test.ts` (incl. the shipped changelog), `ui/menu/menu.test.ts`, `ui/tips/tips.test.ts`.
Gaps: the welcome/returning guess for a browser with no marker uses older Lodestone localStorage keys (a cleared browser looks new); the changelog is per browser, not per account; the grace is not shown to other players (no protocol change); sp2 (the old Ward-first quest walk) predates the starter quest and was not re-run.
Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/combat/{ai,player,pvp,validate,summon}.go` (grace), `server/zone/internal/game/{quests,content}.go` (one kill signal), `server/zone/internal/quests/quests.go` (`killCounts` R 0), `contracts/constants.yaml` (+ generated), `client/src/combat/{runtime,store,CombatHud}.ts(x)`, `client/src/ui/map/store.ts` (`respawnName`), `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `client/src/main.tsx`, `client/src/hud/Overlays.tsx`, `tools/playtest/sp1.mjs`, this section.

## Playtest 2: enemy models + animation ✅ (branch `pt2/enemy-models`)

Playtest: every enemy was a capsule body + sphere head doll (slimes read as green pillars) and damage numbers were huge white blocky text. Art direction: Valheim / Breath of the Wild inspired, low-poly, characterful, readable silhouettes, original designs. Docs: `client/src/combat/README.md` "Enemy models". **No contract, protocol or server change**: everything animates from the interpolated snapshot (position, yaw, speed, health, the casting bit) and Events already on the wire (204 telegraph incl. its attack id and active ms, 200 / HitResult, 201, 206, 207).
