- **Models** (`client/src/combat/enemies/`): a procedural, flat-shaded, vertex-painted model for every enemy in combat.yaml (35 + the CR 023 content creatures: Glintfox, gloamhound, the Huntsman, Starfall Sentinel, Thornhide Behemoth, Vault Keeper) and the three CR 020 summons, on six rigs: **slime** (translucent jelly dewdrop with a visible nucleus, big eyes, a moss cap and sprout for the Mossback), **quadruped** (boar with tusks and bristle ridge, wolf, woolly ram with curled horns, ice-spined Rime Stalker, low mossy Bog Lurker, Glintfox with its glinting tail, collared gloamhounds, thorn-backed Behemoth), **crawler** (scorpion scuttler with pincers and sting, glowing-eyed cave spider, bloated void mite), **biped** on the player avatar's skeleton (`net/avatar.ts` `AVATAR_RIG`) with weapons (hooded cutthroat with a sword, archer with cloak, quiver and bow, bearded brute with a club, antlered Huntsman with a spear; stone sentinel, starfall sentinel, mud and crystal golems; ember imp; eight hunched, horned, glowing-eyed demons and the Starless Sovereign with crown, cape and cleaver; the Warden with a hammer and the Vault Keeper with a keystaff as boss set pieces), **floater** (arcane wisp, frost mote, spark wisp, hooded shade and Rime Wraith with an ice lance, the robed Archon with a halo and orbiting orbs, the Sunlit Sentinel turret) and **winged** (gale harpy, gloom bat, lumen moth). The contract colour is the body, the element the accent and eyes; elites are 1.2x; summons are **spectral** (translucent, glowing, in their element colour). Elite / element / boss aura rings stay (instanced).
- **Animation** (`enemies/anim.ts` state machine + `enemies/pose.ts` per-rig poses, pure and allocation-free): locomotion cycles locked to distance travelled (trot, tripod gait, biped walk, slime hops with squash-and-stretch and a jiggle on landing, wing flaps), lean into turns, idle breathing, **head tracking** toward you; **wind-ups** from Event 204 (the attack decides the pose: bite, swipe, slam, punch, stomp, charge with a hoof scrape, pounce / leap arcs, bow draw, spear throw, cast, nova, sting, spit, dive) with a tremble and a warm rim pulse at the end; the casting bit alone starts the default attack (missed or out-of-range telegraphs) and an early drop of the bit is an interrupted wind-up (stagger); **strike** for the event's active ms, then recovery; **hits** flash (real time, through hitstop) and flinch (heavier for heavy moves); **staggers** from stuns, knockback and parries (207); freeze / petrify **hold** the pose; **deaths**: slimes splat into a puddle with jelly droplets, beasts roll onto their side, bipeds buckle and fall back, crawlers flip, flyers drop from their hover, floaters sink into motes, golems and the bosses shatter, demons break up with embers and smoke (blocky dissolve).
- **Damage numbers** (`client/src/combat/numbers/`): constant pixel size (19 px; crits 28 px with a 1.75x pop and a "!"), coloured by the hit's element (your spell's element or weapon gem; physical white, crits gold, resist greyed, taken red, heals green), rise and fade, stacked in screen space so a burst never overlaps; one instanced draw from a glyph atlas (no canvas texture per number any more). Event 200 numbers now show crit / weak / resist from its flags.
- **Performance** (`docs/evidence/pt2-enemy-models-bench.json`; the enemy gallery, 1280x720, high tier with post and shadows, uncapped, a wandering crowd of every family with telegraphs and hits): one InstancedMesh per (part, material) for every enemy of a variant, per-enemy frustum culling, LOD full rig < 38 m (at most 48 full rigs: the radius shrinks under a crowd), the merged rest pose moved by the body joint < 95 m, one shared tinted box beyond; no per-frame allocation. **200 enemies: 149 -> 191 fps, p50 6.2 -> 5.3 ms, p99 12.0 -> 8.4 ms, scene draw calls 1,147 -> 651. 2,000 enemies: 15.5 -> 169 fps, p50 62 -> 5.8 ms, p99 97.5 -> 8.6 ms, draw calls 10,656 -> 631.** Enemy layer CPU p50 0.6 ms at 200, 2.8 ms at 2,000 (animators + matrices). Every program (opaque, jelly, spectral, their instance-colour variants, bars, rings, number glyphs) is in the WP19b warm-up (`enemies/warm.ts` via `world/warmExtras.ts`).
- **Graphics styles** (pt2/graphics, merged): the enemy materials are patched MeshStandardMaterials, so the style chunks (toon bands, rim light, retro vertex snap) and the shared `lsStyle` / `lsRetro` uniforms reach them like every other lit material; `enemies/material.ts` installs the chunks before any enemy program compiles and chains the style `onBeforeCompile` hook (no extra programs: a style switch stays a uniform write). The gallery takes `?style=`.
- **What's new**: entry `pt2-enemy-models` ("Monsters with character").
- **Evidence** (`npx vite-node client/src/combat/enemies/dev/enemy-shots.ts`, the real renderer and post chain in `enemies/dev/gallery.html`): `docs/evidence/pt2-enemy-models-{slimes,beasts,crawlers,bandits,guardians,elementals,demons,bosses,summons}.jpg` (each family idle, walking, winding up, striking, hit, dying), `-before-after.jpg` and `-crowd.jpg` (200), `-numbers.jpg` (third and first person), `-styles.jpg` (the same lineup with no hooks and in each style's material hooks).
- **Tests**: `enemies/enemies.test.ts` (every contract enemy and summon -> its own style on its family's rig, family fallbacks, elites / bosses / demons / element tints, every model and elemental variant builds with finite geometry inside the triangle budget, the state machine: move / idle, telegraph -> strike -> recover, casting-bit fallback, interrupted wind-up, hit flash through hitstop, hold, death; finite poses for every rig x attack kind x state, the wind-up pose differs from idle; the renderer batches 200 slimes into < 10 draws with LOD and follows events by entity id), `numbers/layout.test.ts` (colours, sizes, pop, rise and fade, stacking without overlap).

Gaps: measured on the dev desktop GPU in the gallery (synthetic snapshots), not in a live zone fight; the rescued villagers (CR 023) are NPC-kind townsfolk drawn by `world/buildings/npcs.ts`, unchanged; summons have no telegraph, so their wind-up is the casting bit with a fixed short pose (bite / bow shot / bolt); enemy shadows do not dissolve with the body; health bars keep the old billboard style (instanced now) and, like before, take the post chain's distance fog over far ground.

Shared files touched (small): `client/src/ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`, `client/src/combat/{CombatLayer.tsx,runtime.ts,store.ts,events.ts}` (the renderer and number layer mounted; hits carry their target and element; Events 201 / 206 / 207 to the enemy animator; telegraphs decode attack id and active ms), `client/src/net/avatar.ts` (exports `AVATAR_RIG`), `client/src/world/warmExtras.ts`, this section.

## Playtest 2: camps ✅ (branch `pt2/camps`)

Nicole: "all sorts of good shit" (Valheim and Breath of the Wild). A Valheim-flavoured camp, no full base building. CR 025 (`docs/cr/025-camps.md`). One data file: `contracts/camps.yaml` → `npm run gen` → `server/pkg/campdefs` + client `CAMPS`. Migration **0026** `camp_pieces` + `items.camp_piece_id` (run `npm run migrate`), Events **1100-1107** (new range 1100-1119), Interact **90** `camp`, items `wood:firewood` and five `camp:*` kits with recipe stations `camp` (anywhere) and `workbench`. Docs: `client/src/ui/camps/README.md`, `server/zone/README.md` "Camps", `contracts/events.md`.

- **Pieces** (P: the camp menu: what each does, its kit's recipe with have/need, Craft kit, Place): **Campfire** (a cooking station; burns on firewood, 3 in-game hours from the kit, +2 h per firewood, 12 h at most; while it burns demons never rise within 14 m (+6 m), never step into the circle and leave the players inside alone; flames, a warm light, glowing embers and the positional fire loop), **Bedroll** (E: rest → **Rested** for 10 min, 17 min by a burning fire: x2 health regen, x1.5 mana regen, +25 stamina wheel; your **respawn point**: the downed screen says "Back on your feet at your bedroll"), **Storage chest** (16 slots; locked to you, or shared with your party; items move through the item journal, exactly once), **Workbench** (torches, rope, a whetstone, copper and iron arrows from ore and firewood), **Signpost** (named in a small dialog; on your and your party's map and compass as a tent).
- **Placement mode**: a ghost ahead of you snapped to the terrain, green or red with its footprint (a campfire also shows the demons' circle) and a bar saying why it is red; mouse wheel or R turns it 45 degrees, left mouse places it, right mouse or Esc cancels (Esc does not open the menu). Hold E on your own piece to pick it up (its kit back). Rules (zone-checked, mirrored by the ghost): outside settlements (+14 m), slope under 28 degrees and 0.9 m of relief, dry, not underground, no overlap with buildings, structures or pieces, 8 m from someone else's camp, one camp per character within 18 m, 6 pieces (2 campfires, 1 bedroll, 2 chests, 1 workbench, 2 signposts).
- **Firewood**: fallen branches in the wild (a `deadfall` forage patch, drawn as branches, E for 2-3) and the general store (2 gold).
- **Collision**: chests, workbenches and signposts are solid for players (the movement step, both sides) and enemies; campfires and bedrolls are walk-through. A new box overlay in the TS/Go twin after the doors, golden vectors `server/pkg/camps/testdata/camps.json` replayed bit for bit by vitest; movement and town goldens untouched.
- **Persistence and ownership**: pieces in `camp_pieces` (write-behind, keyed per piece; a placed piece's row only after its kit's removal committed, a picked-up one's deleted before the kit's grant), restored before the zone ticks; only the owner picks up, renames or locks; a camp whose owner stayed away 14 real days decays (its chest's items with it). WP16: the zone owning the ground is the authority and mirrors every change to the others (`campset`); Rested rides a handoff.
- **Network**: event driven; pieces once per session per 256 m area (~40 bytes each), changes to the sessions that have the area. Camps are off in atlas-less worlds, so `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` is **identical** (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`).

Tests: Go `server/pkg/camps/camps_test.go` (every placement rule: reach, town margin, slope, water, structures, caves, overlap, someone else's camp, the camp's area, kind and total limits; footprint overlap in 45 degree steps; firewood fuel and its cap; decay; solid pieces push, non-solid don't, the wrapper passes through; the golden vectors `testdata/camps.json`), in-process on the official world `internal/game/camps_test.go` (dev kits; refused out of reach and in a town; a campfire and a chest placed and stored; overlap, a third campfire, someone else's pickup, a locked chest and a signpost by someone else's camp refused; the area baseline for a newcomer; chest put/take, not picked up while full, shared with the party and emptied by the party member; pick up refunds the kit and deletes the row; a restart restores the pieces; the fire: demons don't rise within the circle + margin, can't step in, players inside sheltered, burnt out = no protection, relit with firewood; Rested by the fire: duration, stamina wheel, regen, respawn beside the bedroll, the end; a chest pushes the body out in the frame ground and blocks enemies; decay never while the owner is online, then after `decay.days`), `camps_store_test.go` (Postgres: every field round trip, `owner_seen_at` never moves back, a chest item loaded with its piece, never restored as a pickup, taken once). Vitest `client/src/ui/camps/camps.test.ts` (the vectors bit for bit, the camp ground, the placement preview's rules, ghost spot and facing, wire decoders and packing, the store). Full suites after merging main: gen:check, `npm run migrate` (applied 0026 on the dev DB), go vet and go test for every Go module (all packages ok; `TestHandoffAcrossBorderKeepsPredictionAndIdentity` failed once while eslint/typecheck ran beside it and passed 3/3 alone and in the post-merge run), client typecheck, `eslint .`, `npx vitest run` (69 files, 662 tests), contracts and worldgen vitest, test:golden PASS (wp01 evidence restored), `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` identical.

Evidence (`cd client && npx vite-node src/ui/camps/dev/camp-shots.ts`: the real client and zone on the official world at dusk, one headless browser; `docs/evidence/pt2-camps-shots.txt`): `pt2-camps-menu.png` (the camp menu), `-ghost-valid.png` (a campfire ghost, green), `-ghost-invalid.png` (a workbench ghost red over the fire: "Something is in the way"), `-dusk.png` and `-dusk-third.png` (the finished camp at dusk: fire, light, "Ember Hollow" signpost, "By the fire · demons keep away"), `-rested.png` (Rested by the fire), `-storage.png` (the chest panel with firewood, a draught and pelts).

Known gaps: no repairs (items have no durability); Rested is not saved across a logout; a decayed chest's items are deleted; party members can use a shared chest and bedroll but not move pieces; local profiles cannot store items in chests (their items live in the browser's save; reason shown); names and numbers are proposals (`contracts/camps.yaml`). `npm run typecheck` fails only in `tools/creator` in a worktree (ajv resolution, as before).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/game/{server,session,doors,night,obstacles,explore,progression,items,items_store,items_journal,cluster,handoff,content}.go` (hooks), `server/zone/internal/combat/{ai,player}.go` (Rested regen), `server/zone/internal/npc/talk.go` (firewood for sale), `server/zone/{main,towns}.go`, `contracts/{items.yaml,content.yaml,events.md,codegen/gen.mjs,codegen/lib/items.mjs,ts/src/index.ts}`, client `world/core/runtime.ts` (CampGround), `net/NetPlay.tsx`, `screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `systems/NightLayer.tsx` (flame exports), `audio/{bridge,engine}.ts` (loop sources), `ui/inventory/{CraftPanel.tsx,items.ts,events.ts,store.ts}` (stations), `ui/map/{Compass,MapScreen}.tsx`, `ui/content/ContentLayer.tsx` (deadfall drawn by camps), `ui/menu/controls.ts`, `combat/downed.ts`, `ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`.

## Playtest 2: fishing, emotes, photo mode ✅ (branch `pt2/fishing-emotes-photo`)

Nicole: "all sorts of good shit" (Valheim and Breath of the Wild). Three self-contained features. CR 026 (`docs/cr/026-fishing-emotes-photo.md`). Data in CR 023's `contracts/content.yaml` (sections `fishing`, `emotes`, four feats) and `contracts/items.yaml` → `npm run gen`. Events **1080-1091** (new range 1080-1099), Interact **88** `fishing` and **89** `emote`. **No migration** (fish records ride the `character_content` jsonb). Photo mode is client only.

- **Fishing** (`server/zone/internal/content/fishing.go`, `internal/game/fishing.go`; client `client/src/ui/fishing`): a **Fishing Rod** (general stores 25 g, tool merchants 22 g, a forge recipe from a copper ingot, a camp workbench recipe from firewood and rope). With a rod in the pack, facing deep water: "[E] Cast your line" (fishing yields E to the town, cave, loot, content and camp prompts). The float lands 4-19 m out; false nibbles dip it; on the bite it goes under with a "!" and a splash ring: strike with E, Space or the left button within 1.2 s (striking on a nibble spooks the fish). The reel fight: hold E / Space / left button to reel; the tension bar reddens when the fish surges; let go before the line snaps; the float darts about and comes in as you reel; the catch card shows rarity, weight, water, "New to your journal!" / "Your biggest yet!" and the lore. Walking 3 m away reels in; an emote reels in. **14 species** (6 common, 4 uncommon, 2 rare, 2 legendary: Silverfin Minnow, Reed Perch, Speckled Trout, Mudback Carp, Tidewing Mackerel, Sandflat Flounder, Bog Pike, Frostgill Char, Emberscale Loach, Gloam Eel, Stormray, Moonlit Koi, Humming Sturgeon, Deepmaw) by water (lake, river, sea: the atlas node flags), biome, hour (day, night, dawn, dusk) and weather (rain, clear). **13 hearth recipes** (Silverfin Skewer to Deepmaw Banquet, heal 28-240). Journal (J) **Fish** tab: every species; caught ones with count, biggest, size range and lore; uncaught ones say where and when to look. Feats **Hooked**, **Angler** (50, title), **Master of the Waters** (all 14, title), **Tall Tale** (a legendary, title). Others within 48 m see the angler's pose with a rod in hand, the line and the float, and the catch held up (the emote event, ids 20 and 21); in first person a rod is held in front of the camera (the weapon view model steps aside).
- **Server authority**: the zone rolls the catch at the cast (seed: world seed, character, the character's persisted cast count, the bobber's 4 m cell; the species weighted by rarity among those that bite there and then; weight skewed small; the bite time; nibbles; the fight's seed) and keeps the species until it is landed. A hook before the bite spooks the fish; after the window + 700 ms it is missed. A landing sooner than the fastest possible fight (1250 ms x the species' stamina, by the zone's clock and by the client's fight ms) loses the fish. Casts: 1.5 s cooldown after every outcome, a burst of 6 then one per 4 s. The fish goes into the inventory (persisted like any item), the codex (`content.State.Fish`) and the feats. Dev: `LODESTONE_ZONE_FISH_FAST=1` (bites after ~1.5 s), Interact 88/9 gives a rod (dev builds).
- **Reel minigame** (`client/src/ui/fishing/minigame.ts`, pure, 60 Hz, seeded): reeling eases the tension toward 0.25 + 1.1 x pull (a strong surge drives it past the top; 0.4 s in the red snaps the line) and gains 0.6/stamina per second against a calm fish; letting go eases the tension and gives a little line back; 90 s at most. Tuned so a player who watches the bar lands everything (legendaries 16/20+ in the test, 20-50 s), holding through every surge snaps rares and legendaries, and never reeling lets the fish go.
- **Emotes** (`server/zone/internal/game/emotes.go`; client `client/src/ui/emotes`): **hold H** for the wheel (move the mouse toward one and release; a tap replays the last; with a free cursor the names are buttons) or `/emote <name>` / `/e` in chat (`/emote` lists them, `/emote stop`): **wave, bow, cheer, point, sit, dance, laugh, shrug, salute, rest by the fire**. Posed on the avatar rig (`net/avatarAnim.ts` `applyEmote`: whole body, blended in and out; sit, dance and rest loop until the body moves; an attack ends an emote; `followEmote` drives your body and everyone's from one record). Event 1090 to players within 48 m (not the actor); a looping emote is told once more to anyone who comes within range while it lasts; a token bucket per player (3, then one per 1.5 s; the client keeps the same limit). First person: a camera nod (a bow dips the view, a laugh shakes it, a shrug tilts it, sit and rest lower the eye until you move) and a hand in the corner of the view (wave, salute, point, cheer).
- **Photo mode** (`client/src/ui/photo`; **L**, since P is the camp menu): the camera leaves your character (fly with WASD, Space or E up, Ctrl or Q down, Shift fast, Alt slow, Z / C roll, the wheel zooms) within 30 m of your eye and never under the terrain or water (8 m in a cave); your character stands still (the zone sees an idle player) and its body shows; the HUD hides (one CSS rule over everything but the game canvas and the panel). Panel: visual style (restored on leaving), a time-of-day preview 0-24 h or Live (the light only: `WorldScene` lights from the preview hour; the world clock and the zone are untouched), depth of field with auto focus (the view's centre on the terrain) or a set distance, vignette, grain, field of view, nameplates on or off; Enter or the button saves a PNG of the frame (no HUD) right after it renders; H hides the panel; Esc frees the mouse, Esc again (or L) leaves. The pass (`world/post/photoEffect.ts`: a 24-tap depth-weighted disc blur, vignette, grain) is its own EffectPass at the end of the chain, enabled only in photo mode, compiled with the shader warm-up.
- **Keys** (all in the Esc menu's Controls, checked by menu.test.ts): E fish (cast, strike, hold to reel), H emote wheel, L photo mode (Enter shot, H panel, Esc leave).
- **Network**: event driven, nothing per tick; an emote is one Event per player within 48 m; a line or a loop costs a distance check per player once a second, only for the players holding one; fishing sends 3-4 Events to the angler per cast. The scale scenario's bots never fish or emote: `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` **identical** after merging main (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`).

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/content/fishing_test.go` (rolls deterministic per seed and the seed depends on world, character, cast and cell; species only where and when they bite; every species reachable; weights, bite times and nibbles in range; codex records, best weights, feats, JSON round trip; the fastest fight), `internal/game/fishing_test.go` (in process on a pond: no rod, dry land, out of reach, a cast into a lake, the near observer sees the line and the far one doesn't, a second cast refused, an instant landing refused, an honest fight landed into the inventory and the catch held up for the observer; an early strike; the cooldown and the burst limiter; walking away), `internal/game/emotes_test.go` (interest radius: near yes, 100 m no, never the actor; unknown and zone-only ids refused; the burst, refusals, a refill; a loop told once to a newcomer, the stop to everyone who saw it). Vitest `ui/fishing/fishing.test.ts` (minigame determinism, a bar-watching angler lands every species never faster than the zone allows, perfect reeling is exactly the fastest fight, holding through surges snaps strong fish, never reeling lets it go, the red-zone grace; the cast point; the packed cast; the state machine from cast to catch, a missed bite, walking off), `ui/emotes/emotes.test.ts` (the wheel's sectors, the rate limit, /emote parsing, every pose, loops end on movement, the rod and the held fish, replicated records, the first-person nod), `ui/photo/photo.test.ts` (radius, terrain, water and cave clamps, flight, auto focus, shot names). Full suites after merging main (camps, enemy models): gen:check, go vet and go test for server/pkg and server/zone (all packages ok, the game package 339 s), client typecheck, `eslint .`, `npx vitest run` (72 files, 687 tests), whatsnew check, test:golden.

Evidence (`tools/playtest/sp11.mjs` on a private stack with `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV_SPAWN=1 LODESTONE_ZONE_FISH_FAST=1`: two characters in one headless browser at the nearest river to the spawn; `docs/evidence/pt2-fishing-emotes-photo.json`): the Angler casts into the river (`pt2-fishing-prompt.png`, `-waiting.png`, `-bite.png`: the float under and "Strike!", `-fight.png`: the tension and line bars beside the crosshair, `-caught.png`: Silverfin Minnow, "New to your journal!", held up), three fights landed by watching the bar (Silverfin Minnow, Speckled Trout x2; the fish in the inventory), `-journal.png` (the Fish tab); the Watcher 7 m away sees the rod, line and float (`-remote.png`) and the catch held up (`-remote-catch.png`). Emotes: the wheel (`pt2-emotes-wheel.png`), the Watcher's view of every emote as it arrived (`pt2-emotes-remote-{wave,bow,cheer,dance,sit,salute,rest,point,shrug}.png`, `-remote-laugh-chat.png` from `/emote laugh`; the Watcher's animator reported each id), first person (`-firstperson-salute.png`, `-firstperson-wave.png`: the hand). Photo mode: `pt2-photo-panel.png` (no HUD, the panel), `-dusk-dof.png` (a 17:24 preview while the world was at night, focus on the angler, vignette, grain), `-painterly-noon.png`, `pt2-photo-saved.png` (the PNG Enter saved, as downloaded), `-after.png` (Esc: HUD back, the style restored, no Esc menu); flying away stopped at 30.0 m and the Watcher saw the Angler stand still the whole time. 0 issues but a pageerror in two of three runs (below).

Known gaps: remote anglers' floats don't show the bite or the fight (their line and pose do); the auto focus follows the terrain, not avatars or props (set the distance by hand for a portrait); emotes don't cross a zone border (WP16 ghosts) and a handoff drops a line in the water; the account upgrade does not copy fish records (like all CR 023 content state); emotes have no sounds; the free camera does not collide with buildings or trees (terrain and water only); a rejoin while the shader warm-up runs can log `reading 'isReady'` from three's compileAsync (seen once in the evidence run, during a scripted rejoin; not from this branch's code); all names and texts are proposals for Nicole (`contracts/content.yaml`).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/game/{server,session,content}.go` (hooks, Event 1000 `fish`), `server/zone/internal/combat/atlas.go` (`NodeFlagsNear`, `SeaLevel`), `server/zone/internal/content/state.go` (`Fish`, `Landed`), `server/zone/internal/npc/talk.go` (rods for sale), `server/pkg/contentdefs/contentdefs.go`, `contracts/{content.yaml,items.yaml,events.md,codegen/lib/content.mjs,codegen/lib/items.mjs}`, client `net/{avatarAnim,avatar}.ts`, `net/RemotePlayers.tsx` (emote poses, rod and fish meshes, `liveAvatars`, nameplates hidden in photo mode), `scene/{PlayerController,LocalAvatar,GameCanvas,FirstPersonArms}.tsx`, `combat/weapons/FirstPersonWeapon.tsx` (hidden in photo mode and while holding a rod), `world/WorldScene.tsx` (time preview, photo pass uniforms), `world/post/postfx.ts` (photo pass), `state/live.ts` (`view.photo`, `view.fpProp`), `input/lookRouter.ts` (new), `social/{commands,session}.ts` (/emote), `ui/content/{JournalTabs.tsx,events.ts,store.ts}` (Fish tab), `ui/menu/controls.ts`, `screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `ui/whatsnew/changelog.json` (three entries).

## Playtest 2: mounts ✅ (branch `pt2/mounts`)

Nicole: "all sorts of good shit" (Valheim and Breath of the Wild). A summonable **spirit steed**. CR 027 (`docs/cr/027-mounts.md`). Contract: `contracts/constants.yaml` group `mounts` (version 6), SelfState flags **bit 3 mounted** (protocol.yaml doc; no version change), player typeId **bit 14 riding**, Events **1140-1143** (new range 1140-1159), Interact **110** `steed`, item `tool:spirit_whistle`, migration **0028** `character_mounts` (run `npm run migrate`), local saves `mounts`. Docs: `client/src/ui/mounts/README.md`, `client/src/sim/README.md` "Riding", `server/zone/README.md` "Mounts", `contracts/events.md`.

- **Getting one** (first hour): a **Spirit Whistle** at every general store (40 gold; new characters carry 20). **Key 5** whistles (every letter is bound: H/L are emotes/photo mode); the first call bonds a steed to the character (persisted), every call brings it: it **gallops in** from 26 m behind you over 2.4 s (a dry spot it could stand on), or **materializes** in a fade of light where there is none. **E** beside it rides, **E** again (or **Space** standing still) gets off; it stands beside you, fades after 10 min alone. **Character (C) → Your steed**: name it, six colours (Moonmist, Ember, Verdant, Dusk, Frost, Shade), whistle, send away.
- **Riding is the shared movement step** (`client/src/sim/ride.ts` = `server/pkg/movement/ride.go`, bit-exact): the zone alone sets the mounted flag, the step only clears it. **W trots** (8.5 m/s), a **Shift press spurs** (BotW: 18 stamina per spur, 5 on a 100 wheel; +4.5 m/s at once, gait up: walk → trot → gallop 13.5; at a gallop a burst up to 19 that fades over ~1.3 s), the spur pips sit under the crosshair; W/A/D steer toward the camera at a limited **turn rate** (150/110/75 °/s by gait, literal cos/sin per step; pivots below 1 m/s); **S** reins in to a walk, brakes, then backs up. **Slopes**: walkable to 35°, steeper uphill is a wall (never climbs; 0.5 m lips stepped), past 45° it slides; **water**: refuses deeper than 1.2 m, wades slower; **Space** jumps fences (1.36 m); a fall over ~8 m or into deep water **throws** you; no dashes in the saddle. **Collision**: your own push-out plus the steed's chest 0.9 m ahead against the same colliders (buildings, props, camp pieces, caves).
- **Combat**: bows, staves, wands and spells from the saddle; swords and spears can't swing, parry or charge, no dodges, dashes or forms (client timeline and the zone's `mountedFrame` agree). A hit that costs health throws you 25 % of the time (going down always does). At night a demon within 12 m **spooks** the steed (35 %/s): you are thrown, it bolts, 20 s before it answers again. Fast travel / respawn leaves it behind.
- **Validation**: a mounted step is bounded by the spur cap; a ground step **without** a mount or dash can't gain more than 1 m/s over its last speed beyond sprint (12.8), so a gallop on foot is a violation (the flag is the zone's alone).
- **Visuals**: a low-poly translucent glowing horse (`ui/mounts/model.ts`): four-beat walk, diagonal trot, rotary gallop phase-locked to speed, body bob and rock, head nod, rippling mane, streaming tail, saddle, reins to the rider's hands, an aura. Riders sit on the avatar rig (`avatarAnim.ts` seated pose: hips on the saddle, thighs around the barrel, hands on the reins, a lean into the gallop; the look turns the torso; facing the drawn motion, no false dodge on a spur). In the saddle the eye rides 0.95 m higher, the third-person boom pulls back 1.6 m, a gallop kicks the FOV (+5°, +9° on a burst; no speed lines); first person shows the neck and ears.
- **Wire**: typeId bit 14 flips on mount/dismount only; Event 1141 tells players within 140 m about a steed (on change, and newcomers every 2 s; nothing while nobody has one out); 1140 at join only for owners and whistle holders. `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` **identical** to main (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`); `BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000` 86.8-88.9 vs 87.1-88.4 M cycles/tick, 134.0-134.8 vs 133.8-134.5 kbps/client (`docs/evidence/pt2-mounts-bench.txt`).
- **Goldens** (deliberately added; existing ones byte-identical): `client/src/sim/golden/movement_pt2_ride_{flat,plane,course_east,course_west}.json` (format 5): gaits, spurs, turns, brake, back up, jump, a dodge that does nothing, hop off; a course with a fence, a building wall, a hill, a 10 m drop that throws, a ford, a lake it refuses, a cliff it can't climb. Go replays them at 1e-9 m with every discrete field exact.

Tests: Go `server/pkg/movement/ride_golden_test.go` (goldens, every mounted step inside the bound, gaits and spurs, never mounts itself, no dash while riding, mounted-only speeds incl. a forged on-foot gallop refused, can't climb or swim), `server/zone/internal/game/mounts_test.go` on the official world (no whistle refused; bond, arrival heard by another player, persisted; too far refused; mount → SelfState bit; a predicted gallop equals the zone's with zero violations; the typeId bit seen and cleared; melee and dodge stripped; E off, the steed stands; hits throw; fast travel leaves it; a demon at night spooks it and the cooldown refuses; name/colour through a restart). Vitest `client/src/sim/ride.test.ts`, `client/src/ui/mounts/mounts.test.ts` (store, decoders, arrival poses, name chunks, rig gaits, the seated avatar facing its motion, the timeline in the saddle, the FOV kick). Full suites after merging main (CR 026): gen:check, `npm run migrate` (applied 0028 on the dev DB), go vet and go test for every Go module (all packages ok; `TestTowerClimbRevealsRegionThenFastTravel` failed once in the full run and passed 2/2 alone), client typecheck, `eslint .`, `npx vitest run` (74 files, 705 tests), contracts and worldgen vitest, test:golden PASS (wp01 evidence restored), `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` identical. `npm run typecheck` fails only in `tools/creator` in a worktree (ajv resolution, as before).

Evidence (`cd client && npx vite-node src/ui/mounts/dev/mount-shots.ts`: the real client and zone on the official world, one headless browser with two pages; `docs/evidence/pt2-mounts-shots.txt`): `pt2-mounts-summon.png` (the steed galloping in after the whistle, third person), `-gallop-third.png` (a spur's burst at 16 m/s, boom pulled back, spur pips), `-observer.png` (another player's view: Ash seated on a galloping Ember steed, remote gait `gallop`), `-first.png` (first person in the saddle at 13.5 m/s), `-panel.png` (renamed "Misty", Ember).

Known gaps: shots leave from the 1.6 m combat eye (the aim converges onto the crosshair, so they land where you aim); no mounted melee swing; no hoof or whistle sounds yet; the steed has no health; it walks through trees and boulders (as players do); interactions reach from the raised eye; an account upgrade doesn't copy `mounts`; names, colours and numbers are proposals.

Shared files touched (small): `server/pkg/movement/movement.go` (dispatch, `finish`, `MaxHorizontalSpeed`), `client/src/sim/movement.ts` (dispatch, MoveFlag.mounted), `server/zone/internal/game/{server,session,combat,items,explore,traversal}.go` (hooks, typeId bit kept, `mountedFrame`, vertical bound), `server/zone/internal/npc/talk.go` (the whistle for sale), `server/pkg/localsave/state.go` (`Mounts`), `contracts/{constants.yaml,items.yaml,events.md,protocol.yaml}`, client `net/{avatar,avatarAnim}.ts`, `net/{NetPlay,RemotePlayers}.tsx`, `scene/{PlayerController,LocalAvatar}.tsx`, `state/live.ts` (`mounted`, `gait`), `combat/{timeline,dashFeel}.ts`, `ui/fishing/FishingHud.tsx` (no cast in the saddle), `ui/progression/ProgressionHud.tsx` (Your steed), `ui/menu/controls.ts` (Riding), `screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `client/src/sim/golden/{generate,io}.ts`, `ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`.

## Playtest 2: instruments ✅ (branch `pt2/instruments`)

Nicole (a musician): "all sorts of good shit": instruments you actually play, heard by the people near you. CR 028 (`docs/cr/028-instruments.md`). Contract: `contracts/content.yaml` section `instruments` (radius, batching and rate limits, songs, applause, fireside, jam, four instruments, wares), emote **22** `playing` (system), feats `bard`, `troubadour`, `crowd_pleaser`, `fireside_tales` (stats `notes`, `ovations`, `firesongs`), items `instrument:{lute,wooden_flute,hand_drum,handbells}` (category `instrument`) and four recipes, Events **1120-1123** (new range 1120-1139), Interact **120** `music`. No migration.

- **Getting one**: general stores sell the lute (60 g), the wooden flute (24 g) and the hand drum (36 g); tool merchants sell the handbells (80 g) and a flute. A flute, a drum and a lute are made at a camp workbench, handbells at a forge. The item card has **Play (U)**.
- **Play mode** (`client/src/ui/music/InstrumentHud.tsx`): **U** picks up the instrument you carry (U still declines a pending duel). The keyboard is four rows of a scale: **Q W E R…** from the root, the **number row** an octave up, **A S D…** an octave down, **Z X C…** two down. **Shift** raises a note an octave, **Space** accents it, the **mouse wheel** moves by octaves, **← →** change the key, **↑ ↓** the scale (11: major, minor, pentatonic, minor pentatonic, dorian, mixolydian, phrygian, lydian, harmonic minor, blues, chromatic), **Tab** the instrument, **Enter** the metronome, **Esc** puts it away. The overlay names every key's note (the drum's bass, tone and slap), with roots in gold and held keys lit; key, scale, octave, dynamics and tempo are also clickable and remembered per browser. While playing, the body stands still and no other key, click or wheel reaches the game; the handler is installed first, like photo mode's.
- **Sound** (`ui/music/voices.ts`, `synth.ts`), all synthesized, with no sample files: Nicole's libraries are Novation genre loop packs and an encrypted Kontakt piano, none of them single notes. The **lute** is Karplus-Strong with all-pass tuning (within 12 cents in the tests), a double course and a body resonance. The **flute** is a breathy near-sine with a chiff, a scoop and delayed vibrato, held while the key is down. The **hand drum** is membrane modes with a strike pitch drop; the register picks bass, tone or slap, and the note tunes the skin. The **handbells** are tuned partials (fundamental and twelfth) beating as pairs, with a clapper. Struck notes are rendered once and cached (two variants per note), pre-rendered in idle slices when you pick the instrument up; the sound is a lazy chunk.
- **Hearing others** (`ui/music/audio.ts`): your notes sound at once. Others' notes are positional at their bodies through the audio system's falloff, air, occlusion and reverb send (caves and closed buildings ring), via a jitter buffer that keeps their rhythm. Voices are capped, and the score ducks while instruments play within 24 m (`audio/acoustics.ts` duckLevels `instrument`).
- **Wire**: notes are 16-bit events (MIDI note, velocity, ms offset). The first note after a pause goes at once; the rest are batched, five per Interact 120/2, at most one message per 50 ms, only while playing. Interact 120 has **its own message bucket** (12, then 25/s; the generic Interact limiter of 10, then 5/s would drop a melody and count violations). The zone checks the instrument and range, applies a notes bucket (40, then 16/s) and relays one Event 1120 per batch to players **within 40 m** only. Nothing is sent per tick, and **not while mounted** (CR 027's riding bit). The scale bots never play: `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` **identical** to main (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`).
- **Bodies**: emote 22 poses the avatar: the lute across the belly with the right hand strumming, the flute at the lips, the drum at the waist with alternating hands, a handbell in each hand. Each note moves the hands. Props live in `net/avatar.ts` and poses in `net/avatarAnim.ts`; `followMusic` drives them from note hits. **First person** (`ui/music/FirstPersonInstrument.tsx`) holds the instrument up with its own hands; the weapon and arms step aside (`view.fpInstrument`).
- **Hooks**: a finished song (16+ notes over 8+ s, then 4 s of silence or Esc) is applauded by up to 6 **townsfolk within 20 m**, who cheer or clap (emote 3 on the NPC; `world/buildings/npcs.ts` animates their arms), once per 45 s. A **fireside song** (30 s of playing at night within 8 m of a burning campfire) makes you and everyone by the fire **Rested** with the fire's bonus. **Feats**: Bard (100 notes), Troubadour (2,000), Crowd Pleaser (5 ovations), Fireside Tales. **Jam**: Enter sets a shared metronome. Players playing within 40 m get the same downbeat on the server clock (Event 1123), so every click lines up; a beat dot pulses in the overlay.

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/game/music_test.go`: the note codec (the TS layout); relay within 40 m and not at 60 m, no echo, the pose to listeners; refusals (no instrument, not playing, unknown) and out-of-range notes dropped; the notes bucket (the burst, one "too fast"); 30 batches at 20/s all relayed past the generic Interact limiter; the Bard feat at 100 notes; a song's end, applause by the townsperson within 20 m (not the one at 30 m), the pose ending for listeners, no second ovation within 45 s; the jam to near players and a late joiner (the same downbeat) but not far ones, and its end; fireside Rested for the player and a listener by the fire, not one 12 m away, once per song; ending with the pose; not from the saddle. Vitest `client/src/ui/music/music.test.ts` (19): scales and rows, Shift, note names in sharp and flat keys, range folding, default octaves, velocity; the 16-bit codec, batch packing and clock wrap, the batcher (first note at once, the window, five per batch, at most 20/s), the jitter buffer (spacing under jitter, reset after a pause, wrap); the pluck's pitch at four notes, its decay and headroom, drum strokes and tuning, bell partials and pitch, the flute's envelope and brightness; note hits in time order, the playing pose and a note moving the hands for every instrument, the townsfolk's applause arms. Also `audio/acoustics.test.ts` (the instrument duck). Full suites after merging main: gen:check, go vet and go test for server/pkg and server/zone (all ok; `TestG4LoopForgeSunsteelBlade` was short of ore once under load and passed alone), client typecheck (all workspaces), `eslint .`, client vitest (75 files, 724 tests), contracts vitest (52), whatsnew check.

Evidence (`node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV_SPAWN=1`, then `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=… node tools/playtest/sp12.mjs`: a private stack after merging main, one headless browser with two pages, Draedmore's square at 10:30; `docs/evidence/pt2-instruments.json`). `pt2-instruments-overlay-{lute,wooden_flute,hand_drum,handbells}.jpg` show the overlay mid-phrase on the real keyboard, with the keys lit. `-remote-*.jpg` show the listener 3.4 m away seeing each playing pose; that listener's audio held a positional source with 3-9 voices. `-firstperson-*.jpg` show the instrument held up. `-applause.jpg` shows six townsfolk applauding a 102-note, 31 s song. `-jam.jpg` shows the listener on the flute, at the bard's 96 bpm. Each instrument's phrase was rendered offline in the page (OfflineAudioContext, 44.1 kHz stereo 16-bit) to `pt2-instruments-{lute,flute,drum,handbells}.wav`, plus `-lute-cave.wav` through a 10 m cave's reverb (peaks -15.8 to -4.7 dBFS, 6-7 s each).

Known gaps: notes are relayed as played, so a hostile client can play any note in range up to the rate (no worse than chat). A zone border (WP16) isn't heard across, and a handoff picks the instrument up again once. Remote notes arrive about 45 ms + the path's latency late. The townsfolk applaud with their single-box arms. Names, numbers and tunings are proposals (`contracts/content.yaml instruments`).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/game/{server,session,content}.go` (tick, leave, the Interact 120 bucket and dispatch, instrument wares), `client/src/audio/{acoustics,bridge,engine}.ts` (the instrument duck), `client/src/net/{avatar,avatarAnim,RemotePlayers}.ts(x)`, `client/src/scene/{LocalAvatar,GameCanvas,FirstPersonArms}.tsx`, `client/src/combat/weapons/FirstPersonWeapon.tsx`, `client/src/state/live.ts` (`fpInstrument`), `client/src/world/buildings/npcs.ts` (applause), `client/src/ui/inventory/{InventoryScreen,ItemIcon}.tsx`, `client/src/ui/menu/controls.ts`, `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `contracts/{items.yaml,content.yaml,events.md,codegen/lib/{items,content}.mjs}`, `server/pkg/contentdefs/contentdefs.go`, `client/src/ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`.

## Playtest 2: pets ✅ (branch `pt2/pets`)

Nicole: "all sorts of good shit" (Valheim and Breath of the Wild). **Taming and pet companions.** CR 029 (`docs/cr/029-pets.md`). Data: `contracts/content.yaml` section `taming` (9 species, 9 abilities, every number), four feats, emote 25 → `npm run gen`. Events **1200-1205** (new range 1200-1219), Interact **130** `pet`, migration **0029** `character_pets` (run `npm run migrate`), local saves `pets`, a handoff field `pets`. Docs: `client/src/ui/pets/README.md`, `server/zone/README.md` "Pets", `contracts/events.md` (incl. "Pets" entities).

- **Taming** (Valheim feel, server-authoritative): carry a food a creature likes and weaken it below half its health: it stops fighting and sniffs the air (30 s; a "?" over it); **E** feeds it (the favourite first: faster and likelier) and the **taming meter** fills (3-8 s by species) while you stay within 8 m and nothing hurts it or you (walk off or get hit and it startles and turns on you). Then the zone's deterministic roll (world seed, character, the character's roll count, the creature): tamed, or it snatches the food and fights on. **Shiny** (3 %: gold, +10 %) and **elemental** (8 %: the region's element) variants. Tameable: Dewdrop and Mossback slimes, Thornback boar, Mossjaw wolf, Crag ram, Rime stalker, Bog lurker, Dust scuttler and the Glintfox (never demons or bosses; foods from foraging, fishing, cooking and the stores).
- **Pets** (on the CR 020 summon machinery, but persistent): one follows you (the rest wait in your **stable**: within 24 m of one of your camp pieces or in any town), joins fights that are already on (never starts one), bites, uses its **ability** (Soothing Ooze and Mossbloom heal you / your party, Rallying Howl +15 % damage, Tusk Charge, Headlong, Frost Breath, Mire, Venom Sting, Dazzle), **levels** from the creatures it helps defeat (to 20; +12 % health, +8 % bite a level), regenerates out of a fight, catches up at once after a fast travel. Enemies turn on it when its bites out-threat you and their strikes and shots hit it; at 0 health it is **knocked out**, limps home to recover (2.5 min), then comes back to you.
- **Keys** (every letter is bound; U is CR 028's instruments): **6** tapped sends your pet at what the crosshair is on (nothing: heel); **6** held: the pet wheel (attack, follow, stay, come here, pet, feed); **7** stay / follow; **E** feeds a calm creature, else pets your pet (an emote others see, hearts). They stand aside while you play an instrument. All in the Esc menu's Controls ("Taming and pets"; menu.test.ts checks them).
- **Client**: pets are the enemy models (`combat/enemies/spec.ts` `petSpec`) with a friendlier tint, no glowing eyes, a red collar and a gold tag (`models.ts` `collar`; gold for a shiny, a ribbon on a slime), a green health bar and a warm "Owner's Name" nameplate; the prompts, the meter, a pet card (name, level, health, follow/stay), the wheel, in-world moments (`PetLayer`: the "?", hearts, the tame's burst, ability rings), the **pet panel** (Character (C) → Your pets: every pet's level, variant, health, bite, XP, kills, favourite food, ability; rename, take out / stable at a stable, feed, stay, release; how to tame), the bestiary says which creatures are tameable, with what, and their ability. Feats: **Beast Friend**, **Keeper of Beasts** (all 9), **Glimmerfriend** (a shiny), **Packleader** (a pet at level 10). What's new: `pt2-pets`.
- **Network**: a pet is an ordinary snapshot entity (EntityKind effect, typeId bit 15; one per player with one out); Events go to owners, tamers and players within 48 m. Taming is off in atlas-less worlds and bots own no pets: `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` **identical** after merging main (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`). `BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000` (4 runs each): 86.2-88.5 vs 86.9-89.0 M cycles/tick, 133.8-134.7 vs 134.0-134.6 kbps/client, p99 3.8-4.2 vs 4.0-4.4 ms: noise (`docs/evidence/pt2-pets-bench.txt`).

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/content/pets_test.go` (the roll deterministic and moved by world, character and roll count; odds and variants over 20,000 rolls; elemental takes the region's element; favourite faster and likelier; stats, XP to the cap, the stable's size, ids, release, JSON; the tamed feats), `internal/combat/pets_test.go` (follow and warp, one per owner, gone with the owner; never starts a fight; joins one, bites it down, earns the XP; the creature turns on it and knocks it out; stay; an attack order; howl, mend, the charge's stun; a weakened tameable reported once, a calm creature never moves or strikes, pets leave it alone, a hit breaks the calm, Tame without XP, a calm runs out), `internal/game/pets_test.go` on the official world (no food no calm; a dev-weakened slime calms for its food's carrier; out of reach refused; meter and roll; the pet in the snapshot with the pet bit and "Ash's ..."; saved; a walked-away meter interrupted, the wolf turns on you; stay / follow; no stable in the wild; knockout home and back only after the recovery; the stable in town; rename saved; a restart brings it out; the handoff field), `pets_store_test.go` (Postgres: round trip, epoch fence, missing row). Vitest `client/src/ui/pets/pets.test.ts` (decoders and reducer from calm to tamed / interrupted / failed, notes, acts; the numbers match content.yaml; name chunks; the crosshair pick; every species as a pet with a collar, plain and shiny, within budget). Full suites after merging main (instruments, CR 028): gen:check, `npm run migrate` (applied 0029 on the dev DB), go vet and go test for every Go module (all ok, the game package 392 s), client typecheck (`npm run typecheck` fails only in `tools/creator` in a worktree: ajv, as before), `eslint .`, `npx vitest run` (76 files, 732 tests), contracts and worldgen vitest, whatsnew check, test:golden PASS (wp01 evidence restored), `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` identical.

Evidence (`cd client && npx vite-node src/ui/pets/dev/pet-shots.ts`: the real client and zone on the official world, one headless browser, a dev-weakened wolf and a dev bandit camp; `docs/evidence/pt2-pets-shots.txt`): `pt2-pets-calm.png` (the calm wolf, "?" over it, "[E] Feed Raw Meat to the mossjaw wolf · its favourite!"), `-taming.png` (the meter at 62 %, hearts), `-tamed.png` (the burst, Beast Friend), `-follow.png` (Fang trotting after you), `-fight.png` (Fang biting a bandit beside you, sent there with 6), `-wheel.png` (the pet wheel), `-panel.png` (the pet panel at level 5: Rallying Howl, favourite food, XP).

Known gaps: pets never fight players and players can't hurt them; pets take no statuses; one pet out, no saddles or riding pets; pets walk through trees and boulders (as enemies do) and follow into caves by warping; no pet sounds; the account upgrade doesn't copy `pets`; names and numbers are proposals (`contracts/content.yaml taming`).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/combat/{ai,enemy,par,player,summon,validate,world}.go` (hooks: pet targeting `chasePet` / `windupAt`, strikes and shots on pets, the calm in `think`, weakened and calm-break in `applyDamage`, pet XP in `kill`, the howl in `damageOf`, `stepPets`), `server/zone/internal/content/state.go` (`TamedKinds`, stats), `server/zone/internal/game/{server,session,combat,content,explore}.go` (tick, join, leave, Interact 130, the pet's nameplate, feat stat `petlevel`, local saves), `server/pkg/{contentdefs/contentdefs.go,localsave/state.go}`, `contracts/{content.yaml,events.md,codegen/lib/content.mjs}`, client `combat/{CombatLayer.tsx,enemies/{spec,models,renderer}.ts}`, `net/{NetPlay.tsx,avatarAnim.ts}`, `screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `ui/{content/JournalTabs.tsx,menu/controls.ts,progression/ProgressionHud.tsx,whatsnew/changelog.json}`.

## Playtest 2: weather ✅ (branch `pt2/weather`)

Nicole, overnight: "all sorts of good shit" (Valheim and Breath of the Wild). Dynamic weather, deterministic and shared: every client and the zone compute it from the seed, nothing per tick on the wire. CR 030 (`docs/cr/030-weather.md`). Contract: `contracts/constants.yaml` v7 group `weather`, Events 1220-1222, one byte on Event 500.

- **States by biome and region** (`server/pkg/weather/states.go` = `client/src/systems/weather/weather.ts`, line for line): clear, cloudy, windy, fog, drizzle, rain, thunderstorm, snow, snowstorm, heat haze (09:00-18:00), sandstorm. 8 x 8 weather cells of 512 m. Each cell's climate is voted from the atlas biome grid (official world: 38 temperate, 8 wet, 10 highland, 3 cold, 5 arid). Fronts of 2 x 2 cells keep a severity roll for 10 minutes from a seeded phase (fronts change at different moments). Each cell nudges the roll by ±7, and a climate table ordered fair to foul turns it into a state, so one front is a thunderstorm over the meadows, a snowstorm on the peaks beside them and a sandstorm in the badlands.
- **Smooth**: a change crossfades over 90 s centred on it; a neighbouring cell bleeds in over 90 m at a border; the camera eases again. Wet ground dries over ~2 min, settled snow melts over ~3 min; a fast travel arrives in the weather there.
- **HUD**: the clock pill shows the weather where you stand (icon + name) and a forecast line when it changes within 4 minutes ("Storm coming soon", "Clearing in 3 min", "Blizzard coming soon"), plus "Freezing", "Chilled" or "Warm 9m". Solo play shows the same pill.
- **Thunderstorms**: a seeded strike per storm cell every 3 s at 35 %. A bolt lands on the highest of nine points 12 m apart (peaks, towers, roofs), or jumps to a player in the open within 10 m (30 m holding a sword or spear; plus half their height above the ground). Holding metal in a storm also draws bolts of your own (6 % per 3 s): the weapon crackles 2 s ahead (Event 1221, a warning). Switch to a bow or staff, or shelter in a cave or town, and it lands 30 m off. 45 damage (armor and shields apply; it can down you), half within 4 m (players and enemies); it sets dry grass alight through the chemistry engine (rain usually douses it). **Server-authoritative**: the zone resolves only strikes a player could see and sends Event 1220 to players within 260 m. Clients draw seeded strikes farther than 200 m from them themselves (deduplicated by strike id), so a distant storm costs no bytes. Thunder follows each strike at the speed of sound (the audio QoL pass's `thunderFor`), with a crack within 450 m. The flash lights the sky, the clouds, the air and the hemisphere light.
- **Gameplay**: rain (any strength) where you climb keeps WP10's rule (half speed, slips); **snow slows walking and sprinting** (x0.9, snowstorm x0.8); **cold**: a cold-climate cell at night or a snowstorm, 20 s without warmth, makes you **Chilled** (no health regeneration, mana x0.6). Warmth is a burning campfire or a town hearth within 9 m, a settlement, a cave, Rested, or **warm food** (stews, broths, chowder, curry, skewers, pie, grilled meat: 10 min; Event 1222). **Wind** pushes a glider downwind (1.25 m/s per level above 1: 1.25 in a windy spell or a storm, 2.5 in a snowstorm or sandstorm) in the shared movement step, and drifts arrows (0.6 m/s² per level). **Fog banks, snowstorms and sandstorms** cut enemies' aggro to 60 % and visibility to ~100 m. Fishing reads rain where the bobber lands. Chemistry reads rain per burning cell.
- **Movement twin**: `movement.Env.Weather` / `StepEnv.weather` (rain, speed factor, glider push at the step's position), read once per step; the zone builds one field per chemistry step, read by every input worker. Without a field every existing golden is unchanged (regenerated byte-identical). New TS goldens `movement_pt2_weather_{snow_walk,gale_glide,storm_climb}.json` replayed by Go within 1e-9 with the discrete state exact; the glide speed bound allows the push.
- **Visuals per style** (`world/sky/weatherStyle.ts`: bolt width/brightness, flash, rainbow, fog banks, storm darkness, flake size, square flakes, shimmer): cloud cover and storm darkness on the sky dome; storms grey the air and dim sun and sky light; **fog banks** (patchy, drifting, ground-hugging density in the fog pass that also swallows the sky); **snow** points (Retro: square) and **snow settling** on upward-facing terrain; **puddles and wet sheen** in level hollows; **blowing sand** and ochre air; **heat shimmer** (a UV wobble in the fog pass); **rainbows** after about half of the showers (primary and secondary bow opposite a low sun); **lightning bolts** (seeded branching HDR ribbons: everyone sees the same shape). Gales bend the grass harder. **Low stays cheap**: 900 snow / 500 sand points (High 3,000 / 1,600), no extra passes or render targets.
- **Audio** (through the weather submix, so roofs, walls and caves muffle it): rain level follows the weather (drizzle quieter), wind rises and **gusts** in a gale, a **storm roll** under 140 Hz, blowing **sand** hiss, thunder from real strikes only, insects quiet in snow and gales.
- **Wire**: nothing per tick. A strike that could hit someone is one Event 1220 (~25 B) to players within 260 m; a metal draw one 1221; cold status one 1222 to you on change. Atlas-less worlds (the flat test and G5 scale worlds) keep WP10's global rain stub with no lightning, snow, wind or cold (`Config.Weather` nil). `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` **identical** to main (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`; a first build appended the Event 500 byte for every zone and moved them, so the byte is only sent by zones running weather). `BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000` alternating main and head: 86.2-87.8 vs 87.0-88.5 M cycles/tick, 134.2-134.7 vs 134.0-135.1 kbps/client (noise; `docs/evidence/pt2-weather-bench.txt`). With weather on, the zone builds one 64-cell field per chemistry step (5 Hz) and rolls 64 cells per 3 s lightning slot.
- **Dev**: zone `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEBUG_WEATHER=<state>` (`_DEBUG_RAIN=1|0` = rain/clear everywhere), `_NO_WEATHER`, `_NO_LIGHTNING`, `_NO_COLD`; client `?weather=<state>`, `__lodestone.setWeather / weather / weatherStrike / setRainbow`.

Tests: Go `server/pkg/weather/states_test.go` (vectors; tables sum to 100 and start fair; each climate's states at their shares over 20,000 windows; fronts coherent: a storm over a meadow is snow on the cold cell beside it 80 %+ of the time; phases differ; heat haze only by day; move rules; strikes only in storms, inside their cell, at 35 % ±5 when forced; tall spot), `server/pkg/movement/weather_golden_test.go` (the TS goldens, bound checks, snow ratio exact, field rain = `Env.Raining`), `server/zone/internal/chem` (a sixth vector: rain per weather cell), `server/zone/internal/game/weather_test.go` on the official world (a bolt goes to the sword at 20 m before the bow at 8 m, to the bow without metal, to nobody 68 m off, not into a cave; 45 damage to the victim, nothing to a bystander 12 m off, both told; a spear draws a bolt in 400 slots, warned 2 s ahead, switching to the bow dodges it (lands ≥ 4 m away), holding the spear is struck; chilled after 20 s in a snowstorm and not before, no regeneration while chilled, a stew warms at once and for 10 min, bread doesn't, a campfire does; a clear temperate day is not cold; fog cuts aggro to 0.6, the field reaches the movement env; the official world has 3+ climates). Vitest `systems/weather/weather.test.ts` (Go vectors bit for bit: climates, states, windows, rolls, fields, arrow drift, 1,294 strikes and tall spots), `blend.test.ts` (crossfade half-and-half at the change and whole outside, continuity, border bleed, overrides, easing and ground memory, rainbows only after rain with a low sun on ~half the clearings, the forecast and its line, the runtime's override order and field cache), `sim/weatherMove.test.ts` (goldens unchanged, the gale and the storm climb show), `systems/chem/chem.test.ts` (the sixth vector). Also `TestSnowWalkPredictedEqualsZone` (a sprint through a snowstorm predicted with the shared field lands within 2 cm of the zone, more than 1 m short of the calm path, 0 speed violations) and `combat_test.go`'s health guard (HurtByWeather is the one environmental source). Full suites after merging main (CR 029 pets): gen:check, go vet and go test for every Go module (all packages ok), client/contracts/worldgen typecheck (`tools/creator` fails in a worktree on ajv resolution, as before), `eslint .`, `npx vitest run` (79 files, 752 tests), contracts (52) and worldgen (28) vitest, test:golden PASS (wp01 evidence restored), the wire digest identical.

Evidence (`node client/src/systems/weather/dev/weather-shots.mjs`: solo Vite, one headless Chromium on the RTX 3070, the weather forced per shot with the dev handle; `docs/evidence/pt2-weather-shots.json` has each shot's frame times and the mix the client reported, 0 page errors): `pt2-weather-{clear,cloudy,windy,fog,drizzle,rain,thunderstorm,thunderstorm-night,rainbow,snow,snowstorm,heat-haze,sandstorm}.png` on High (snow on the official world's cold cells, sand and heat in its arid ones), `pt2-weather-{thunderstorm,snow}-{painterly,grim,retro}.png`, `pt2-weather-{rain,snowstorm,thunderstorm}-low.png`, `pt2-weather-seeded-hud.png` (seeded weather with the forecast line). Every shot held p50 16.7 / p95 16.8 ms (vsync).

Known gaps: weather does not stop at a cave ceiling in the step (as WP10's rain didn't); shelter from bolts and cold is a cave or a settlement, not any roof; snow settles on terrain only; puddles don't reflect; fog banks follow the camera's altitude, not the valleys; region elements and the WorldSpec's `stormFrequency` don't flavour the tables yet; zone strikes arrive after half a round trip; wind on arrows is client-side (inside the zone's 30° aim slack). No listening pass yet on the storm and sand layers (synthesized; levels in `audio/ambience.ts`).

Shared files touched (small): `server/pkg/movement/movement.go` (Env.Weather, snow speed, glider push, glide bound), `client/src/sim/movement.ts` (the twin), `server/zone/internal/chem/sim.go` + `client/src/systems/chem/sim.ts` (rain per cell), `server/zone/internal/combat/{ai,player,world}.go` (Chilled regen, AggroMult hook), `server/zone/internal/game/{server,traversal,night,fishing,items}.go` (the tick, env, wet, Event 500 byte, bobber rain, warm food), `server/zone/main.go` (one line), `client/src/world/{WorldScene.tsx,sky/skyDome.ts,post/postfx.ts,terrain/terrainMaterial.ts}`, `client/src/audio/{ambience,engine,acoustics}.ts`, `client/src/combat/runtime.ts` (arrow drift), `client/src/systems/{night.ts,NightHud.tsx}`, `client/src/sim/stepEnv.ts`, `client/src/scene/PlayerController.tsx`, `client/src/screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `client/src/devHandle.ts`, `contracts/events.md`, `client/src/ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`.

## Playtest 2: spell cards ✅ (branch `pt2/spell-cards`)

Nicole, on the character panel (C): "make this screen have higher contrast ratio in text. in the dropdown in the spell equipper menu and have the options do a better job of showing what school of magic theyre from and the spell type and mana and damage. maybe we do spell cards instead of a dropdown and each of them shows deep info on hover but class and type on the spell card not on hover." All client-side (`client/src/ui/spellcards`, README there): **no contract change** (assignment is the existing `equipSpell` Interact 50/3).

- **Cards instead of dropdowns**: the panel's Spells section shows the four slots of a loadout as cards (toggle between loadout 1 and 2, the active one marked; follows X) and below them a grid of known castable spells (school order, then tier; passives listed in a line, "always on"). Each card face: name, school in its colour with a glyph (flame, snowflake, bolt, mountain, gusts, sun, crescent, ring; SVG, no emoji fonts), the type (the CR 020 mechanic when there is one: Stream, Homing, Charge, Brand, Delayed burst, Orbit, Summon, Volley, Rain; else Projectile, Lobbed, Ray, Beam, AoE, Domain, Targeted, Rune, Mobility, Defensive) with the delivery beside it ("Stream · projectile"), tier, damage (×hits / ×volley, "Kills", or heals / shield / ally hits for damage-free spells), mana (per shot for streams) and cooldown. A green badge says which slot of the shown loadout holds a card.
- **Deep info on hover or keyboard focus**: a tooltip beside the panel (never over the cards) with every number the skill tree computes (`spellFacts`: damage, cooldown, mana, wind-up, range/radius/cone, chains, statuses, CR 020 mechanics, kills demons), passive lines, "your hit" with the active build, and which slots hold it. The same component (`SpellDetail`) renders the skill tree's tooltip and its picked-node card.
- **Assigning**: click a card then a slot; drag a card onto a slot (pointer drag with a ghost card; slot onto slot moves the spell); keyboard (Enter picks, Enter on a slot places, Delete clears); x clears a slot. Offline the cards show but assignment is off, with a note.
- **Contrast**: every text colour on the panel is a solid token from `spellcards/theme.ts` (no opacity-dimmed text any more): secondary `#cfd5dd`, tertiary `#a9b2bd`, gold, green, warn, red, mana blue; the panel background is 0.97 opaque. Stats rows, section text, the XP line, buttons (styled; the native Learn and Turn on buttons were grey), the learn rows (now with the school glyph, type, damage and mana), Where you stand, Coming next and the PvP section. The Dark school colour went from `#e0607e` to `#ee7390` (it was 4.3:1 on a highlighted card). The skill screen's dim text uses the same tertiary token.
- **Skill tree (N)**: its sidebar slots are the same slot cards (2 x 2, one loadout at a time with a toggle: both at once pushed the picked card off a 720p screen) and its hover tooltip is the same `SpellTooltip`. `useChar` moved to `skills/char.ts` so both screens share it. Panel width 430 → `min(580px, 100vw - 48px)`.
- **Tests**: `spellcards.test.ts` (card data for all 135 spells in combat.yaml: school, colour, glyph, type label, tier, mana, headline number, cooldown; the mechanic type of every CR 020 spell; named examples; damage-free spells; grid order; slots) and `theme.test.ts` (WCAG maths against reference values; every text token and school colour on every panel surface at least 4.5:1, the translucent panel over a black and a white world). Full suites after merging main: client typecheck, `eslint .`, `npx vitest run` (81 files, 1019 tests).
- **Evidence** (`node client/src/ui/spellcards/dev/shots.mjs`, solo, 38 checks PASS, no page errors: no dropdowns left, panel fits with no horizontal scroll, slot and grid faces, tooltip numbers and placement, pick + click, drag onto a slot, slot to slot, x, loadout 2, keyboard focus/pick/place, C closes, the skill tree's slot cards and toggle): `docs/evidence/pt2-spell-cards-{panel,hover,drag}-{1920x1080,1280x720}.png`, `pt2-spell-cards-skill-tree-{1920x1080,1280x720}.png`, `pt2-spell-cards-shots.txt`. `client/src/ui/skills/dev/shots.mjs` re-run (all PASS; its pt2-skill-tree-* shots refreshed).

Gaps: not run against a live zone (the dev handle's offline stand-in answers equip the way combat_sync does); dragging onto the hidden loadout's slot needs the toggle first; the skill tree's graph nodes stay compact boxes (cards would not fit seven lanes).

Shared files touched: `client/src/ui/progression/ProgressionHud.tsx` (the panel), `client/src/ui/pvp/PvpHud.tsx` (its panel section's colours), `client/src/ui/skills/{SkillScreen.tsx,tree.ts,dev/shots.mjs}`, `client/src/ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`.

## Playtest 2: integration QA ✅ (branch `pt2/qa`)

The ~12 playtest 2 branches merged overnight were each tested alone. This pass plays them together as one new player on a private stack (`tools/playtest/stack.mjs` with `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV_SPAWN=1 LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV_COMBAT=1 LODESTONE_ZONE_FISH_FAST=1` and sp9's debug camp) and fixes what clashed. No contract, protocol, server or migration change. Main merged three times on the way ("No full-screen flashing", the lightning tuning, spell cards + hands/arms); the spell cards are in the key and HUD audits. Evidence: `docs/evidence/pt2-qa.txt`.

**Playtest scripts** (every `tools/playtest/sp*.mjs`, one browser at a time):
- Pass: sp1 (HUD with each panel), sp4 (camp fight, loot), sp6 (tower climb, attune), sp9 (welcome, Esc menu, Controls, Esc closes panels, returning What's new, downed and respawn), sp11 (fishing, emotes, photo mode; 0 issues now), sp12 (instruments), sp13 (new, below).
- Script fixes: sp2 abandons the new starter quest in the journal before walking the Ward; sp3 routes through the streets, closes the welcome card and reports a fight that never happened (it used to report nothing); lib.mjs waits for the towns handle (sp5 raced it); sp7 / sp8 / sp10 close the welcome card before pressing E (it now waits until no panel is open and holds E while up, as a modal should).
- Left: the scripts' street routing (lib.mjs `go`) can't leave a building interior, so sp2 (smithy to the cave), sp5 (walk out of town), sp7 (forge to anvil) and sp10 (the hand-in walk, timed out at 1200 s after the talk and the slime kills passed) stall against walls. Script limitation, not the game.
- Fixed from the runs: the welcome card waited for the 7 s level-up card (sp9: it came ~10 s late, over the first tip and the first Esc); now the level-up card waits for the welcome, as it already waits for the arrival card. sp11's "reading 'isReady'" page error (three's compileAsync poll after a rejoin; `world/warmup.ts compileAsyncSafe`); sp1's level-up card over the journal and the character panel over content notes; sp8's welcome card over the level-up card; the welcome card opening over a new player's first shop dialogue.
- New **sp13** (`tools/playtest/sp13.mjs`): every playtest 2 overlay at once (weather pill with forecast and Chilled, a world event, a sealed vault, a pet out, content notes, Rested, a tip), plus per scene the camp menu, steed panel, pet panel, instrument overlay, fishing fight and catch card, emote wheel, pet wheel, taming meter, character panel with spell cards and Esc menu, checked with `hudBoxes` / `overlaps` at 1920x1080, 1600x900 and 1280x720. `hudBoxes` now also measures what a full-width fixed bar holds (the hotbar stack was skipped as "too wide").

**Keys** (`client/src/input/bindings.ts` lists every binding by situation; a read-only pass over every handler found the clashes):
- **E** answered nine features through four copied `othersBusy()` helpers; the pet and steed checked nothing; and `stopPropagation` in a window capture listener doesn't stop the other capture listeners. So with your pet or steed beside you, E petted or mounted instead of talking, mining or attuning, and one press could do two things (rest in a bedroll and mount; take loot and pet). Now `client/src/input/interact.ts` `E_ORDER` decides: town, cave, tower, loot, content, camp, fish, tame, pet, steed. Each hides its "[E]" prompt and leaves E alone while one above shows a prompt, a panel or wheel is open, or you ride (in the saddle E only gets off); the one that acts stops the event. Someone else's bedroll no longer swallows E; mining ignores an E typed in chat.
- **V** fired the weapon technique *and* push-to-talk: the technique moved to **Tab** (V stays push-to-talk, as the HUD says). With a panel open Tab moves the focus instead (the spell cards' "tab to it").
- **Spell cards** (merged from main): Enter on a focused card (or any focused button) also opened chat, and Space also jumped; chat now opens only on an unhandled Enter outside a button, and movement skips a Space a control used.
- **U / Y**: a pending duel keeps Y / U even while you play an instrument (they were notes).
- **Instrument and photo mode** swallowed every key and button release, so a Z, spell key or mouse button held when U or L was pressed stayed held for combat; releases pass now.
- **Esc** with the emote or pet wheel held opened the Esc menu too (it cancels the wheel only now); Esc typed in chat closed the town panel / emote wheel / What's new (typing guards).
- Already separated by mode, now declared per situation: **H** (emotes / photo mode's panel), **5 / 6 / 7** (whistle, pets / instrument notes), **R, wheel, mouse buttons** (camp placement / combat), **arrows, Tab, Enter** (instrument / movement, chat). Smaller: camp placement turns the piece only for a wheel over the game; a held O no longer flickers the friends panel; Ctrl+C / Ctrl+5 no longer toggle the character panel / whistle; the character panel's "Your steed 5" / "Your pets 6" hints are gone (5 whistles, 6 commands the pet; no key opens those panels).
- **Controls** (Esc menu) now match the code: Tab technique, everything E does and the one-at-a-time rule, fishing's Space / left mouse, a Photo mode group, map drag / zoom / pins, Esc cancels the wheels, Z is always the weapon's move, 1-4 also pick the right button's slot, G / Delete drop, spell card keys, debug fly.
- **Test** `client/src/input/bindings.test.ts` reads every handler file. It fails on a key code not declared in `bindings.ts`, a declared one that is gone, a key bound twice in one situation without a rule (`SHARED`: only E and Esc), a player binding missing from Controls, or an E handler outside `E_ORDER`. It caught the spell cards' keys on the merge.

**HUD** (`client/src/hud/layout.ts`; sp13 found 15 kinds of overlap, 0 after across 36 scenes):
- The world event tracker (fixed at top 92) ran into the clock pill's forecast line. It now sits under the measured clock column (`--ls-clock-h`, `BELOW_CLOCK`) and steps aside for the level-up card, a new place's card, a catch card, a held wheel and centred panels.
- The pet card sat on the vault panel below 1600x900; it moves under it (`--ls-vault-h`).
- Centred panels (camp menu / chest / signpost, steed, pets, lore tablet, journal, quest offer, inventory), the instrument overlay and the character panel covered the event tracker, content notes, vault panel, pet card, Rested strip, pet / steed notes and the chat button at 1280x720. Those now wait under them. The wider character panel (spell cards) also hides the clock pill below ~1450 px and the world event tracker below ~1680 px, and now stops above the hotbar.
- A held wheel covered the tip and the lock hint; the level-up and arrival cards sat on centred panels; the taming meter (under the crosshair) met the town prompt at 1600x900 (it sits above the crosshair now).
- The compass printed labels at one bearing over each other (a world event, a cave mouth and the quest step). The first placed keeps its label.

**No flashing** (after main's "No full-screen flashing"; treated as an accessibility bug):
- Lightning bolts strobed on-off-on through the bloom. They now come on, then fade once, at about half the HDR level.
- Fire lights strobed the ground at night: grass fires ±67 % at 2-7 Hz, campfires and town lanterns ±15 % at 2-5 Hz. Now a slow ±5-9 % sway.
- FX flashes kept 15 % opacity around the camera, so Static Step's blink flash (one at your chest every 2 m, ~15/s) strobed the whole view. They now fade out near the camera.
- Checked and left: there is no auto-exposure (exposure eases by time of day and underground only); bloom is fixed per style; the shield's edge tint is held; dash speed lines and FOV follow speed; the day/night grade eases; weather crossfades over 90 s; nothing visual reads the audio analysers.

**What's new** (`node scripts/whatsnew.mjs check`: 20 entries): one entry per feature. A returning player sees 8, and they came in merge order, so content, spells and steeds fell below the cut. Same-date entries are now ordered by how much a player will notice them (content, spells, steeds, pets, camps, weather, fishing, onboarding, controls, spell cards, graphics, music, monsters, emotes, photo mode). The footer counts the rest ("10 more: Esc, What's new" for a browser that last saw pt2-world). Graphics' rain line moved into weather. A new `pt2-controls` entry tells players about E and Tab. No shipped id was renamed or removed.

**Performance** (`client/src/world/bench/turn.mjs` against the stack's Vite, solo world: `LODESTONE_BENCH_QUERY=solo`; RTX 3070, ANGLE D3D11, headless): vsync High (1920x1080) and Low (1280x720) pan and flick p99 16.8 / max 16.8 ms, 0 frames over 33 ms, 0 long tasks. Uncapped, pan p50 / p99 / max: High 4.7 / 7.5 / 9.9 ms (flick max 7.8), Low 2.8 / 4.5 / 7.4 ms (flick max 6.6), 0 long tasks. The graphics branch measured High 4.5 / 6.6 / 8.9 and Low 2.6 / 4.1 / 10.2 before weather, pets, mounts and the rest: no new hitch to attribute.

**Checks** (after the last merge of main): `npm run gen:check` up to date; `node scripts/whatsnew.mjs check` (20 entries); go vet and go test for every Go module, all ok (the zone game package 405 s, the zone process tests 268 s; nothing flaky this run); client, contracts and worldgen typecheck (`npm run typecheck` fails only in `tools/creator` in a worktree: ajv resolution, as before); `eslint .` clean; client `npx vitest run` 84 files, 1042 tests; `npm run test:golden` PASS (wp01 evidence restored).

**Left**:
- Esc still closes every open panel at once rather than the top one (e.g. a shop and the inventory); the journal's Esc also dismisses a quest offer.
- The click that captures the mouse also swings the weapon (combat ignores pointer lock); fishing's E / Space still reel under the Esc menu; the right-click context menu is blocked document-wide (no right-click in chat); on layouts where "/" is Shift+7 the press that opens chat also toggles the pet's stay.
- The playtest scripts' street routing, above.
- The welcome card can still come up mid-walk (a one-time modal; E waits under it).
- The What's new order within a date assumes none of the 2026-11-10 entries has shipped yet.
- The turn bench ran solo; an online run (zone, other players' avatars) was not measured.

Shared files touched (small, besides the new `client/src/input/{interact,bindings}.ts`, `bindings.test.ts` and `tools/playtest/sp13.mjs`): every E handler (`world/buildings/townHud.ts`, `world/caves/caveManager.ts`, `ui/inventory/LootHud.tsx`, `ui/content/ContentHud.tsx`, `ui/camps/CampHud.tsx`, `ui/fishing/FishingHud.tsx`, `ui/mounts/MountHud.tsx`, `ui/pets/PetHud.tsx`, `ui/progression/ProgressionHud.tsx`), `combat/runtime.ts` (Tab), `ui/music/InstrumentHud.tsx`, `ui/photo/PhotoMode.tsx`, `ui/menu/{EscMenu.tsx,controls.ts}`, `ui/emotes/EmoteWheel.tsx`, `social/ui/{SocialOverlay,ChatPanel}.tsx`, `input/useKeyboard.ts`, `hud/layout.ts`, `systems/NightHud.tsx`, `ui/map/Compass.tsx`, `ui/whatsnew/{WhatsNew.tsx,news.ts,news.test.ts,changelog.json,README.md}`, `world/warmup.ts`, `world/sky/{lightningBolts,weatherStyle}.ts`, `systems/NightLayer.tsx`, `ui/camps/CampLayer.tsx`, `world/buildings/townsManager.ts`, `combat/fx/transients.ts`, `tools/playtest/{stack,lib,sp2,sp3,sp7,sp8,sp9,sp10}.mjs`, this section.

## Playtest 2: achievements ✅ (branch `pt2/achievements`)

Nicole: "add a whole achievement system, add "Tony Stark built this in a cave with scraps" that one quote from Iron Man, for when you take flight using magma dash". CR 031 (`docs/cr/031-achievements.md`). Contract: `contracts/achievements.yaml` (new, generated with content.yaml), Events **1240-1259** (1240 `achievement_announce`, 1241 `achievements_caught_up`; 1002 is now `achievement_earned`, 1000 gains `earned` and `points`), Interact 80/5 takes an achievement id. **No SQL migration** (the counters and the earned set ride in CR 023's `character_content` jsonb, state version 2); `npm run migrate` on the dev DB: up to date.

- **One table, 119 achievements** in nine categories (Exploration 18, Combat 21, Magic 19, Crafting & Gathering 19, Social 8, Companions 10, Music 7, World Events & Dungeons 11, Secrets 6); tiers bronze 10 / silver 25 / gold 50 / platinum 100 points (29 / 53 / 33 / 4; 3,665 points in all); gold and XP by tier unless set; **56 titles**; 5 item rewards; 6 hidden (shown "???" with a hint until earned); 30 chains (74 steps) shown I / II / III. The generator checks every stat and argument, item, chain, unique names and that no goal is out of reach. Full list: CR 031's table source.
- **Feats migrated**: the 37 feats (CR 023 plus fishing, pets, instruments) moved in with their keys, stats and counts (pinned by a test), so earned feats, titles and counters carry over. A state saved before CR 031 earns the new achievements its old counters already reach in **one catch-up** on its first check (one summary toast, Event 1241, rewards paid), then saves as v2. Rewards keep CR 023's `feat:<key>` grant marker: nothing is paid twice.
- **Server-authoritative counters** fed from every system (`server/zone/internal/game/achievements.go`, one-line hooks): kills by family and element, bosses, demons; spells per school (`cast:<element>`) and schools cast; dashes, metres per dash, magma jet airtime and best height, magma dives; deaths; PvP downs; duels; lightning survived (and drawn by your own metal); nights seen dusk to dawn; gold earned; items found by rarity; crafting per station; veins; herbs; fish (and by species); towers, regions, vaults found and cleared, tablets, bestiary, level; quests, bounties, honored towns; emotes; taming, petting, best pet level; metres ridden; notes, songs, ovations, fireside songs; world events by kind. Per-tick quantities add up in the session and reach the state once a second; checks run at most once a second and only after a change; saves go through the existing write-behind queue (coalesced, every 5 s at most).
