- **"Tony Stark built this in a cave with scraps"** (Secrets, gold, hidden: "Some fire is for burning. Some is for flying."; title **the Ironclad**, 150 gold, 600 XP, 5 iron ingots: the scraps). Earned the first time a **held** Magma Dash jet (the zone paid the hold: `combat.Player.DashHolding`) keeps your feet **more than 6 m above the ground or water for over 2 s in a row** in one dash; checked at once so the toast lands mid-flight. A tap never counts. Siblings: Icarus Was an Amateur (60 m up, hidden), Frequent Flyer (a minute of high jet flight), Afterburner (2 km), Superhero Landing (a crater dive, hidden).
- **Unlocks**: Event 1002 to the player only; **gold and platinum** also go to the party (same zone) and players within 64 m (Event 1240): a short content note there, never a toast.
- **Client** (`client/src/ui/achievements/`): the **Achievements screen** on **Y** (the one free world letter; a pending duel keeps Y for "accept"), also in the Esc menu, the character panel and the journal (J: the Feats tab became Achievements): points total and per category, category list, progress bars, tier badges, filters (all / earned / not yet / hidden), search (hidden ones only match "???"), recently earned, chains collapsed to the earned steps and the next one, "Show" for titles; it asks the zone for fresh counts on open and every 5 s while open. **Unlock toast** (top centre under the clock): tier colour, points, rewards, one at a time (~6 s), at most 5 queued then "+N more", waits (and pauses) while the welcome / What's new card, an arrival card, the level-up fanfare, the Esc menu or any panel is up; a **fanfare** from the existing level-up stings (by tier) plays when the card appears, so it never lands under the level-up sting. HUD rules (`hud/layout.ts`): the panel joins the centred panels; the toast takes the world event tracker's place for its seconds and hides under the big cards and panels. **Titles** are picked in the character panel (a list of earned titles, "No title") or the screen. Bindings: KeyY declared, Controls row added, `bindings.test.ts` asserts world Y is the screen and the duel keeps its Y.
- **Network**: nothing per tick; an unlock is one ~250 B Event; the sync grows ~3 KB. Content is off in atlas-less worlds, so the scale scenario has no achievement state: `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` **identical to main (p90_e600 `831bb7f4...`, p300_e2000 `8c29d408...`; bots never dash, ride or earn)**.

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/content/achievements_test.go` (80-120 achievements, all 37 feats unchanged, ids, 9 categories, 4 tiers, Tony Stark's definition; chains ordered and unlocking in order; **the migration**: a v1 state keeps its feats and title, catches up getting_the_hang / squelch / light_reading / x_marks / something_fishy / duelist once, round-trips as v2 and never again, a new state never catches up; derived stats (schools, eventkinds, fish by species, vaults found), SetMax, hidden progress kept secret until earned, points, titles, `at` saved), the existing content / fishing / pets tests on the new API; `server/zone/internal/game/achievements_test.go` in-process on the official world: **a real held magma jet** sent over the wire (60 frames/s, looking up) earns Tony Stark (name, title, iron ingots in the pack, Event 1002 to the player, Event 1240 to a player 20 m away and none to one 400 m away; dashes, metres, airtime, best height counted; the Ironclad title chosen; the state reaches the store), while a burst (no hold) earns nothing; the counter hooks (emotes, kills by element, a night from dusk to dawn but not a dawn alone, metres ridden and dashed with their remainders) and a pre-CR 031 character's single catch-up event (no toasts) followed by a normal toast (Night Owl). `content_game_test.go`'s Duelist check now waits for the Duelist unlock by id (a tablet, a herb and a vault found unlock first now). Vitest `client/src/ui/achievements/achievements.test.ts` (17: the requested one, feats kept, progress clamp, masking and search, filters, chain collapse, points, recent, titles, the 1000 / 1002 / 1240 / 1241 reducers, the toast card and queue: one at a time, held while blocked, capped "+N more"), `bindings.test.ts`, `audio/triggers.test.ts`, `ui/content/content.test.ts`. Full suites: go vet and go test for every Go module (all 53 packages ok, the zone game package 414 s, the zone process tests 276 s; nothing flaky this run); gen:check up to date; `npm run typecheck` (client, contracts, worldgen ok; `tools/creator` fails only in a worktree on ajv resolution, as before); `eslint .` clean; client `npx vitest run` (85 files, 1060 tests), contracts (52) and worldgen (28) vitest; `node scripts/whatsnew.mjs check` (22 entries: `pt2-achievements` first); `npm run test:golden` PASS (wp01 evidence restored).

Evidence (`cd client && npx vite-node src/ui/achievements/dev/achievements-shots.ts`: the real client and zone binary on the official world, one headless browser, the zone teaching Magma Dash with `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEBUG_SPELLS=magma_dash`; the player flew from y 129 to 167 on a held jet and the zone unlocked it): `docs/evidence/pt2-achievements-toast.jpg` (the Tony Stark toast: Gold, +50 pts, +150 gold, +600 XP, title "the Ironclad", 5 x Iron Ingot), `docs/evidence/pt2-achievements-screen.jpg` (the screen on Secrets: Tony Stark earned and shown as the title, the other five "???" with their hints, points 60 / 3,665, 2 of 119, recently earned).

Known gaps: counters nobody kept before (casts, dashes, deaths, nights, gold, crafting, mining, riding...) start at CR 031; nights count only on the zone that saw dusk and dawn, and "survived" means seen through; gold earned counts grants, not merchant sales; the announcement reaches the party on the same zone only; the screen polls the sync every 5 s rather than streaming progress; the pt2-content What's new line still says "feats" (shipped text left as it was); all names are proposals for Nicole (`contracts/achievements.yaml`).

Shared files touched (small): `contracts/{content.yaml,events.md,codegen/gen.mjs,codegen/lib/content.mjs,ts/src/index.ts}`, `server/pkg/contentdefs/contentdefs.go`, `server/zone/internal/combat/{player,dashes}.go` (`Dashes`, `Slams`, `DashHolding`), `server/zone/internal/content/state.go`, `server/zone/internal/game/{content,combat,items,mining,mounts,music,night,pets,pvp,weather,worldevents,emotes,fishing}.go` (hooks), client `screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `hud/layout.ts`, `input/{bindings,bindings.test}.ts`, `ui/menu/controls.ts`, `audio/{triggers,triggers.test}.ts`, `ui/progression/ProgressionHud.tsx`, `ui/content/*`, `ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`.

## Playtest 2: dev console ✅ (branch `pt2/dev-console`)

Nicole: "build a dev mode dev console locally where i can equip every item in the game, see every item, equip every spell, etc". CR 032 (`docs/cr/032-dev-console.md`). Contract: **Interact 250 `dev`** (27 actions), Events **1400-1419** (1400 `dev_status`, 1401 `dev_result`, 1402 `dev_places`). No protocol, YAML or migration change.

**How to use it**: `npm run dev` (scripts/dev.mjs now starts the zone with `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1`; a running stack must be restarted to pick it up), play, press **F2** (or the button in the ` debug panel). Tabs: **Items** (all 300 items: search, 16 categories, Give with a quantity, Equip into loadout 1 or 2 / Use; gear rolled at any rarity, item level, affix and socketed gem), **Spells** (every spell card: Learn, Unlearn, Learn all, equip to slot 1-4 of either loadout, Unlock every tier), **Character** (level, XP, gold, skill points, weapon skills; refill; god mode, infinite mana, infinite stamina, no cooldowns, fly + speed, free skill tree, freeze time; reset), **World** (teleport by x/z, minimap click, any tower / town / vault; reveal map, attune all towers; time, freeze, weather; spawn enemies (count, level, elite) and bosses, despawn; world events; tameable creatures, pets of any species / shiny / element, a steed of any colour; finish quest steps, complete or abandon quests; unlock one or all achievements). The **command line** mirrors it all (Tab completes, arrows recall): `/give voidglass_sword mythic gem=ruby affix=brutal`, `/equip iron_helm rare l2`, `/learn all`, `/level 30`, `/gold 50000`, `/skill all 100`, `/god`, `/fly`, `/speed 4`, `/tp 1200 900`, `/tp "Dekairnan"`, `/time 21:30`, `/weather thunderstorm`, `/spawn boar 5 20 shielded`, `/spawn warden`, `/pet wolf shiny 10`, `/quest all done`, `/achieve all`, `/reset yes`, `/help`.

- **Local only**: the zone needs `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1` *and* the development ticket key (a zone with `LODESTONE_TICKET_PUBLIC_KEY` refuses it and logs why). Without it every command gets 1401 "refused" and changes nothing, and the status question gets no answer, so the client never shows the console; the client also needs a debug build. No deploy sets the variable: `server/zone/devconsole_test.go` scans every file under `infra/`. The zone logs `DEV CONSOLE ON` at start and each command.
- **Server-side, normal paths** (`server/zone/internal/game/devconsole.go`, new; small new files `internal/{items,combat,explore,quests,content}/dev.go`): inventory adds/equips/uses and `commitItems` (persisted like loot, source "dev console"), `Profile.Grant`, `levelChanged`, `Teleport`, `SetClockOffset`, the weather override, `TestGroup`/`TestBoss`, `devStartEvent`, pet and steed records, quest happenings (rewards paid), `achReward`. God mode zeroes hits in `mitigate` (`Player.DevGod`); mana and cooldowns are refreshed every tick; infinite stamina and fly are applied before each input frame, and the client predicts them identically (`StepEnv.dev`, `sim/devStep.ts`; the combat timeline's `devCombat`), so flying online is server-checked with no corrections.
- Hooks in shared files (one or two lines each): `game/{server,session,combat,progression}.go`, `combat/{player,ai}.go`, `zone/main.go`, `scripts/dev.mjs`; client `sim/{fly,localSim,movement}.ts`, `net/{prediction.ts,NetPlay.tsx}`, `combat/timeline.ts`, `hud/DebugPanel.tsx`, `screens/PlayScreen.tsx`, `input/bindings.ts` (F2, ctx debug; the command box's Enter/Tab/arrows as text-field keys), `ui/menu/{controls.ts,EscMenu.tsx}` (a "Dev (local dev zone only)" group shown only on a dev zone), `ui/inventory/items.ts` (source label).

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/game/devconsole_test.go`: **refused without the flag** (give, gold, level, teleport, learn, flags, spawn, reveal: all 1401 value 1, no status event, inventory, position, level and god mode untouched) and **every command's effect** on the official world (a mythic level-20 starmetal sword with a brilliant ruby and Brutal in loadout 2's hand, a rare voidglass helm equipped, potions, gold, a tome used and learned, learn all = 135 / unlearn all = the kit, free skill tree learning a tier-3+ spell at level 1, level up and down, skill points, swordsmanship 40, god mode takes 0 from a 500 bolt, infinite mana, teleport, time 21:30, rain, 3 boars and a boss then despawned, places (16 towers, towns, vaults), reveal and attune every tower with boons, a two-step quest stepped then finished with its gold, one achievement then all 119, a shiny level-7 pet, a colour-3 steed with its whistle, fly 2x matching `DevFlyStep` to 5 cm over 60 frames, reset); `server/zone/devconsole_test.go` (the flag needs the dev key; nothing under infra/ mentions `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV`; dev.mjs sets it). Vitest `client/src/ui/devconsole/devconsole.test.ts` (11: FNV-1a vectors = Go's, the give packing, no item hash collisions, every command parsed, bad input refused with a reason, completion; **catalog completeness**: every `items:` key, gem x cut, ore and ingot in items.yaml, a tome for every combat.yaml spell, every item key mentioned in content.yaml / camps.yaml / achievements.yaml, every spell, enemy, boss, species and achievement; the dev step: fly at 20 x speed, stamina refilled; Controls lists F2 only on a dev zone), `bindings.test.ts`, `menu.test.ts`.

Evidence (`node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1`, then `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/devconsole-shots.mjs`: a new character, F2, buttons and commands; checked in the client's stores): `docs/evidence/pt2-dev-console-items.jpg` (weapons, gear roll options, the voidglass sword equipped in hand, the log), `-spells.jpg` (135 of 135 known after Learn all, slot buttons, loadout 1), `-character.jpg` (level 25, god mode + infinite mana on, skills 60), `-world.jpg` (minimap, 16 towers / 42 towns / 14 vaults, a forced thunderstorm, 3 boars spawned, quests, achievements; teleported to Dekairnan, flew from y 34.5 to 58.8).

Known gaps: walking speed is the shared movement step's (the speed multiplier is fly speed); extra skill points last the session; reset keeps pets, steed, achievements, camps, renown; time, weather and freeze are zone-wide.

## Playtest 2: bow rework ✅ (branch `pt2/bow`)

Nicole: "bows are fucked. they dont pull all the way back and fire basically instantly. should be able to hold it mouse1 and then let go mouse1 it fires, infinite ammo". CR 033 (`docs/cr/033-bow-rework.md`). Contract: `bow_charged.minChargeMs: 150` (combat.yaml; `minChargeSteps` generated); no protocol, event format, constants or migration change.

**How it plays**: with a bow, **hold the left mouse button** to draw and **let go** to shoot. 1 s to full draw (x2.4 damage, 75 m/s, 90 m reach); a tap still draws 150 ms first (a weak shot: x1.21, ~54 m), never an instant one. A draw pressed during the last shot's recovery starts as soon as it ends. **Z** (or the right button with the weapon picked) lowers the draw without shooting; the right button with a spell picked still casts; Tab is still arrow rain. A ring round the crosshair fills with the draw and turns gold at full draw. **Infinite ammo**: shots never spend arrows; using an arrow stack fits its arrowheads (damage multiplier, sunsteel kills demons) and keeps the stack (tooltip, "Fit arrowheads" button and Controls say so).

- **Server authority** (`server/zone/internal/combat/player.go`): the draw is the existing `alt_attack` bit counted per frame (`altHeld`), the release the first `attack` frame without it, exactly as the spear charge (and the bots' bow) already worked; the zone now refuses a bow attack with no draw (the old instant `bow_quick`) and a release drawn for fewer than 9 steps (one step of tolerance). The client timeline (`combat/timeline.ts`: `drawWeapon`, the bow branch of `step`) sends exactly those bits from the left button, so prediction and the zone name the same move with the same charge. Reach scales with the draw (`ShotRange` / `shotRange`: the same flight time at the lower speed), checked in hit validation and flown by your arrow and others'.
- **First person** (`combat/weapons/FirstPersonWeapon.tsx`, `models.ts`): the bow rises to an aiming hold as you draw, the string and nocked arrow come back step by step to `BOW_MAX_DRAW` (0.45 m, was 0.24) with the hand at the cheek anchor, the arms tremble at full draw, the release snaps the string (it rings down), kicks the bow and the hand follows through.
- **Others** (`combat/held.go`, `castState.ts`, `net/avatarAnim.ts`, `net/avatar.ts`): Event 226 now carries the archer's aim while they draw (like a stream; a parry or spear charge still only keeps alive), a late observer starts mid-draw from its progress, the avatar's draw grows over the same 1 s, never times out (was 3 s), trembles at full draw and the release looses from the draw it reached; each archer has their own bow geometry, so the string comes back with the hand and an arrow sits on it (before, every third-person bow shared the first-person buffer), and the bow stays upright in the raised hand (it used to lie along the arm).
- Controls (`ui/menu/controls.ts`), `input/bindings.ts` (Z: "a bow: lower the draw"), the combat hint line and the hotbar's secondary label ("lower · Z").
- `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1`: unchanged by construction: the G5 scale bots only walk; the combat bots already drew the bow a full second through `alt_attack`.

Tests: Go `internal/combat/bow_test.go` (hold/release fires with charge = held/60; minimum draw: no draw and 2 steps refused, 9 legal; an attack still carrying the draw bit is not a release; the spear unchanged; damage full/tap = 2.4/1.21; a tap's arrow refused at 70 m, a full draw's accepted; a turning draw sends aim updates, a spear charge does not), `internal/game/bow_test.go` (real session: a bow shoots with no arrows; using 20 iron arrows fits iron and keeps 20; three drawn hits later still 20). Vitest `combat/timeline.test.ts` (7 bow/spear cases), `combat/weapons/hands.test.ts` (full draw reaches the cheek anchor ~0.4 m back, arrow along the view, head past the riser, workable wrist, string ring-down), `net/avatarAnim.test.ts` (draw follows hold time, half-draw release, late observer, 20 s hold), `net/avatar.test.ts` (independent strings, arrow on while drawing), `combat/castState.test.ts`, `input/bindings.test.ts`.

Evidence (`node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1`, then `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/bow-shots.mjs`: an archer with `/equip oak_bow` holds the real left mouse button, a second player 3.5 m away watches; checked in the client state: draw 0.38 at 380 ms, 1.0 at ~1.1 s, the shot `bow_charged` charge 1, a tap `bow_charged` charge 0.15, third person the same, 0 refusals from the zone; the observer's avatar of the archer in `draw` with its string at 0.72 when the screenshot was taken): `docs/evidence/pt2-bow-fp-partial.jpg`, `-fp-full.jpg` (string to the bottom-right edge, arrow on it), `-fp-loose.jpg` (string snapped, arrow away), `-tp-partial.jpg`, `-tp-full.jpg` (your own third-person body drawing), `-observer-drawn.jpg` (the archer seen side-on by the other player, bow upright, string at the chin).

Known gaps: no movement slow while drawing (the movement step is shared Go/TS golden code; left out); no "perfect release" bonus (there was none); the archery techniques `quick_draw`, `double_shot`, `charged_pierce` are still data only; the first-person hold is tuned for the 75 degree default field of view.

## Playtest 2: first-person view model ✅ (branch `pt2/viewmodel`)

Nicole, with production screenshots: "fix the view" (the sword stood upright through the middle of the screen and cut a boss straight ahead in half) and "fix this fucked up hand" (holding a staff: a lumpy clump, boxy oversized thumb and fingers, two fists stacked on the staff, flat orange, an abrupt wrist). No contract, protocol or server change; the bow pose (CR 033) is untouched.

- **Rest poses keep the middle of the view clear** (`combat/weapons/FirstPersonWeapon.tsx` `POSES`): the sword is carried low on the right, blade up and leaning a little left with its tip off the top of the screen right of centre; the staff stands upright on the right with its head in the top-right corner (casts still leave from it); the wand is held low with its tip beside the crosshair; the spear's point is low, aimed at a foe's waist below the crosshair (both hands on it, apart). Nothing at rest enters the middle 40% of the screen (width and height) at 16:9 or 21:9 at the 75 degree field of view, nor the middle 25% at the widest a dash or gallop kicks it (91 degrees). The field of view is vertical, so past 16:9 the whole rig slides right in proportion (`aspectShift`) to stay as far from the crosshair on an ultrawide.
- **Swings still cross the view**: melee swings are now keyframed (`SWINGS`, `swingPose`): a forehand is cocked over the right shoulder, cuts through the middle down to the lower left and returns; a backhand is the mirror; the staff and wand swing 70% as far. (They used to be Euler rotations about a shoulder pivot tuned for the old upright rest.)
- **A new hand** (`combat/weapons/handMesh.ts`, used through `models.ts`): a palm whose cross-section wraps the grip, rounded at both sides, with the heel and the ball of the thumb behind the grip, tapering into a wrist; four fingers of three phalanges each (slimmer to the tip, a knuckle ridge, a swell at each joint), each phalanx laid tangent to the grip so the fingers close on each weapon's own radius (a wand pulls the tips into the palm, a staff leaves them on its side), the little finger shorter and curled more; a two-phalanx thumb wrapped the other way round the grip above the index finger instead of pointing off it; the open casting hand rebuilt from the same fingers. Slightly under adult size. **Shading**: a small ray-cast ambient occlusion baked into the vertex colours (against the hand itself and the grip), darker and warmer in the creases and the curl, in the avatar's skin colour (`#c9a27e`). Each first-person fist is built and baked once per grip radius (~30 ms), all of them when the rig mounts, so a weapon switch never waits; the third-person fist (avatar, plain material) is built without the bake and stays under 400 triangles. The forearm narrows at the wrist (the hand's wrist runs into it) and ends in a sleeve cuff (lip, band, seam).
- **The staff is one-handed**: the right hand alone holds it (there was no second fist in the code; the old fist's knuckle block and heel blob read as two). Only the spear and the bow have the off hand on the weapon.
- Dev tools: `rig-dev.html` / `node client/scripts/rig-shots.mjs [prefix]` gained 16:9 sheets with the middle-40% box and crosshair (`fp-rest`, `fp-swing-wide`), a `pose` override to try a hold without rebuilding, an `orbit` close-up round a hand, and `RIG_SHOTS_SHEET='{cols,rows,items}'` one-off sheets.

Tests: vitest `combat/weapons/hands.test.ts`: every weapon's rest pose (sword, spear, staff, wand) at 16:9 and 21:9 keeps the weapon, hands and forearms out of the middle 40% at 75 degrees and the middle 25% at 91 (every vertex and points along every triangle edge, projected); a forehand and a backhand sweep through the middle 15%; the skin is shaded by its shape (the darkest crease under 75% of the back of the hand); the staff, wand and sword have no off hand on them, the spear's hands are apart; the existing wrist-angle, forearm-at-the-wrist, right/left-hand and fingers-close-on-each-grip-radius checks still pass. Deliberate expectation changes: first-person fist under 2000 triangles (was 1400), open hand under 1500, forearm under 320 (was 200: the cuff).

Evidence: real client, a new local-profile character on a `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1` zone out in the meadow with a boss (`/spawn warden`) straight ahead, `/equip` each weapon, the middle-40% box drawn on each frame (`LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/viewmodel-shots.mjs <before|after>`): `docs/evidence/pt2-viewmodel-before-{rest,action,wide}.jpg` (main, the user's dev stack) and `pt2-viewmodel-after-{rest,action,wide}.jpg` (this branch, own Vite against the same zone): at rest the boss is in clear view with every weapon at 16:9 and 21:9; the forehand and backhand frames catch the follow-through with the swing trail across the middle; the sword's off-hand palm and the staff's cast. Plain backdrop: `pt2-viewmodel-after-sheet-fp-rest.jpg`, `-fp-swing-wide.jpg`, `-fp-close.jpg` (compare `pt2-hands-arms-fp-close.jpg`), `pt2-viewmodel-after-hand-custom.jpg` (the fist from the eye, the thumb side and the palm side, on the sword and wand, and the open hand).

Known gaps: the iron sword's blade reads dark navy in the game's light when it doesn't catch the sun (the weapon material's sky reflection is dim on a blade facing the camera; the rig lab's light shows it silver); the hand is a single mesh per grip radius (no per-finger animation; the bow's string hand uses the same closed fist); a faint crease remains where the wrist meets the forearm in close-ups; 32:9 is not checked (the shift covers it partly).

## Playtest 2: visible armor ✅ (branch `pt2/armor-visuals`)

Nicole: "make armor appear on characters and have that be shared client / server state, so everybody sees the same thing". CR 034 (`docs/cr/034-visible-armor.md`). Contract: **protocol v6** (`EntityRecord.appearance`, u64), Event 400 `ap`, items.yaml armor `style`, `cloth` material, eight armor items and three sunsteel recipes (all appended). No new Event kinds, constants or migrations.

- **What you see**: every armor piece on every avatar (yours in third person and everyone else's): caps, helms (open nasal in iron; closed great helms with a ridge, a starmetal crest and wings, voidglass spikes, a crown of sunsteel rays), hoods, circlets; jerkins, cuirasses with plate pauldrons and a fauld, mail hauberks with a tabard, robes with a mantle and long skirt; leggings and boots, greaves with knee cops and sabatons, tall boots. Coloured by material (sunsteel glows), trimmed in the rarity's colour (a glow from relic up, a holographic sheen for Bitchin'), a gem inlay when socketed. The navy shirt, skin and trousers under it are hidden. In first person your forearms wear the body armor's bracers, vambraces or sleeves.
- **Shared state**: the zone packs the three equipped pieces (visual id, material, rarity, gem) into one number, recomputed only when items change (`server/zone/internal/game/appearance.go`). It rides the player's snapshot record: sent on change and on first sight (late joiners, players walking into range, ghosts and presence from the next zone, after a handoff or rejoin), nothing per tick, nothing for a player wearing nothing. Your own body reads the same number from your inventory event. The baseline frames still hold 40-byte states (the appearance is interned into old padding); ghost/presence frames list it only for armored players.
- **Cost**: one draw call per outfit (one skinned mesh bound rigidly to the rig's joints; it replaces up to 11 bare meshes), 1,856-2,848 triangles per full outfit, geometry shared by everyone wearing the same appearance and built only when it changes; mid LOD a static silhouette (at most 225 triangles), far LOD the box in the outfit's colour.
- **Wire digest**: the G5 bots wear nothing, so `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` byte counts are identical (p90_e600 21,071,668, p300_e2000 55,563,662); the hashes change only by the protocol version number in Hello/Welcome (with the constant set back to 5 they are identical to main: `831bb7f4...`, `8c29d408...`).
- **New armor**: Traveller's Hood, Woven Robe (cloth), Travel Boots, Iron Mail Hauberk, Silver Circlet in loot; a forged sunsteel set (helm, mail, greaves; blueprints). The dev console's gem option gives armor a cosmetic socket so the inlay can be seen.

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/game/appearance_test.go` (encoding round trip and the shared vector; in-process replication: equip three pieces and the observer's record equals the zone's number and the owner's `ap`, no re-send while unchanged, a late joiner on first sight, unequip clears the slot for both; ghost and presence frames, byte-identical when nobody is armored; a two-zone handoff recomputes it and the old zone's ghost carries it to an observer there), `server/pkg/snapshot/appearance_test.go` (40-byte `encEnt`, delta coding, interning). Vitest `client/src/combat/armor/armor.test.ts` (every armor item in items.yaml at common, relic with a gem and Bitchin': parts on the right bones, finish, hidden parts, one skinned geometry on real bones, mid silhouette, first-person sleeve; an avatar: fewer draw calls than bare, rebuilt only on change, shared between wearers, helm at head height and greaves on the legs, freed by the last wearer; mid and far LOD; first-person dressing), `net.test.ts`.

Evidence: two browsers on a private dev stack (`node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1`, then `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/armor-shots.mjs`): the Knight equips a mythic starmetal helm (aquamarine), a legendary silver cuirass and rare iron greaves with the dev console; the Watcher draws exactly the Knight's inventory appearance (39686077724106, 19 parts); unequipping all three gives 0 for both; a rejoin (local profile) keeps it and the Watcher sees it again; 0 issues. `docs/evidence/pt2-armor-observer.jpg` (the Knight's own third person beside the Watcher's view from the front), `pt2-armor-back.jpg`, `pt2-armor-fp.jpg` (first-person vambraces). Rig lab sheets (`node client/scripts/armor-shots.mjs`): `pt2-armor-gallery.jpg`, `-gallery-back.jpg`, `-gallery-side.jpg` (every style and material, rarities, a sword swing), `pt2-armor-fp-sheet.jpg`, `pt2-armor-lod.jpg`.

Known gaps: armor bases have no sockets (only dev gems inlay); the rig has no feet (boots add one); legs swing through a robe's skirt at full stride; a local profile's blob is re-issued at most every 5 s (pre-existing), so an equip change in the last seconds before leaving waits for the next change or the 30 s checkpoint.

## Playtest 2: enemy pathfinding ✅ (branch `pt2/pathfinding`)

Playtest: enemies (and the pets and summons that move the same way) stopped at walls, fences, closed doors, camp pieces, cliffs and water, then went home; in towns and vaults it looked dumb, and a player behind a fence could fight from safety. Server only (`server/zone/internal/combat`, README "Pathfinding"): **no contract, protocol or wire change, no CR, no migration**. This closes "Playtest 1: server polish"'s known gap (no pathfinding) and the pets' "follow into caves by warping".

- **The grid** (`nav.go`): 0.5 m cells sampled with exactly the rules an enemy step obeys (ground height for climbs, water deeper than 0.6 m, `World.Obstacles`: town boxes and walls, closed doors, solid camp pieces), one walkable bit per body class (radius 0.45 / 0.75 / 1.25 m), built lazily per 16 m chunk the first time a search needs it, kept in a deterministic LRU (768 chunks, ~4 MB), dropped where a door opens or closes or a camp piece is placed or picked up (`internal/game/obstacles.go` `navDoorChanged`, `navCampsChanged`).
- **Only when the straight step is refused**: enemies keep the cheap steering (the +/-69 degree fan); after 3 refused steps in a row (slope, water, a box, a door, a cave wall; never a ward or a boss arena) they ask for a path. The ask is a flag (safe on the AI workers); one serial nav pass after the enemy loop queues asks in spawn order (then summons, then pets) and runs weighted A* (octile x2, 8-connected, no corner cutting, uphill-limited edges, a 128 m window) one search at a time within a **global budget of 1,200 node expansions a tick** (a chunk build costs 160), time-sliced across ticks; a search that has not arrived after 600 expansions floods the goal's pocket once (a player in a shut house fails in a few hundred cells). Paths are string-pulled into a few waypoints. The last 32 answers are shared: a pack chasing one player round one house costs one search (`navshare.go`).
- **Following**: waypoints with the same stepMove; a re-plan when the target has moved 2.5 m + a quarter of the distance from where the path was planned (the old path is followed meanwhile), a shortcut when the target comes into the open (grid line of sight, every 4th tick), a re-plan when the walker stops making progress. Flyers never path (they float over), bosses neither (open arenas); demons path and still shadow-step. On a path the leash is x1.5 (a detour round a building is not "too far") and a returning enemy gets 20 s instead of 6 s before it is put home; a chaser whose searches keep failing (no way in) gives up and walks home instead of waiting at the wall; melee enemies don't strike through a wall they are pathing round.
- **Pets and summons** (`navcave.go`): the same paths; they now walk the players' level ground (town floors, cave floors) and are kept out of cave walls. When the owner goes down a cave mouth (or comes out) the pet follows the cave graph's own nav graph (mouth, entry tunnel axis points, chambers) after a grid path to just outside the mouth; the warp stays the fallback (no route, or 15 s without arriving).
- **Determinism**: the same seed and inputs give the same paths, with or without workers (`TestNavDeterministic`: serial vs 4 goroutines, 200 enemies, compared every tick).

Measurements (`docs/evidence/pt2-pathfinding-bench.txt`):
- **Scale world** (`BenchmarkScale/p300_e2000`, 600 ticks, -cpu 4): no towns and flat ground, so no step is ever refused and the nav pass never runs: within noise (quieter hour, 3 vs 2 runs: tick p50 4.66-4.79 -> 4.57-4.73 ms, p99 6.79-7.21 -> 6.70-7.50 ms, combat 0.74-0.79 -> 0.71-0.79 ms; alternated in a busier hour: p50 5.52-6.33 vs 5.35-5.88 ms). On the official terrain (`LODESTONE_BENCH_TERRAIN=1`, 2 alternated runs) enemies meeting cliffs and water now path: combat phase 1.16-1.23 -> 1.27-1.29 ms mean, tick p50 6.34-6.48 -> 5.90-6.20 ms and p99 11.2-13.6 -> 10.4-11.1 ms (no worse; noise), downlink 136.1 -> 135.8 kbps (behaviour changed). `LODESTONE_WIRE_DIGEST=1` **identical** on main and the branch (p90_e600 21,071,668 bytes `a21b9b94...`, p300_e2000 55,563,662 bytes `c34d60a4...`).
- **Town crowd** (new `BenchmarkTownCrowd`, combat package: 200 enemies in 40 packs chasing 20 players running laps round a 6 x 6 town of closed houses, 4 workers): 65-68 us a combat step vs 59-65 us without pathfinding (+~6 us): 1.5 asks and 27 expansions a tick, 30 % of asks answered from a shared path, ~25 enemies on a path at any time.
- **Saturated budget** (`BenchmarkNavSaturated`: 500 enemies west of a 100 m wall all asking at once, the whole budget spent every tick): 225-243 us vs 93-96 us, so **the budget caps the serial nav pass at ~0.14 ms a tick** on this desktop (about 0.2 ms on a 2C/4T cloud slice by the G5 ratio), against the 0.4-1.0 ms of p99 headroom scenario 1 has there; towns are where it runs, not the soak's open country.

Tests: `internal/combat/nav_test.go` (a wolf round a 60 m wall, round a closed house to a player behind it, through a 2.2 m gate in a 240 m fence, up the only ramp onto a sheer 6 m plateau; never through a closed door: it gives up and goes home, and walks in when the door is open; door invalidation; flyers never ask; 500 asks: expansions never over the budget in any tick, the overrun (one expansion's chunk builds) under 3 chunk builds, most wolves round the wall within 20 s; determinism; a pet round a wall to its owner without a warp), `navbench_test.go` `TestTownCrowdChasesRoundHouses` (chaser-seconds standing still more than 6 m from their player: 55 with pathfinding vs 313 without), `obstacles_test.go` (a 600 m wall still stops a chaser; an enemy inside an obstacle can leave), and on the official world `internal/game/nav_test.go` (a pet walks into two cave mouths after its owner in the entrance chamber 9-11 m down, in ~3 s, and back out; no step over 0.7 m, no warp) and `obstacles_test.go` `TestEnemiesDoNotWalkThroughBuildings` (wolves behind a spawn-town house now come round it to the player at its door; still never inside it or overlapping a wall). Full suites: gen:check, go vet for every Go module, `go test ./...` in server/zone (all ok; `TestZoneProcessTowerClimbAndFastTravel` failed once under the parallel run on a prediction mismatch of the player's tower climb and passed 2/2 alone) and server/pkg (ok), client `tsc --noEmit` and eslint for the new dev script.

Evidence (headless, one browser, a private zone with `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1` on the official world: `cd client && npx vite-node src/combat/dev/pathfinding-shots.ts`; `docs/evidence/pt2-pathfinding-shots.txt`): a small house in Draedmore; from behind it `/spawn wolf 3` and run round it to its door (`pt2-pathfinding-run.png`: the pack along the side wall after you); then `/despawn`, three wolves spawned behind the house and you `/tp` off its front corner: they come round the back corner and along the side wall (`pt2-pathfinding-round-corner.png`, 2.5 s) and are at you in 6.4 s (`pt2-pathfinding-arrived.png`); 0 of 297 wolf samples inside the walls: PASS.

Known gaps: enemies (not allies) still walk the surface heightfield only, so they never follow a player into a cave; the grid's height is the surface ground's, so enemies don't path up stairs or onto porches; a path through a door that closes meanwhile is noticed when the walker is stopped (then a re-plan), not at once; detours beyond the 128 m window (round a walled town to a gate on the far side) fail and the chaser goes home; a melee enemy's first strike can still land through a thin wall before it has been refused a step; trees and boulders are still no obstacle for anyone.

## Playtest 2: polish ✅ (branch `pt2/polish`)

The UX leftovers from "Playtest 2: integration QA" (its Left list) and the view model's dark iron sword. Client only: no contract, protocol, server or migration change.

- **Esc closes one thing at a time** (`client/src/input/panelStack.ts`): one stack of open panels. Every data-ui-panel, mode and held wheel registers when it opens (`trackPanel` on its store, `usePanelEsc` for component state, `syncPanel` / `showPanel` elsewhere): inventory, crafting station, character, skill screen, journal, quest offer (separately: the journal's Esc no longer dismisses an offer), map, achievements, steed, pets, tablet, camp menu / chest / signpost / placing, town panel, sound, graphics, friends, chat, dev console, What's new, instrument and photo mode, the emote and pet wheels. The Esc menu's capture listener (installed first) pops the newest and stops the event; with nothing open it opens the menu; with the menu open it closes the menu first. Panels no longer listen for Esc themselves; text fields keep theirs (chat, the dev console's line, the signpost name). Esc in a slider or checkbox closes its panel (only text boxes count as typing). An Esc that closed a panel while the browser dropped the mouse capture does not also open the menu.
- **The capturing click doesn't attack** (`input/useMouseLook.ts isCaptureClick`, asked by `combat/runtime.ts`): a click on the view with the mouse free only captures it. Where a browser keeps refusing the lock (two refusals in a row) clicks fight unlocked, so nothing is stuck. `tools/playtest/bow-shots.mjs` clicks once before its first draw.
- **Held actions under the menus** (`input/held.ts`): the fishing reel, a bow's draw, streams and charges (1-4, right button), Z and instrument notes let go when the Esc menu opens, the mouse capture or the window focus is lost, or the page hides. Under the Esc menu or a panel that freed the mouse, fishing ignores E / Space / clicks (and swallows them while a line is out: no jump), and 1-4 / Z / clicks start nothing (1-4 still pick the right button's slot). The instrument rests under the menu.
- **Right-click** (`useMouseLook blocksContextMenu`): the browser's menu is blocked only over the game view and while the mouse is captured; chat, text boxes and panels have it again (the map, chat names, the skill screen and the Esc menu keep their own).
- **Chat shortcuts on any layout** (`input/useKeyboard.ts isChatShortcut / notGameKey`): handlers bind `e.code` (the key's place) and now also ask `e.key`: a press that types "/" (Shift+7 on German / Nordic / Spanish layouts, Q on Hebrew) is chat's. It toggled the pet's stay (7) and could take a form (Q). Audit: every world letter and digit handler (23 files, incl. voice push-to-talk) goes through `notGameKey`; `bindings.test.ts` fails on one that doesn't.
- **Steel reads as steel** (`combat/weapons/material.ts METAL_GLSL`, used by the weapon and the armor material): the sky reflection was a fifth of the hemisphere light on a blade facing the camera and took the zenith's saturated blue, so iron went dark navy unless the sun caught it. Now the reflection sees ground, a pale horizon and the zenith by the reflected direction (rough metal sees an averaged sky, not a darker one), metal reflects its own colour face-on and goes toward white at grazing angles, metalness stops at 0.8 (a fifth stays diffuse, lit by every light and the styles' bands), and a broad sheen reflects the scene's own lights (sun, moon, campfires, cave lights: underground, where the sky term is near zero, the blade shines in the torchlight). Armor uses the same chunks at half gain (`MT_GAIN`: its plate is about half metal and already lit diffusely; the full gain washed iron plate to white). Program cache keys v2.
- **The welcome card waits until you stop walking** (`whatsnew/news.ts cardMayShow`: standing still 1.2 s, at most 3 min), on top of waiting for panels and arrival cards.
- What's new: `pt2-polish` (`node scripts/whatsnew.mjs check`: 25 entries).

Tests: vitest `input/panelStack.test.ts` (newest first, one per Esc; journal over an offer; re-open vs refresh; store tracking; every file drawing a data-ui-panel registers on the stack), `ui/menu/menu.test.ts` (`escAction` 'pop'), `combat/input.test.ts` (the capturing click; unlocked fallback; "/" on Digit1 / KeyQ casts nothing; a stream, a draw and Z let go when the menu opens and don't start under it; `releaseHeld`; fishing doesn't reel under the menu and stops when it opens), `input/useMouseLook.test.ts` (context menu), `input/bindings.test.ts` (chat shortcut; every world letter / digit handler guarded; Esc bindings redeclared: one world Esc, text fields in `NOT_HANDLERS`), `combat/weapons/material.test.ts` (the reflection on the CPU: face-on iron > 2.5x the old one and less blue, at least a matte surface under the sky, sky / horizon / ground faces differ, Fresnel, rough not darker, night dimmer; both materials use every chunk), `ui/whatsnew/news.test.ts` (the card's timing).

Verified in the real client on a private stack (`node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1`; the user's 5173 stack runs a protocol 5 zone, main's client speaks 6 since the armor merge, so it can't log in until that zone is rebuilt): the first click captured the mouse without a swing and the next swung; holding 1 then opening the menu dropped the stream; I then J, Esc closed the journal only, Esc the inventory, Esc opened the menu; right-click blocked on the canvas, allowed in the chat box; Esc in chat closed only chat; a Shift+7 "/" opened chat with "/" and left a pet's mode alone. `tools/playtest/sp9.mjs` (Esc menu, Controls, Esc closes each panel, welcome, What's new): 0 issues.

Evidence (`LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/material-shots.mjs <before|after>`: a new character in the meadow, `/equip iron_sword` and `iron_cuirass`, the lower right of the first-person view): `docs/evidence/pt2-polish-material-before.jpg` (main's material) and `pt2-polish-material-after.jpg`: day facing both ways, dusk, night, the painterly / grim / retro styles, and a cave (walked in along a cave route). Before, the blade is navy in every frame but the one facing the sun and near black in the cave; after, grey steel with lighter edges in daylight, warm at dusk, moonlit at night, torchlit in the cave. Third person, the iron helm, cuirass and greaves keep their shading (checked against main's).

Checks: client typecheck; `eslint .` clean (tracked files); client `npx vitest run` 90 files, 1145 tests. No Go touched.

Left: the playtest scripts' street routing (QA); the What's new same-date order note (QA); an online turn bench (QA). The sheen ignores shadows (a blade in a building's shadow still catches a little sun).

## Playtest 2: main story + endgame ✅ (branch `pt2/main-story`)

Nicole: the game has no way to beat it; she wants a two-player "trying to beat the game" evening. CR 035 (`docs/cr/035-main-story.md`), design, lore and pacing in `docs/decisions/0024-main-story.md` (**every name, line and number is a proposal**). Events **1600-1612** (range 1600-1629), Interact **140 `story`**, dev console action **28** (`/story`); combat.yaml (the Hush, its Hollow Bells, attacks 89-95), items.yaml (five relics), achievements.yaml (11 appended, a new category "The Last Song"), content.yaml (codex). **No protocol change, no migration** (acts ride in `character_quests`, the post-game counters and the vow in `character_content` stats).

- **"The Last Song"**, six acts after the prologue, each a quest of the new kind 3 "Main story" (`story:main:<n>`; the tracker, journal, markers, saving and handoff as for any quest; it can't be abandoned), started by the zone when the last ends (Act I when "Ward against the night" is done), with a title card in the discovery-intro style and a level gate (5/8/10/12/14): **I The Fading Hum** (Ulus the innkeeper of Draedmore, the Last Keeper's stone, the first tower), **II The Tuning Forks** (three towers in three regions), **III Verses in the Deep** (two sealed vaults: the Verses of Dawn and Dusk), **IV The Night Unsung** (Teiraben's vigil: the beacon starts the town defense, then the **Starless Sovereign** walks out of the dark), **V Dawnforged** (Breus of Steinmadun, the Archon of the Sanctum of Bukefoim, the Dawnheart), **VI The Heartfall** (the last tower in the Verdant Downs, the Heartfall, the Hush). Places are world-global and deterministic (`internal/game/story.go resolveStory`), so party members get the same acts. ~3.5-4 h for one new player.
- **The finale, the Hush** (`internal/game/finale.go`): armed when someone due to fight it (or who beat the game) walks into the Heartfall; a 7 s entrance (letterbox, title card, its lines; it is still and untouchable); phase 1: its attacks and **the shroud** (8 % of its health that only sunsteel or Light breaks; broken: a stagger and 1.5x damage; unbroken in 10 s: the Long Quiet hits everyone and it heals); phase 2 (70 %): **the choir**, untouchable while 2-4 Hollow Bells (Unsung: sunsteel or Light) toll and the chorus falls under every player; the last bell: it reels (2x damage); phase 3 (35 %): faster, a lance, the shroud again, and **the endless night** every 30 s: only players in a **well of dawn**, one each, are spared. Scaling 1-4 (party health/damage scaling; bells and wells by players). Victory: Act VI ends, **the ending** over a sunrise and **the credits** (the game's lines, then CREDITS.md). **Post-game**: calmer nights (0.6x hunters), the **Starless Vow** (journal: 1.5x hunters, the Hush 4 levels higher with every mechanic quicker), the rematch, titles the Dawnsinger / the Dawnsworn / the Unbroken / of the Long Night / the Vigilant / the Steadfast.
- **Two players**: a step one member completes completes for every member within 200 m at the same act and step (talks, reads, gates, vaults, the vigil, the finale); each member's act and step in the quest tracker; the one behind **catches up** from the journal (relics of the skipped steps, half their XP); **revives**: downed by an enemy with a friend within 40 m you wait 20 s; the friend holds **E** 3 s beside you and you rise with half your health (Space respawns now). Loot stays personal.
- **Combat hooks** (`internal/combat/story.go`): `Enemy.Guard` (one line in `applyDamage`), `HeldUntil` (in `disabled`), story strikes, `HurtPlayerBy`, `HoldDowned` / `Revive` / `RespawnNow`. Merged with main's pathfinding (bosses never path; the Hush stays in its arena) and polish (the ending joins the panel stack; the story's keys use `notGameKey`).
- **Dev console**: `/story act 4 2`, `/story heartfall`, `/story hush 34`, `/story bells`, `/story down`, `/story win` (the Hush, or the Sovereign during the vigil), `/story vigil`, `/story places`, `/story reset`.

Tests: Go `internal/story/story_test.go` (acts, gates, attune by tower, shared steps, catch-up), `internal/combat/story_test.go` (guard, hold, story strike, held down / revive / respawn now), `internal/game/story_game_test.go` (**two players in a party play Acts I-VI to the ending in process on the official world**: shared steps, a real tower attunement, one wandering off and catching up, the vigil's defense and the Sovereign, the sanctum and the lens, the Dawnheart, the Heartfall's arming and entrance, scaled for two, the shroud, three bells, the endless night (one well each: spared; one well for two: struck), a revive, the ending for both, achievements and titles, calmer nights, the vow and the harder rematch, the saved state after a reconnect; 18 s). Vitest `ui/story/story.test.ts`, `devconsole.test.ts`, `bindings.test.ts`, `enemies.test.ts`. Full suites after merging main (pathfinding, polish): gen:check up to date; go vet every module clean; go test every module ok (the zone game package 453 s, the zone process tests 273 s; `server/social` TestTwoProcessesAcrossZones failed once in the full run and passed alone, a multi-process timing flake; `content` TestAchievementsKeepEveryFeat now allows 80-140 achievements and 10 categories); client typecheck ok (`tools/creator` fails only in a worktree, ajv resolution, as before); `eslint .` clean; client vitest 91 files, 1150 tests; contracts vitest ok; `node scripts/whatsnew.mjs check` (26 entries, `pt2-main-story` first); `npm run test:golden` PASS (wp01 evidence restored); no migration.

Evidence (`node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1 --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_NO_WORLD_EVENTS=1`, then `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/story-shots.mjs`: two headless browsers, two new characters partied through the social service; 0 issues): `docs/evidence/pt2-main-story-act1-intro.jpg` (Act I's card), `-shared-step.jpg` (B's tracker advanced by A's talk), `-vigil.jpg` (Teiraben's defense), `-sovereign.jpg` (held; the Sovereign's line), `-hush-intro.jpg` (the entrance), `-shroud.jpg`, `-choir.jpg` (three bells for two), `-wells.jpg` (the endless night's countdown), `-revive.jpg` (B down, A holding E), `-ending.jpg`, `-credits.jpg`, `-postgame.jpg` (the journal: the acts done, the Starless Vow).

Known gaps: the Heartfall's arena is client-drawn (no pillar collision, no worldgen structure); shared steps and the party line cover party members on the same zone; the vigil reuses the town defense as it is; the Hush borrows the archon's model and the bells the shade's; the boss bar shows "phase 1/1" until the Hush's first phase message (the client's guess, as for other bosses); during the entrance other HUD cards (a level-up, a tip) are only dimmed, not hidden; all names and lines are proposals for Nicole.

## Playtest 2: immersion pass ✅ (branch `pt2/immersion`)

Nicole: "make the game really good and immersive ... really analyze the assets you make for immersion". LOOK, SOUND and FEEL only: no gameplay rule, contract, protocol, server or migration change (another agent ran the two-player PLAY playtest).

**Audit** (`docs/evidence/pt2-immersion-audit.md`): a structured screenshot set from the real client on a private dev stack (`tools/playtest/immersion-shots.mjs`: one new character driven by the dev console, High + a Low sheet, default Lodestone style; contact sheets `docs/evidence/pt2-immersion-<sheet>-{before,after}.jpg` for biomes by day/dusk/night/rain/snow, towns, caves and towers, enemies, the Heartfall, avatars, first person, spells, camp and fishing, HUD and menus, Low, world life) and the audio trigger map read against every action and Event. A critique per asset class (silhouette, scale, colour, light, motion, audio, UI, world life, spell breakers) and the top 15 immersion breakers ranked by impact / effort; 13 of them fixed, each its own commit:

- **The Hush and its Hollow Bells** (`combat/enemies`: styles `the_hush`, `hollow_bell`; models + allocation-free poses): the final boss wore the Archon, its bells the Shade. The Hush: a towering mourning veil with no body, a porcelain mask split by a glowing crack in an open hood, a crown of broken tuning-fork tines, a ribcage opening over the stolen songs, long sleeves and pale fingers, a broken halo, trailing ribbons; slow breath, a listening head tilt, arms open for the rend, drawn up for the toll. A Hollow Bell: a dark bronze bell on a broken chain, violet cracks, a glowing hollow mouth, rings of sound; it sways on its pivot, swings through the toll as the rings spread, cracks and drops (shatter). Gallery sheets `pt2-enemy-models-story.jpg` / `-story-before.jpg`.
- **No floating horns over demons** (`systems/NightLayer.tsx`): WP15's generic cone horns and ball eyes still hung over every demon beside the new models' own (and would have crowned the bells).
- **The cinematic plays alone** (`ui/story/cinematicMode.ts`): during the Hush's entrance and the ending/credits one stylesheet rule hides every element that neither holds the canvas nor sits in the cinematic's layer (region card, tracker, boss bar, tips, hotbar, a welcome card); counted holders; the welcome card waits for it.
- **World life** (`world/life/ambientLife.ts`): flocks of birds (hawks over highlands and badlands), butterflies, fireflies on warm dry nights (slow glows, never blinking), falling leaves in woods, pollen motes; by biome, hour and weather; anchored at world positions in a box that wraps around the camera; none underground, over water (insects) or in rain; 5 draws, Low half the counts; in the shader warm-up.
- **Chimney smoke** (`world/buildings/chimneySmoke.ts`, kit `SettlementMesh.smoke`): plumes over the 40 nearest chimneys within 260 m, GPU-animated (one instanced draw), busiest at breakfast and supper, thin in rain, faint by moonlight.
- **Town flames move** (`townMaterial.ts`, key v3): the fire layer's quads sway and lick, the picture wavers, the glow breathes ±8 % at 1-2 Hz (no strobe).
- **Players get a face and hair** (`net/avatar.ts`): nose and ears; eyes, brows and a mouth in the old visor's slot (matte, `hides.visor` unchanged); hair in 8 per-player colours (high and mid figures), hidden under helms/hoods/caps; the mid head is skin, not navy.
- **Townsfolk get faces** (`npcs.ts`): eyes, brows, mouth, one more instanced draw.
- **Clouds soften shadows** (`sunShadows.ts`): shadow intensity 1 → 0.28 with the overcast (rain, snow, fog banks).
- **The story is heard** (`audio/bells.ts`, `triggers.ts`, `music/director.ts`, `engine.ts`): synthesized bells (classic partials with the minor-third tierce, beating pairs, rendered once on the audio thread as `synth:` cues) for a Hollow Bell's and the Hush's toll, on the strike; Events 1602-1612 voiced (act cards, the Hush rising, the choir's three bells, the endless night, the dawn, the ending, revives); music: silence for the entrance, the void realm for the fight and choir, the endless night's countdown as a timed objective, the day at once after the victory.
- **Wildlife voices** (`audio/wildlife.ts`, `ambience.ts`): songbirds (five phrase shapes, dawn chorus, evening song), a hawk's cry over the heights, frog choruses on wetland nights, owls in the woods; through the weather submix; rain, snow, gales, towns and caves respected.
- **Hoofbeats and fishing sounds** (`movement.ts`, `ui/fishing/store.ts`): in the saddle one/two/three beats by gait instead of footsteps; fishing's cast, plop, nibbles, bite (on time), hook, catch by rarity, a lost fish.

**Performance** (`docs/evidence/pt2-immersion-bench.json`; RTX 3070, ANGLE D3D11, headless Chromium, uncapped; before = main `390d60c`, after = this branch with the ambient life live): turn bench High (1920x1080) pan p50/p99/max 5.8/10.0/16.1 -> 5.6/9.5/12.0 ms, flick 5.6/10.6/12.7 -> 5.3/8.6/10.7; Low (1280x720) pan 3.6/7.1/11.2 -> 3.3/5.7/7.9, flick 3.3/5.7/11.7 -> 3.3/5.6/14.1; 0 frames over 33 ms and 0 long tasks in every run. Enemy crowd gallery: 200 enemies p50/p99 7.6/11.2 -> 7.2/11.5 ms (665 vs 662 draws), 2,000 enemies 6.5/10.9 -> 6.2/10.1 ms (622 draws both). No regression beyond noise. New programs (ambient life wing shader x3 and points x2, chimney smoke, the town program v3) are in the shader warm-up (`warmExtras.ts`).

**Evidence**: `docs/evidence/pt2-immersion-audit.md` (the critique, the top 15, before/after per fix), `pt2-immersion-<sheet>-{before,after}.jpg` (16 sheets each), `pt2-enemy-models-story.jpg` / `-story-before.jpg`, `pt2-immersion-avatar-face-after.jpg`, `pt2-immersion-npc-faces-after.jpg`, `pt2-immersion-overcast-shadows-after.jpg`. Recipe: `node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1 --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_NO_WORLD_EVENTS=1`, then `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/immersion-shots.mjs --tag after [--only towns,life]`.

**Tests**: vitest `world/life/ambientLife.test.ts` (weights by biome, hour and weather; wrapping; above ground; off water; none underground; Low halves), `world/buildings/chimneySmoke.test.ts` (hearth hours, nearest-in-range cap), `kit.test.ts` (one smoke source per chimney, above the roof), `ui/story/cinematicMode.test.ts`, `combat/enemies/enemies.test.ts` (the Hush and the bell: own styles, rig, shatter, wind-up poses differ and stay finite), `combat/armor/armor.test.ts` (hair shown bare, hidden under the helm), `audio/triggers.test.ts` (bells on the strike, story cues, revives, hooves, fishing, the bell's partials), `audio/music/director.test.ts` (the finale), `audio/wildlife.test.ts`, `audio/movement.test.ts` (hoofbeats by gait), `ui/fishing/fishing.test.ts` (sounds on time). Client typecheck ok (`tools/creator` fails only in a worktree, ajv resolution, as before); `eslint .` clean; client vitest 95 files, 1172 tests; `node scripts/whatsnew.mjs check` (27 entries, `pt2-immersion` first). No Go touched.

Shared files touched (small): `client/src/world/{WorldScene.tsx,warmExtras.ts,README.md}`, `world/sky/sunShadows.ts`, `world/buildings/{kit.ts,buildings.worker.ts,townsManager.ts,townMaterial.ts,npcs.ts,README.md}`, `net/avatar.ts`, `systems/{NightLayer.tsx,README.md}`, `combat/enemies/{spec,models,pose}.ts` (+ the gallery's story sheets), `ui/story/StoryHud.tsx`, `ui/whatsnew/{WhatsNew.tsx,changelog.json}`, `ui/fishing/store.ts`, `audio/{cues,sfx,triggers,ambience,movement,engine}.ts`, `audio/music/director.ts`, `audio/README.md`, this section.

Not fixed (next): grass stays summer green under snow cover; interior windows are black at noon; dusk is over by 19:00; rain does not ripple water; tips pop up mid-fight.

## Playtest 2: vibe explorer ✅ (branch `pt2/vibe-explorer`)

Nicole: "i wanna build a local dev thing where I can teleport to a new chunk with specified params like what is in that chunk like landmarks, what level it is, and the biome. I want to assess vibe and difficulty. also let me change my level too. this will be used to compose new music." CR 036 (`docs/cr/036-vibe-explorer.md`). Contract: Interact 250 actions **29-35**, Events **1403** `dev_vibe`, **1404** `dev_vibe_here` (in the dev console's 1400-1419). No protocol, YAML or migration change. Dev only, the dev console's gate: the zone needs `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1` and the development ticket key (refused with 1401 value 1 otherwise), the client a debug build.

**How to compose with it (step by step)**:

1. `npm run dev` (starts the zone with `LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1`), play, click once in the world (the sound starts), press **F2**, open the **Vibe** tab. The left column finds places and sets the vibe; the right column is the music.
2. **Find a place**: pick a biome (meadow, forest, highlands, snowpeak, badlands, wetland), what should be there (a hamlet/village/walled town, crystal tower, cave mouth, ruin, shrine, watchtower, bandit camp, enemy camp, wizard tower, sunken temple, demon lair, vault, the Heartfall, a live world event, a lake shore, river bank, coast, peak, deep forest, standing stones, a great tree, a hot spring...), a region, an area level range or tier, stamped or not, a distance from the spawn; **Search**. Each match shows its name, kind, biome, region, area level and tier (unstamped areas: the level you would stamp them at now), distance from the spawn and from you, and what else is within 250 m. **Teleport** lands you on dry, walkable ground outside buildings with 5 s of invulnerability; **Next match** / **Prev** walk the list, **Random match** picks one anywhere in the matches. Command line: `/vibe find biome=forest landmark=shrine level=4-8 region=hollow 12`, `/vibe go 3`, `/vibe go next`, `/vibe go random`.
3. **Set the vibe**: the "Here" card shows where you stand (biome, region, area level, tier, weather, time, demons). Set **this area's level** (`/vibe level 14`: the area's enemies respawn at once at that level and tier; `/vibe level clear`, `/vibe level clear all` give back exactly what was there), **spawn its typical enemies** (`/vibe spawn [count] [level]`), **your level** (`/level 30`), the **time** (dawn, morning, noon, dusk, night buttons or `/time 21:30`), the **weather** (`/weather snowstorm`), **demons** on/off (`/vibe demons off`), a **world event** here. **Freeze the vibe** (`/vibe freeze`) holds the time, the weather where you stand and every enemy, so you can listen as long as you like.
4. **Listen**: "Now playing" shows the director's state and track (position, loop), whether the game, a forced track or one of your mapped files is playing, **why** (e.g. "night: 22:14 is after dusk (18.5) and before dawn (6)", "combat: hits or telegraphs in the last few seconds", "cave: underground 0.62"), the biome, time of day, weather, area tier and level, story act and the **intensity** (0..1: the state's base + 0.04 per tier + rough weather + a boss), every ambience layer's level (wind, gusts, rain, rain on the roof, storm roll, sand, insects, songbirds, hawk, frogs, owls, underwater, drips), the positional loops (fires, water) and each bus. **pin** keeps a small copy over the game while the console is closed. `/music now` prints it in the log.
5. **Steer the music**: force a state at once (day, night, town, cave, void, combat, timed, silence; `/music force night 0.6`), play any built-in track (`/music force void_realm`), force the intensity (`/music intensity 0.9`; for `timed` it picks the time crunch's intro/loop/"5 sec left"), loop a section of a forced track (A = now / B = now, or `/music loop 1:05 1:21`), **solo** or **mute** music, ambience, sfx, ui (`/music solo music`, `/music mute ambience`). **Auto** (`/music force auto`) gives the music back to the game.
6. **Try your new music without a rebuild**: **Load audio files...** (`/music load`) takes WAV/MP3/OGG/FLAC/M4A from disk; they decode in the browser, stay in this browser (IndexedDB) after a reload, and play through the music bus with the game's ducking and a crossfade. **Play** one, or **map** it: "fill from here" takes the current vibe, tick what matters (state, biome, time of day, weather, tier range, act, combat), set gain, loop and fade, **Add mapping**. From then on that file plays whenever the vibe matches (the most specific mapping wins; nothing matching = the game's own music); the readout says "Mapped".
7. **Hand it back**: **Export JSON** (`/music export`) downloads and copies `lodestone-music-map-<date>.json`; send it with the audio files. The format and how each mapping is wired in for good: `client/src/audio/README.md` "Composer mappings: the export format and wiring a file in". **Import JSON** (`/music import`) loads one back.

- **Search** (`server/zone/internal/game/vibe.go`): exact, over an index built once from the atlas (every settlement by size, tower, wilderness waypoint, cave mouth, natural landmark by variant, enemy camp, structure by kind), the vaults, the Heartfall, the macro terrain (per 256 m area at most one peak, lake shore, river bank and coast, from the atlas grid: no chunk is generated), live world events and one "wilds" spot per dry area: 617 places on the official world (167 wilds, 71 coasts, 52 lake shores, 46 river banks, 18 peaks, 29 hamlets, 11 villages, 2 towns, 16 towers, 32 caves, 26 shrines, 24 ruins, 19 watchtowers, 14 vaults, the Heartfall...). Sorted by distance from the spawn, so a query always lists the same places. Safe landing: the nearest spot within 120 m inside the world, dry, a walkable slope with level room around, outside every building footprint (1.5 m margin), town box and camp piece.
- **Area level override** (`internal/difficulty/dev.go`): sits on top of the stamp (never stored; a stamp landing meanwhile goes underneath), clears back to the stamp, the Haven's 1 or unknown, logged (`dev vibe area level`); the area's atlas groups respawn at once at the new level and tier (`combat.World.DevRespawnArea`; bosses rise where the tier allows them). Typical enemies: the area's biome camp table, region element and the tier's beginner share. Freeze: the clock held, the weather where you stand forced, every group stands like a training dummy (new ones too); unfreeze restores each.
- **Composer** (`client/src/audio/composer/`, loaded with the tab): `override.ts` (the override state machine, director inputs for a state, intensity, the "why"), `mapping.ts` (matching, specificity, export/import), `composer.ts` (its own deck on the music bus; the game's player goes quiet while it plays), `files.ts` (IndexedDB), `hook.ts` (the engine's one seam: empty until the composer is used, so normal sessions run as before).

Tests: Go `server/zone/internal/game/vibe_test.go` on the official world: the filter packing vector (= vitest's), the landmark and biome lists equal `filters.ts`'s, **search** (617 places, the same list twice, sorted, every landmark filter returns only its kind, biome/region/distance/tier/level/stamped filters hold, unstamped levels projected for the player, an override found by level and tier), **safe landings** for the first towns, villages, hamlets, lake shores, coasts, river banks, peaks, caves, shrines, towers, the Heartfall and bandit camps (dry, outside buildings, within 120 m), **commands** over Interact (find + go to a lake shore: dry ground, invulnerable, Event 1404; 1 s of invulnerability ends; a random forest match; a Haven pack at level 1 respawns at **12** after `/vibe level 12`, cleared back to the Haven; clear all; typical enemies; freeze and unfreeze; demons), **refused without the dev flag** (every vibe action 1401 value 1, no 1403/1404, no override, freeze, teleport or demons change); `internal/difficulty/dev_test.go` (override reversible over a stamp, the Haven and an unknown area; a stamp landing under it; clamp; `Projected`). Vitest `ui/devconsole/vibe/vibe.test.ts` (packing vector, `/vibe find` parsing incl. short keys, prefixes, ranges, region names, refusals; go next/prev/#n/random, beyond the page; level/spawn/freeze/demons/here; `/music` parsing; the console routes both), `audio/composer/override.test.ts` (the override machine, a forced state reaches and holds every director state, intensity picks the time crunch part, time of day, intensity, explanations, force targets), `audio/composer/mapping.test.ts` (every condition, specificity and ties, unloaded files skipped, export/import round trip, junk dropped and clamped, version refused). Checks after merging main (`7e2de6c`): gen:check up to date; `go vet ./...` (server/zone) clean; gofmt clean on every file touched here (`server/pkg/movement/movement.go` and `internal/game/towns.go` are unformatted on main already); `go test` difficulty, combat and game (game: all pass except three timing tests that failed once in the full run on the loaded machine and pass alone, untouched here: `TestTowerClimbRevealsRegionThenFastTravel`, `TestLocalProfileProgressInSaveBlob`, `TestMiningBreakDropsEditsRespawn`) and the zone's dev-flag infra scan; client typecheck clean; `eslint .` clean; client vitest 98 files, 1189 tests.

Evidence (`node tools/playtest/stack.mjs --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_DEV=1 --zone-env LODESTONE_ZONE_NO_WORLD_EVENTS=1`, then `LODESTONE_CLIENT_URL=<url> node tools/playtest/vibe-shots.mjs`: one headless Chromium, a new character; checked in the client's stores; `docs/evidence/pt2-vibe-shots.json`, 0 issues): `docs/evidence/pt2-vibe-results.jpg` (the tab: 617 places at first, forest shrines = 8, then `/vibe find landmark=lake 12` = 52 lake shores with level, tier, distance and "near"), `pt2-vibe-teleport.jpg` (Teleport to the third lake shore, landed exactly on it at 1536, 1920 on dry ground, the area set to level 14 = Dreadlands, 18:10 in drizzle; a 6 s test WAV loaded and mapped to "forest at dusk" plays: the pinned readout says "Mapped · file: forest_dusk_sketch.wav"), `pt2-vibe-now-playing.jpg` (`/music force night 0.6`: the director in night at once, the why lines, every ambience layer and loop, the buses).

Known gaps: the director chooses by state only, so intensity is a readout and a mapping condition (no stems) and a context-specific mapping (biome, time, tier, act) has no permanent slot yet: wiring the first one means a small `music/contextMusic.ts` resolved like the composer does (README). Time, weather, freeze and demons are zone-wide; overrides last until cleared or the zone restarts. Wilds spots are area centres (256 m). Loaded and forced tracks are decoded whole in memory (a 5-minute WAV ~110 MB).

Shared files touched (small): `server/zone/internal/game/devconsole.go` (route actions 29-35, the session's invulnerability, `devTick` lines, `vibeFrozen` in 1400), `internal/difficulty/difficulty.go` (`Level`/`Haven` honour an override), `internal/combat/atlas.go` (keep the macro HEIGHT/WATER sections), `client/src/ui/devconsole/{DevConsole.tsx,commands.ts,store.ts}` (the tab, /vibe and /music, Events 1403/1404), `client/src/audio/{engine.ts,context.ts,music/player.ts}` (the hook, dev bus gain, position in the status), `contracts/events.md`, `client/src/audio/README.md`, this section. New: `server/zone/internal/game/vibe{,_test}.go`, `internal/combat/vibe.go`, `internal/difficulty/dev{,_test}.go`, `client/src/ui/devconsole/vibe/`, `client/src/audio/composer/`, `tools/playtest/vibe-shots.mjs`, `docs/cr/036-vibe-explorer.md`, `docs/evidence/pt2-vibe-*`.

## Playtest 2: voice settings ✅ (branch `pt2/voice-settings`)

Nicole: "put more diagnostics and mic configurability in the voice settings". Client only (`client/src/voice`): no contract, protocol, server or migration change.

**The Voice panel** (`voice/ui/VoiceSettings.tsx`): opened from the HUD's Voice button, the Sound panel ("Voice settings") and the Esc menu's new Voice row; on the panel stack (`'voice'`, Esc closes it, Esc during a key capture cancels the capture), `data-ui-panel`, frees the mouse. Four tabs; every setting is saved in localStorage (`lodestone.voice.v1`, one validated blob, WP11 blobs still load) and applies live (the runtime follows the store):
- **Microphone**: input device (remembered by id and name; `devices.ts pickDevice`: same id, else the same name under a new id, else the system default while it is unplugged, and back when it returns; `devicechange` followed, with a notice), speakers (`AudioContext.setSinkId`: game sound and voice share one context, so both move; hidden where unsupported), input boost -20..+20 dB (`micChain.ts`: a LiveKit audio processor in the send path), push to talk (key rebindable, hold or toggle, mouse side buttons) or open mic (`vad.ts VadGate`: threshold on a live dBFS meter with the threshold line, drag it on the meter; open-after 0-300 ms; hold-open 50-3000 ms), mute microphone + key (default 0), echo cancellation / noise suppression / auto gain (`restartTrack` with new constraints), incoming volume.
- **Voice keys** (`keys.ts`): checked against `input/bindings.ts`: a key another feature binds in the world or the menu (E, WASD, 1-7, K...), the other voice key, or a reserved key (Esc, Enter, Tab, Space, modifiers, arrows) is refused with the reason; a stored key that became invalid falls back to the default. Both defaults are declared there (`voice/keys.ts`: V push to talk, 0 mute) and listed in the Controls.
- **Test** (`micTest.ts`, its own capture, works offline): RMS / peak dBFS meter with a clip light, hear yourself through the voice bus with 0-2 s delay, record 5 s and play it back on the chosen speakers.
- **Players**: everyone you hear now (level bar, distance or radio, volume 0-200 %, mute), saved by character id (a WP11 by-name entry is read until replaced); saved players who are not nearby, with Reset.
- **Diagnostics** (1 Hz while open): room state, server URL, region, server version, connected for, reconnects, attempts / failures, quality; the token's grant (may talk / listen, room, identity, expiry; never the token), listen-only reason, microphone permission; device and pick status, sample rate, channels, actual processing, capture latency, boost, level; sending: codec (opus, FEC, DTX), bitrate, loss, jitter, round trip, path (UDP direct / via NAT / TCP / TURN relay), level sent (`stats.ts` parses `getRTCStatsReport()` of the mic sender and every receiver); output device, context, latency; each speaker's distance, level, codec, bitrate, loss, jitter, concealment; recent errors. Copy diagnostics (JSON: ISO times, URL without its query, no token), Reconnect voice.
- **Guidance** (`guidance.ts`): permission denied (where to allow it, OS privacy settings), no / busy / unplugged microphone, https needed, listen-only and why (moderator mute), mic muted, not started yet, silent or very quiet mic, server unreachable vs reached-but-no-audio (UDP blocked: ports 7882 UDP / 7881 TCP, VPNs, work networks), TURN relay and TCP fallback explained, loss > 5 %, round trip > 300 ms, flapping, audio suspended by the browser.

**Bugs found on the way**: (1) toggling noise suppression did nothing: the meter's clone of the raw track kept the capture source open and Chrome hands a new capture the processing of a capture still open on the device; the clone is released before `restartTrack` now. (2) RTCP's round trip against the local LiveKit read 10.5 s; the ICE pair's STUN round trip is used, RTCP only as a plausible fallback. Noticed, not changed: the microphone goes out as stereo opus with RED at ~100 kbps per talker (LiveKit's default audio preset); a speech preset would cut upload and SFU fan-out a lot (worth a decision with the G5 voice numbers).

**Tests**: vitest `voice/voice-settings.test.ts` (30: every saved field validated and clamped, clashing / duplicate stored keys, persistence round trip, Defaults keeps player volumes, per-player by id and WP11 migration; dBFS RMS / peak / clip, the gate's open-after / hold-open / threshold; sender and receiver stats from arrays and map-like reports, rates over an interval, formatting, transport words, quality; device fallback incl. unplug / replug / empty labels, getUserMedia error kinds, constraints; key validation against the bindings table, mouse buttons, event reading; guidance per failure; diagnostics JSON redaction, error ring; talk controller mute / toggle hooks), `voice/voice.test.ts` (old blob still loads), `input/bindings.test.ts` and `ui/menu/menu.test.ts` (the voice keys declared and in the Controls). Headless (`tools/playtest/voice-settings-shots.mjs`: private stack via `tools/playtest/stack.mjs`, local LiveKit, one Chromium with two players and a fake microphone playing a steady tone): 35/35 checks, `docs/evidence/pt2-voice-settings.txt`: A hears B, meter live, threshold saved and gate open, +12 dB boost raises the level 11.9 dB, noise suppression off re-acquires (capture reports it off), switching to another microphone restarts the track on it and saves id + name, E refused as a talk key, 8 accepted, toggle mode, record and play back, B at 50 % in the mixer and saved by id, room connected / opus / ~100 kbps / 0 % loss / RTT / path / speaker row, Copy diagnostics JSON without the token, Reconnect voice (counted, hears B again), 0 mutes and V then sends nothing, Esc menu > Voice, Esc closes, Sound > Voice settings. Screenshots `docs/evidence/pt2-voice-settings-{mic,meter,keybind,test,players,diagnostics,diagnostics-2,muted-hud}.jpg`. Client typecheck ok, `eslint .` clean, client vitest 99 files / 1219 tests (after merging main), `node scripts/whatsnew.mjs check` (30 entries, `pt2-voice-settings` first). No Go touched.

Files: new `client/src/voice/{keys,vad,devices,stats,diag,guidance,micChain,micTest}.ts`, `voice/ui/{VoiceSettings,LevelMeter}.tsx`, `voice/ui/micTestStore.ts`, `voice/voice-settings.test.ts`, `tools/playtest/voice-settings-shots.mjs`; changed `voice/{runtime,store,talk,mixer,token,debugHook}.ts`, `voice/ui/VoiceHud.tsx`, `voice/README.md`; shared (small): `input/bindings.ts` (V moved to `voice/keys.ts`, 0 added), `ui/menu/controls.ts` (rows), `audio/ui/AudioSettingsPanel.tsx` (the Voice settings button), `devHandle.ts` (`voiceDiagnostics()`), `ui/whatsnew/changelog.json`, this section.

Gaps: the speakers choice moves game sound too (one AudioContext); while the mic test runs, its capture and the voice capture share the device's processing (Chrome), so a processing toggle shows on both only after both restart (they do); per-player volumes are per browser, not per account; no server-side diagnostics (e.g. the SFU's view of a participant) in the page.

## Playtest 2: ground loot ✅ (branch `pt2/ground-loot`)

Nicole: "make the godray of the rarity color way more captivating to look at, like energy flowing down from the heavens and put the actual weapon model on the floor. and we should have way more of the loot be things you pick up off the ground because that is way more satisfying. and add range based loudness that corresponds of the unique hum of each weapon drop of rarity and make dropped items shared state between clients too." Then "oh nvm it already is in there as server state": dropped items were already zone entities; kept and verified. CR `docs/cr/037-ground-loot.md`. No protocol, migration or codegen change; Interact 21/10 and Event 400 `auto` added (`contracts/events.md`).

- **Beams** (`client/src/ui/inventory/ground/beam.ts`): a white-hot thread in a coloured column in a soft glow, noise streaks and bands pouring DOWN into the item, fading into the sky, brightest where it lands, swaying with the wind, a gentle pulse (never a flash), a splash ring rippling out, motes falling and spiralling in, sparkles on every drop. By rarity: none for common (sparkles only), 2.6 m uncommon, 10 rare, 16 relic, 26 legendary, 34 mythic, 40 primordial, 48 m Bitchin' (cycling rainbow). A new drop's beam strikes down from the sky as it lands; the column thins where a rare+ item hovers so the light frames it. Premultiplied blending keeps the colour in daylight; fog dims it, never erases it; Low drops motes and a noise octave. **Every drop's beams, splashes, motes and sparkles are four draw calls in all** (`LootLights`: instanced ribbons and quads, batched points; buffers rebuilt only when the set changes); programs in the shader warm-up.
- **Real models** (`ground/models.ts`, `combat/armor/model.ts armorPieceGeometry`): weapons are their weapon model (type + material from the key), armor pieces their armor model (one piece at its rest-pose joints, centred), everything else one of 36 props on the weapon material (potion flasks in their colour, bread, stew, pies, skewers, fish dishes, meat on the bone, herb bundles, mushrooms, glowing moss, pelts, pouches, fish, ingots, arrow sheaves, pickaxe, rod, torch, rope, whetstone, whistle, kits, lute, flute, drum, handbells, a tome in its spell's element colour, a rolled blueprint, a coin pile that grows with the amount, a quest parcel, a key, logs). Common/uncommon lie on the grass at a slight angle; rare+ float in their beam, turning (swords and spears point down), a little self-lit; Bitchin' is holographic. Ores and gems keep the caveManager models and get light and hum. New drops arc out of the corpse or chest; one taken near you flies to you.
- **More ground loot** (server `loot_ground.go`, `loot.go`): chests put only gold in the purse (with the open marker, as before) and pop every other item onto the ground around them, facing the opener; kill loot scatters 0.9-2.1 m. Walking over your loot takes gold and vein drops always, and with **Auto-collect common loot** (Esc menu, default on; Interact 21/10, echoed in Event 400 `auto`, re-sent after a handoff) common/uncommon materials, ore, gems, ingots, arrows, food and potions after a 0.8 s settle; gear, tomes, blueprints, tools and rare+ need E. A full bag leaves the item and says so once per 10 s (E always says why).
